**[00:19:45.340] Rep. Lolita T. Javier** (asr-000351)  
Mr. Speaker,

**[00:19:46.740] Rep. Lolita T. Javier** (asr-000352)  
distinguished members of the House of Representatives,

**[00:19:50.940] Rep. Lolita T. Javier** (asr-000355)  
ladies and gentlemen,

**[00:19:53.000] Rep. Lolita T. Javier** (asr-000356)

**[00:19:59.520] Rep. Lolita T. Javier** (asr-000357)  
of the Optical Media Board for Fiscal Year 2027.

**[00:20:05.860] Rep. Lolita T. Javier** (asr-000358)  
The Optical Media Board,

**[00:20:07.600] Rep. Lolita T. Javier** (asr-000359)  
by virtue of Republic Act No. 9239,

**[00:20:12.680] Rep. Lolita T. Javier** (asr-000360)  
is mandated to promote and protect

**[00:20:15.180] Rep. Lolita T. Javier** (asr-000361)  
the intellectual property rights

**[00:20:17.780] Rep. Lolita T. Javier** (asr-000362)  
by regulatory authorities,

**[00:20:19.160] Rep. Lolita T. Javier** (asr-000363)  
regulating the manufacturing,

**[00:20:21.420] Rep. Lolita T. Javier** (asr-000364)  
mastering,

**[00:20:23.260] Rep. Lolita T. Javier** (asr-000365)  
replicating,

**[00:20:24.780] Rep. Lolita T. Javier** (asr-000366)  
importing,

**[00:20:26.220] Rep. Lolita T. Javier** (asr-000367)  
exporting,

**[00:20:27.760] Rep. Lolita T. Javier** (asr-000368)  
distributing,

**[00:20:28.660] Rep. Lolita T. Javier** (asr-000369)  
and selling of optical media

**[00:20:30.940] Rep. Lolita T. Javier** (asr-000370)  
and its technical variations nationwide.

**[00:20:35.620] Rep. Lolita T. Javier** (asr-000371)  
For Fiscal Year 2027,

**[00:20:38.300] Rep. Lolita T. Javier** (asr-000372)  
the proposed budget of the OMB amounts to

**[00:20:41.700] Rep. Lolita T. Javier** (asr-000373)  
53.462 million.

**[00:20:44.520] Rep. Lolita T. Javier** (asr-000374)  
It is 6.430

**[00:20:49.160] Rep. Lolita T. Javier** (asr-000375)  
million or 13.67% higher compared to the Fiscal Year 2026 budget of 47.32 million.

**[00:21:00.960] Rep. Lolita T. Javier** (asr-000376)  
Mr. Speaker,

**[00:21:02.600] Rep. Lolita T. Javier** (asr-000377)  
dear colleagues,

**[00:21:04.200] Rep. Lolita T. Javier** (asr-000378)  
I solicit your kind support for the approval of the budget of the Optical Media Board.

of Pilreka Partylist

and of course to our distinguished sponsor,

**[00:21:42.440] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000392)

**[00:21:44.660] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000393)

Okay, thank you.

**[00:21:55.800] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000398)  
About the Optical Media Board,

**[00:21:57.780] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000399)  
as you know,

**[00:21:59.320] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000400)  
there was a Senate-approved bill,

**[00:22:02.660] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000401)  
number 1654,

**[00:22:04.680] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000402)  
last March 2026,

**[00:22:07.500] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000403)  
by a vote of 20-0.

**[00:22:11.540] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000404)  
The measure would abolish

**[00:22:13.000] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000405)  
the Optical Media Board

**[00:22:15.220] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000406)  
and transfer its functions,

**[00:22:17.240] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000407)  
personal records,

**[00:22:19.880] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000408)  
assets,

**[00:22:21.440] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000409)  
and resources

**[00:22:22.240] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000410)  
to the Intellectual Property Office

**[00:22:25.400] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000411)  
of the Philippines,

**[00:22:27.120] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000412)  
also known as

**[00:22:29.020] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000413)  
IPOPIL or EPOPIL.

**[00:22:31.420] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000414)  
Despite this development,

**[00:22:33.980] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000415)  
the 2027 NEP proposes

**[00:22:36.800] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000416)  
a total budget

**[00:22:38.560] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000417)  
of 56 million

**[00:22:41.640] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000418)  
for the Optical Media Board,

**[00:22:44.160] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000419)  
higher than its 50 million programs

**[00:22:46.240] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000420)  
and programs,

**[00:22:47.240] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000421)  
in 2026.

**[00:22:50.320] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000422)  
Question.

**[00:22:51.620] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000423)  
Does the Board support

**[00:22:52.840] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000424)  
the transfer of its function

**[00:22:54.400] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000425)  
to the Intellectual Property Office

**[00:22:57.160] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000426)  
of the Philippines

**[00:22:58.140] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000427)  
once the Senate bill

**[00:23:00.480] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000428)  
Actually, the Senate bill,

**[00:23:02.980] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000429)  
Mr. Speaker,

**[00:23:04.060] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000430)  
they're just waiting for our counterpart

**[00:23:07.140] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000431)  
in House of Congress.

**[00:23:09.920] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000432)  
Are you happy

**[00:23:10.520] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000433)  
with the transfer

**[00:23:11.680] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000434)  
of all records,

**[00:23:14.620] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000435)  
personal assets, and resources?

**[00:23:17.240] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000436)  
To the Intellectual Property Office

**[00:23:18.920] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000437)  
of the Philippines.

**[00:23:20.180] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000438)  
Are you in favor,

**[00:23:21.620] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000439)  
Madam Ponsor?

**[00:23:25.780] Rep. Lolita T. Javier** (asr-000440)  
The Board submits

**[00:23:27.460] Rep. Lolita T. Javier** (asr-000441)  
to the wisdom of Congress.

**[00:23:31.520] Rep. Lolita T. Javier** (asr-000442)  
Sa ngayon po kasi,

**[00:23:33.140] Rep. Lolita T. Javier** (asr-000443)  
ang Optical Media Board,

**[00:23:34.480] Rep. Lolita T. Javier** (asr-000444)  
or OMB,

**[00:23:35.800] Rep. Lolita T. Javier** (asr-000445)  
ay legal pa rin umiiral

**[00:23:37.500] Rep. Lolita T. Javier** (asr-000446)  
sa ilalim ng RA 9239.

**[00:23:42.100] Rep. Lolita T. Javier** (asr-000447)  
Ang Optical Media Act of 2003,

**[00:23:46.100] Rep. Lolita T. Javier** (asr-000448)  
ang Senator,

**[00:23:47.240] Rep. Lolita T. Javier** (asr-000449)  
ang Senate Bill na 1654

**[00:23:48.580] Rep. Lolita T. Javier** (asr-000450)  
ay naapurubahan na po sa Senado.

**[00:23:51.100] Rep. Lolita T. Javier** (asr-000451)  
Pero pending pa rin po dito

**[00:23:53.240] Rep. Lolita T. Javier** (asr-000452)  
sa House of Representatives.

**[00:23:56.320] Rep. Lolita T. Javier** (asr-000453)  
Kaya hindi pa po naging batas.

**[00:24:00.060] Rep. Lolita T. Javier** (asr-000454)  
Kailangan pa rin ng pondo

**[00:24:01.960] Rep. Lolita T. Javier** (asr-000455)  
ang OMB

**[00:24:03.420] Rep. Lolita T. Javier** (asr-000456)  
kasi may mga legal na mandate pa po

**[00:24:06.840] Rep. Lolita T. Javier** (asr-000457)  
na hindi pwedeng basta itigil na lang

**[00:24:10.460] Rep. Lolita T. Javier** (asr-000458)  
ang operasyon ng ahensya.

**[00:24:13.720] Rep. Lolita T. Javier** (asr-000459)  
Kasama na dito

**[00:24:14.540] Rep. Lolita T. Javier** (asr-000460)  
ang pampasahod sa ating mga empley.

**[00:24:17.240] Rep. Lolita T. Javier** (asr-000461)  
At ang mga pagkakagastusan

**[00:24:21.300] Rep. Lolita T. Javier** (asr-000462)  
sa pang-araw-araw

**[00:24:23.220] Rep. Lolita T. Javier** (asr-000463)  
ng operasyon ng ahensya.

**[00:24:27.100] Rep. Lolita T. Javier** (asr-000464)  
Mr. Speaker?

**[00:24:29.380] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000465)  
Mr. Speaker, Madam Sponsor,

**[00:24:31.800] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000466)  
so paboro ba kayo

**[00:24:32.780] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000467)  
once nag-winding down na

**[00:24:35.280] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000468)  
ang OMB,

**[00:24:36.880] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000469)  
once this bill being approved,

**[00:24:39.320] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000470)  
both houses,

**[00:24:40.680] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000471)  
paboro ba kayo na itransfer lahat

**[00:24:42.980] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000472)  
sa Intellectual Property Office of the Philippines?

**[00:24:45.120] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000473)  
Do you have a choice?

**[00:24:47.240] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000474)  
Ang galing choice, di ba?

**[00:24:48.380] Rep. Lolita T. Javier** (asr-000475)  
Pabor po ako, Mr. Chair.

**[00:24:51.360] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000476)  
Thank you.

**[00:24:51.860] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000477)  
Follow-up.

**[00:24:59.740] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000482)  
from the get-go

**[00:25:15.680] Rep. Lolita T. Javier** (asr-000488)

**[00:25:17.240] Rep. Lolita T. Javier** (asr-000489)

**[00:25:19.360] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000493)

**[00:25:21.180] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000494)

**[00:25:29.260] Rep. Lolita T. Javier** (asr-000498)  
at ang OMB.

**[00:25:31.000] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000499)

**[00:25:32.040] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000500)

**[00:25:33.160] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000501)

**[00:25:35.020] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000502)

**[00:25:38.000] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000505)  
Okay.

**[00:25:38.720] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000506)  
Again, before we

**[00:25:39.920] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000507)

**[00:25:42.680] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000508)  
Mr. Speaker, no?

**[00:25:43.940] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000509)  
Ano ba talagang primary

**[00:25:45.140] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000510)

**[00:25:46.640] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000511)

**[00:25:47.240] Rep. Lolita T. Javier** (asr-000512)  
Ang Optical Media Board po

**[00:25:50.800] Rep. Lolita T. Javier** (asr-000513)  
ay patuloy pa rin

**[00:25:52.360] Rep. Lolita T. Javier** (asr-000514)

**[00:25:54.140] Rep. Lolita T. Javier** (asr-000515)

**[00:25:56.360] Rep. Lolita T. Javier** (asr-000516)  
hindi pa tayo

**[00:25:57.780] Rep. Lolita T. Javier** (asr-000517)

**[00:26:00.160] Rep. Lolita T. Javier** (asr-000518)

**[00:26:01.680] Rep. Lolita T. Javier** (asr-000519)

**[00:26:03.720] Rep. Lolita T. Javier** (asr-000520)  
lahat ng mga storage

**[00:26:06.500] Rep. Lolita T. Javier** (asr-000521)

**[00:26:08.760] Rep. Lolita T. Javier** (asr-000522)  
Kagaya po yung K-pop.

**[00:26:10.380] Rep. Lolita T. Javier** (asr-000523)  
Pag dumating po ng Pilipinas,

**[00:26:12.540] Rep. Lolita T. Javier** (asr-000524)

**[00:26:14.320] Rep. Lolita T. Javier** (asr-000525)

**[00:26:15.240] Rep. Lolita T. Javier** (asr-000526)

**[00:26:17.240] Rep. Lolita T. Javier** (asr-000527)  
nire-regulate

**[00:26:19.860] Rep. Lolita T. Javier** (asr-000528)  
at sine-check

**[00:26:20.740] Rep. Lolita T. Javier** (asr-000529)  
para

**[00:26:21.420] Rep. Lolita T. Javier** (asr-000530)

**[00:26:24.320] Rep. Lolita T. Javier** (asr-000531)

**[00:26:26.620] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000532)  
Mr. Speaker,

**[00:26:27.740] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000533)  
this will be my last question.

**[00:26:30.380] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000534)  
The board has 92

**[00:26:32.040] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000535)

**[00:26:35.160] Rep. Lolita T. Javier** (asr-000536)  
Opo.

**[00:26:35.960] Rep. Lolita T. Javier** (asr-000537)  
Correct po, Mr.

**[00:26:37.160] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000538)  
But only 46 field

**[00:26:39.600] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000539)  
positions. Half of its

**[00:26:41.740] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000540)  
authorized plantilia, therefore,

**[00:26:43.180] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000541)  
remains vacant.

**[00:26:45.140] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000542)  
Anong problema rito? Bakit hindi nyo po

**[00:26:47.240] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000543)  
na-fill up itong

**[00:26:49.280] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000544)  
92 authorized position?

**[00:26:51.220] Rep. Lolita T. Javier** (asr-000545)  
Mr. Speaker, marami na po

**[00:26:53.360] Rep. Lolita T. Javier** (asr-000546)  
kasing nag-transfer dahil po sa

**[00:26:55.440] Rep. Lolita T. Javier** (asr-000547)

**[00:26:57.700] Rep. Lolita T. Javier** (asr-000548)  
sila.

**[00:26:59.360] Rep. Lolita T. Javier** (asr-000549)  
At saka OIC na lang po

**[00:27:01.320] Rep. Lolita T. Javier** (asr-000550)  
iyong

**[00:27:01.820] Rep. Lolita T. Javier** (asr-000551)

**[00:27:05.780] Rep. Lolita T. Javier** (asr-000552)  
Limited manpower na po

**[00:27:07.580] Rep. Lolita T. Javier** (asr-000553)  
sila.

**[00:27:08.940] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000554)  
From the get-go po, talagang ito na po

**[00:27:11.600] Rep. Lolita T. Javier** (asr-000555)  
ang... Wala na po kasi silang

**[00:27:13.580] Rep. Lolita T. Javier** (asr-000556)

**[00:27:14.980] Rep. Lolita T. Javier** (asr-000557)  
Kasi OIC na nga lang po

**[00:27:18.540] Rep. Lolita T. Javier** (asr-000558)  
ang mga heads nila.

**[00:27:23.460] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000559)  
So, my next question.

**[00:27:25.920] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000560)  
So, ito po. Regular functions

**[00:27:27.140] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000561)  
being performed by consultants,

**[00:27:28.960] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000562)  
contract workers, or job order workers.

**[00:27:31.180] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000563)  
Yun na rin po.

**[00:27:32.100] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000564)  
So, nag-multi-tasking kayo.

**[00:27:35.380] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000565)  
Yes, sir.

**[00:27:36.320] Rep. Lolita T. Javier** (asr-000566)  
Yes, Mr. Speaker.

**[00:27:37.780] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000567)  
You know what, Mr. Speaker,

**[00:27:40.660] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000568)  
masakit man sabihin to that

**[00:27:42.600] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000569)  
OMB has been in existence for the last

**[00:27:44.980] Rep. Lolita T. Javier** (asr-000570)

**[00:27:46.760] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000571)  
20 years, no?

**[00:27:48.300] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000572)  
It's finally winding down.

**[00:27:50.880] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000573)  
But again, before I close

**[00:27:52.660] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000574)  
my statement, Mr. Speaker,

**[00:27:55.280] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000575)  
thank you for your services

**[00:27:57.000] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000576)  
in case na

**[00:27:58.940] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000577)

**[00:28:00.620] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000578)  
mapasad itong batas neto.

**[00:28:02.940] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000579)  
Yun lang, Mr. Speaker.

**[00:28:04.580] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000580)  
Thank you very much to the OMB

**[00:28:07.060] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000581)

**[00:28:08.110] Rep. Lolita T. Javier** (asr-000581-split-2)

**[00:28:10.000] Rep. Lolita T. Javier** (asr-000582)  
Mr. Speaker.

**[00:28:11.000] Rep. Lolita T. Javier** (asr-000583)

**[00:28:21.800] Deputy Speaker Yevgeny Vincente B. Emano** (asr-000588-split-2)

**[00:28:25.040] Rep. Jernie Jett V. Nisay** (asr-000590)  
Good morning, Mr. Speaker,

**[00:28:26.920] Rep. Jernie Jett V. Nisay** (asr-000591)  
Madam Sponsor, at sa

**[00:28:29.000] Rep. Jernie Jett V. Nisay** (asr-000592)  
bubo ng Optical Media Board

**[00:28:30.840] Rep. Jernie Jett V. Nisay** (asr-000593)  
family. Ilang katanungan lamang po.

**[00:28:34.360] Rep. Jernie Jett V. Nisay** (asr-000594)  
Mr. Speaker,

**[00:28:35.620] Rep. Jernie Jett V. Nisay** (asr-000595)  
Madam Sponsor,

**[00:28:36.860] Rep. Jernie Jett V. Nisay** (asr-000596)  
ang Optical Media Board po ay tinatag

**[00:28:39.000] Rep. Jernie Jett V. Nisay** (asr-000597)  
noong panahon na ang

**[00:28:40.720] Rep. Jernie Jett V. Nisay** (asr-000598)  
pangunahing concern sa piracy,

**[00:28:43.100] Rep. Jernie Jett V. Nisay** (asr-000599)  
ICDs, DVDs, at iba pang

**[00:28:44.840] Rep. Jernie Jett V. Nisay** (asr-000600)  
physical optical media.

**[00:28:47.540] Rep. Jernie Jett V. Nisay** (asr-000601)  
Pero ngayon, ang piracy

**[00:28:48.960] Rep. Jernie Jett V. Nisay** (asr-000602)  
distribution ay copyrighted

**[00:28:50.980] Rep. Jernie Jett V. Nisay** (asr-000603)  
content ay increasingly

**[00:28:52.480] Rep. Jernie Jett V. Nisay** (asr-000604)  
lumilipat sa online

**[00:28:54.960] Rep. Jernie Jett V. Nisay** (asr-000605)  
platforms at digital channels.

**[00:28:57.420] Rep. Jernie Jett V. Nisay** (asr-000606)  
Kanina ho, nabanggit ni Kong

**[00:28:58.780] Rep. Jernie Jett V. Nisay** (asr-000607)  
Fresley, ngayong 2026,

**[00:29:01.600] Rep. Jernie Jett V. Nisay** (asr-000608)  
parehong may mga panukala

**[00:29:02.860] Rep. Jernie Jett V. Nisay** (asr-000609)  
sa Kamara at Senado para

**[00:29:04.800] Rep. Jernie Jett V. Nisay** (asr-000610)  
i-abolish ang OMB at

**[00:29:06.840] Rep. Jernie Jett V. Nisay** (asr-000611)  
ilipat ang functions nito

**[00:29:08.360] Rep. Jernie Jett V. Nisay** (asr-000612)  
sa Intellectual Property Office

**[00:29:10.820] Rep. Jernie Jett V. Nisay** (asr-000613)  
of the Philippines dahil sa mga pagbabago

**[00:29:13.000] Rep. Jernie Jett V. Nisay** (asr-000614)  
pong ito.

**[00:29:14.840] Rep. Jernie Jett V. Nisay** (asr-000615)  
Tanong ko po, habang pinagdidesisyonan

**[00:29:17.380] Rep. Jernie Jett V. Nisay** (asr-000616)  
ng Kongreso

**[00:29:18.260] Rep. Jernie Jett V. Nisay** (asr-000617)  
ang future structure

**[00:29:20.440] Rep. Jernie Jett V. Nisay** (asr-000618)  
ng OMB, ano po ang

**[00:29:22.780] Rep. Jernie Jett V. Nisay** (asr-000619)  
mga unique regulatory function

**[00:29:24.600] Rep. Jernie Jett V. Nisay** (asr-000620)  
na ginagawa ng board

**[00:29:26.340] Rep. Jernie Jett V. Nisay** (asr-000621)  
na ngayon ay kailangan pa rin maisagawa

**[00:29:28.760] Rep. Jernie Jett V. Nisay** (asr-000622)  
at hindi dapat mabitawan?

**[00:29:32.640] Rep. Lolita T. Javier** (asr-000623)  
Mr. Speaker,

**[00:29:34.800] Rep. Lolita T. Javier** (asr-000624)  
ang ginagawa po ng

**[00:29:36.200] Rep. Lolita T. Javier** (asr-000625)  
board ay registration

**[00:29:38.540] Rep. Lolita T. Javier** (asr-000626)  
and licensing.

**[00:29:42.840] Rep. Lolita T. Javier** (asr-000627)  
At patuloy pa rin po sila,

**[00:29:44.840] Rep. Lolita T. Javier** (asr-000628)  
isang nag-i-inspeksyon.

**[00:29:48.340] Rep. Lolita T. Javier** (asr-000629)  
At meron din pong mga

**[00:29:50.420] Rep. Lolita T. Javier** (asr-000630)  
hearing ng mga admin cases.

**[00:29:54.060] Rep. Jernie Jett V. Nisay** (asr-000631)  
Napaka-importante po nito dahil may

**[00:29:56.080] Rep. Jernie Jett V. Nisay** (asr-000632)  
continuing public interest

**[00:29:58.120] Rep. Jernie Jett V. Nisay** (asr-000633)  
sa protection of intellectual property.

**[00:30:01.110] Rep. Jernie Jett V. Nisay** (asr-000634)  
Pero nagbago po ang paraan ng piracy at distribusyon ng content. Kailangang malinaw kung ang current institutional structure ay tugma sa problema ang sinusubukan nating solusyonan.

**[00:30:12.140] Rep. Jernie Jett V. Nisay** (asr-000635)  
Follow-up question lamang po, Madam Sponsor. Ano pong specific functions ng OMB na sa tingin ninyo ay hindi sapat na ma-assume ng ibang existing agencies tulad ng Intellectual Property Office of the Philippines?

**[00:30:25.000] Rep. Lolita T. Javier** (asr-000636)  
Um, kagaya nga nang sinabi ko po kanina, ang office po ng OMB ay hindi pa rin naman po tumitigil yung mandate po nila na mag-inspeksyon at magbigay ng mga lisensya.

**[00:30:38.520] Rep. Lolita T. Javier** (asr-000637)  
Um, hindi po sila magbibigay na nila ng license kung hindi nila nakikita kung ano yung content ng mga iikot na materials sa ating bansa.

**[00:30:51.680] Rep. Jernie Jett V. Nisay** (asr-000638)  
Ang next question ko po ay may kinalaman sa

**[00:30:55.000] Rep. Jernie Jett V. Nisay** (asr-000639)  
53.5 million budget. Mr. Speaker, Madam Sponsor, ang proposed 2027 budget ng OMB ay mahigit 53.4 million mula sa 47.0 million sa 2026 GAA.

**[00:31:11.720] Rep. Jernie Jett V. Nisay** (asr-000640)  
Sa gitna po ng mga panukhalang i-abolish ang OMB, ano ang kongkretong additional activities or capabilities na pupunduhan ng proposed 2027 budget?

**[00:31:23.580] Rep. Lolita T. Javier** (asr-000641)  
Mr. Speaker.

**[00:31:25.000] Rep. Lolita T. Javier** (asr-000642)  
Um, um, nag-increase po talaga yung, uh, salary. Alam naman natin na may wage increase tayo. At yung basic operations. Yung M-O-O-E po ang nag-increase.

**[00:31:41.740] Rep. Jernie Jett V. Nisay** (asr-000643)  
Agripo ko, Madam. Uh, kasi po napakalaga po nito habang may legislative review, sa future structure ng agency, kailangan natin makita kung ang dagdag na pondo ay tumutugon.

**[00:31:53.800] Rep. Jernie Jett V. Nisay** (asr-000644)  
sa actual

**[00:31:55.000] Rep. Jernie Jett V. Nisay** (asr-000656)  
enforcement needs o nagpapatuloy lamang sa existing administrative operations.

**[00:32:00.840] Rep. Jernie Jett V. Nisay** (asr-000657)  
Ang susunod na katulungan ko po ay may kinalaman sa 50% vacancy.

**[00:32:05.700] Rep. Jernie Jett V. Nisay** (asr-000658)  
Mr. Speaker, Madam Sponsor, sa 2027 Stopping Summary ng OMB, may 92 authorized positions pero 46 lamang ang field.

**[00:32:17.080] Rep. Jernie Jett V. Nisay** (asr-000659)  
Ibig sabihin, kalahati ng plantilla positions ay vacant.

**[00:32:20.480] Rep. Jernie Jett V. Nisay** (asr-000660)  
Ano po ang dahilan ng napakataas na vacancy rate at anong impact nito sa enforcement ng operations ng OMB?

**[00:32:28.720] Rep. Lolita T. Javier** (asr-000661)  
Mr. Speaker, kagaya nga po ng sagot ko po kanina, dahil nga po OIC lahat ang head natin,

**[00:32:36.420] Rep. Lolita T. Javier** (asr-000662)  
naglipatan na po lahat ng mga empleyado natin at ang OIC ay wala rin pong power to hire, to acquire.

**[00:32:48.040] Rep. Jernie Jett V. Nisay** (asr-000663)  
Maraming salamat po, Madam Sponsor.

**[00:32:50.300] Rep. Jernie Jett V. Nisay** (asr-000664)  
Ang susunod na katulungan ko po ay may kaugnayan sa 3,000 inspection orders.

**[00:32:56.540] Rep. Jernie Jett V. Nisay** (asr-000665)  
Mr. Speaker, Madam Sponsor, sa 2027 Performance Targets, target ng OMB na maka-act sa 3,000 inspection orders,

**[00:33:05.860] Rep. Jernie Jett V. Nisay** (asr-000666)  
served on optical media establishments with 100% acted upon within the prescribed time frame.

**[00:33:12.960] Rep. Jernie Jett V. Nisay** (asr-000667)  
Pero kung kalahati ng authorized positions ay vacant, sapat po ba ang manpower ng OMB para ma-maintain ang

**[00:33:20.300] Rep. Jernie Jett V. Nisay** (asr-000668)  
3,000 inspection order target?

**[00:33:23.780] Rep. Lolita T. Javier** (asr-000669)  
Mr. Speaker, yan nga po ang malaking problema ng board, kaya po humihingi sila ng funding ng pondo para po mapatuloy natin ang mandato para po sa ahensya na ito.

**[00:33:42.240] Rep. Jernie Jett V. Nisay** (asr-000670)  
Ang susunod na katanungan ko naman po, Mr. Speaker, Madam Sponsor, ay may kinalaman sa 301 watch list.

**[00:33:49.340] Rep. Jernie Jett V. Nisay** (asr-000671)  
Ang pangunahin,

**[00:33:50.300] Rep. Jernie Jett V. Nisay** (asr-000672)  
ang pangunahing outcome indicator po ng OMB ay ang pagpapanatili na hindi mapasama ang Pilipinas sa U.S. Trade Representative o sa USTR Section 301 watch list.

**[00:34:01.300] Rep. Jernie Jett V. Nisay** (asr-000673)  
Ito rin ang target for vote 2026 and 2027.

**[00:34:05.760] Rep. Jernie Jett V. Nisay** (asr-000674)  
Sa panahon po ngayon kung saan malaking bahagi ng piracy ay lumilipat sa digital platforms,

**[00:34:11.460] Rep. Jernie Jett V. Nisay** (asr-000675)  
sapat po ba ang pagiging wala sa watch list bilang pangunahing indicator ng effectiveness ng OMB?

**[00:34:18.340] Rep. Jernie Jett V. Nisay** (asr-000676)  
At may domestic indicator?

**[00:34:20.300] Rep. Jernie Jett V. Nisay** (asr-000677)  
May indicators po ba ang OMB na sumukat kung nababawasan ang piracy copyright infringement of the Philippines?

**[00:34:29.260] Rep. Lolita T. Javier** (asr-000678)  
Ibig sabihin po na wala tayo sa watch list.

**[00:34:33.860] Rep. Lolita T. Javier** (asr-000679)  
Ang pinapahihwating lang po ng OMB na ginagampanin pa rin nila ang kanilang tuntunin mga gawain, kaya tayo po ay wala sa watch list.

**[00:34:47.240] Rep. Jernie Jett V. Nisay** (asr-000680)  
Maraming salamat po, Madam Sponsor.

**[00:34:50.300] Rep. Jernie Jett V. Nisay** (asr-000681)  
Mr. Speaker, Mr. Sponsor, nagpapasalamat po tayo sa Optical Media Board at sa mahalagang layunin nitong protektahan ang intellectual property rights.

**[00:34:59.420] Rep. Jernie Jett V. Nisay** (asr-000682)  
Sa harap ng mabilis na pagdipat ng piracy mula sa physical media patungo sa digital environment,

**[00:35:04.960] Rep. Jernie Jett V. Nisay** (asr-000683)  
mahalaga po na mapanatili ang enforcement capability ng pamahalan at matiyak ng maayos na transition kung magkakaroon naman ng institutional changes.

**[00:35:13.240] Rep. Jernie Jett V. Nisay** (asr-000684)

**[00:35:20.300] Rep. Jernie Jett V. Nisay** (asr-000685)  
sa kontra-piracy, at maprotektahan ng ating mga Pilipino creators at legitimate creative industries.

**[00:35:27.860] Rep. Jernie Jett V. Nisay** (asr-000686)  
Maraming salamat po.

**[00:35:29.640] Rep. Jernie Jett V. Nisay** (asr-000687)

**[00:50:06.900] Rep. Patricia C. Calderon** (asr-000776-split-2)  
Mr. Speaker, may we acknowledge the presence of the heads and officials from the Department of Foreign Affairs, namely Secretary Maria Theresa P. Lazaro, Chief Coordinator Maria Antonina M. Mendoza-Oblena, Undersecretary Maria Theresa B. Dizon-De Vega, and Undersecretary Leo M. Herrera-Lim.

**[00:50:30.000] Rep. Patricia C. Calderon** (asr-000777)  
Undersecretary Maria Andrelita S. Austria, OIC Undersecretary Ezadin H. [unclear], and the other assistant secretaries.

**[00:50:42.440] Deputy Speaker Yevgeny Vincente B. Emano** (asr-000777-split-2)  
We acknowledge the presence of the heads and officials of the Department of Foreign Affairs, led by Secretary Maria Theresa P. Lazaro. Welcome to the House of Representatives. Maayong buntag. Majority Leader.

**[00:50:55.000] Rep. Patricia C. Calderon** (asr-000778)  
Mr. Speaker, I move to recognize Representative Sancho Fernando Oaminal of Misamis Occidental and Representative Niko Raul Daza of Northern Samar of the Committee on Appropriations to answer questions on the proposed budget of the Department of Foreign Affairs.

**[00:51:22.120] Deputy Speaker Yevgeny Vincente B. Emano** (asr-000778-split-2)  
Vice Chair Sancho Fernando "Ando" Oaminal and Vice Chair Niko Raul Daza, you have the floor.

**[00:51:30.000] Rep. Sancho Fernando "Ando" F. Oaminal** (asr-000779)  
Mr. Speaker, distinguished colleagues, ladies and gentlemen, please allow me to welcome to the House of Representatives the Department of Foreign Affairs, headed by Secretary Maria Theresa P. Lazaro, together with its attached agencies: the Foreign Service Institute, the Presidential Commission on Visiting Forces, and the UNESCO National Commission of the Philippines. It is my honor, together with my colleague Representative Niko Raul Daza, to sponsor

**[00:52:00.000] Rep. Sancho Fernando "Ando" F. Oaminal** (asr-000780)  
the DFA's proposed budget for fiscal year 2027 in the amount of approximately 35.17 billion. This represents a 21.77% increase from the fiscal year 2026 GAA and will support the department's work in national security, economic diplomacy, consular services, assistance to Filipinos overseas, and organizational development. Fellow

**[00:52:30.000] Rep. Sancho Fernando "Ando" F. Oaminal** (asr-000781)  
members of Congress, the Department of Foreign Affairs plays a central role in advancing and protecting Philippine interests abroad. Its work remains anchored on the three pillars of Philippine foreign policy: preservation and enhancement of national security, promotion and attainment of economic security, and protection of the rights and promotion of the welfare and interests of Filipinos overseas. This year the Philippines has taken on a major regional responsibility as chair of ASEAN

**[00:53:00.000] Rep. Sancho Fernando "Ando" F. Oaminal** (asr-000782)  
under the theme "Navigating Our Future, Together." Through our chairship, the Philippines has helped advance key regional priorities and would have hosted more than 640 meetings throughout the year at the leaders', ministerial, officials' and technical levels. A key outcome of the May 2026 summit in Cebu is the ASEAN Leaders' Declaration on Maritime Cooperation, which provides for

**[00:53:30.000] Rep. Sancho Fernando "Ando" F. Oaminal** (asr-000783)  
the establishment of the ASEAN Maritime Center to be hosted by the Philippines. Beyond ASEAN, the department continues to strengthen the Philippines' bilateral and multilateral partnerships in pursuit of mutually beneficial cooperation. These engagements serve to build and strengthen alliances and partnerships, promote Philippine economic interests, particularly trade and investment, and strengthen people-to-people ties. Maritime security remains a major foreign policy priority.

**[00:54:00.000] Rep. Sancho Fernando "Ando" F. Oaminal** (asr-000784)  
This year marked the tenth anniversary of the 2016 arbitral award. The 1982 United Nations Convention on the Law of the Sea and the 2016 award remain the twin anchors of our foreign policy in the maritime space as we protect and promote our sovereignty, sovereign rights and jurisdiction in the West Philippine Sea. Mr. Speaker, service to our Filipinos overseas remains at the core of the department's work. This

**[00:54:29.000] Rep. Sancho Fernando "Ando" F. Oaminal** (asr-000813)  
responsibility has become even more important amid continuing conflict in the Middle East.

**[00:54:33.660] Rep. Sancho Fernando "Ando" F. Oaminal** (asr-000814)  
The DFA, through our foreign service posts, continues to assist Filipinos affected by hostilities

**[00:54:40.160] Rep. Sancho Fernando "Ando" F. Oaminal** (asr-000815)  
and coordinates closely with the Department of Migrant Workers and other partner agencies

**[00:54:45.640] Rep. Sancho Fernando "Ando" F. Oaminal** (asr-000816)  
on repatriation and other forms of assistance.

**[00:54:49.780] Rep. Sancho Fernando "Ando" F. Oaminal** (asr-000817)  
The Department continues its diplomatic engagements with governments and international organizations

**[00:54:55.600] Rep. Sancho Fernando "Ando" F. Oaminal** (asr-000818)  
to help safeguard Filipino seafarers and Philippine-flagged vessels

**[00:55:00.580] Rep. Sancho Fernando "Ando" F. Oaminal** (asr-000819)  
operating in areas affected by conflict and heightened security risks.

**[00:55:06.160] Rep. Sancho Fernando "Ando" F. Oaminal** (asr-000820)  
These efforts underscore the need for the Department to maintain the capability to respond quickly

**[00:55:11.020] Rep. Sancho Fernando "Ando" F. Oaminal** (asr-000821)  
whenever the safety and welfare of Filipinos overseas are at risk.

**[00:55:15.800] Rep. Sancho Fernando "Ando" F. Oaminal** (asr-000822)  
The DFA likewise continues to work closely with the Department of Migrant Workers

**[00:55:19.540] Rep. Sancho Fernando "Ando" F. Oaminal** (asr-000823)  
in serving Filipinos overseas.

**[00:55:22.420] Rep. Sancho Fernando "Ando" F. Oaminal** (asr-000824)  
In countries without a resident migrant workers' office,

**[00:55:25.600] Rep. Sancho Fernando "Ando" F. Oaminal** (asr-000825)  
the DFA continues to play a lead role in looking after our Kababayans.

**[00:55:30.700] Rep. Sancho Fernando "Ando" F. Oaminal** (asr-000826)  
The Department also remains responsible for overseas Filipinos

**[00:55:34.320] Rep. Sancho Fernando "Ando" F. Oaminal** (asr-000827)  
who are not classified as migrant workers,

**[00:55:37.500] Rep. Sancho Fernando "Ando" F. Oaminal** (asr-000828)  
including students, tourists, permanent residents, and other Filipino nationals.

**[00:55:43.000] Rep. Sancho Fernando "Ando" F. Oaminal** (asr-000829)  
At the same time, the Department continues to bring consular services closer to the public.

**[00:55:47.500] Rep. Sancho Fernando "Ando" F. Oaminal** (asr-000830)  
In 2026, the DFA processed more than 1.5 million passports,

**[00:55:53.480] Rep. Sancho Fernando "Ando" F. Oaminal** (asr-000831)  
issued hundreds of thousands,

**[00:55:55.600] Rep. Sancho Fernando "Ando" F. Oaminal** (asr-000832)  
and continued to provide services through its 42 consular offices nationwide.

**[00:56:03.600] Rep. Sancho Fernando "Ando" F. Oaminal** (asr-000833)  
The 2027 budget will support preparations for the next-generation Philippine passport,

**[00:56:10.120] Rep. Sancho Fernando "Ando" F. Oaminal** (asr-000834)  
which is expected to feature enhanced security features,

**[00:56:13.740] Rep. Sancho Fernando "Ando" F. Oaminal** (asr-000835)  
updated contactless chip technology,

**[00:56:16.520] Rep. Sancho Fernando "Ando" F. Oaminal** (asr-000836)  
and an option for additional passport pages.

**[00:56:19.900] Rep. Sancho Fernando "Ando" F. Oaminal** (asr-000837)  
It will likewise support continued investments in ICT,

**[00:56:24.940] Rep. Sancho Fernando "Ando" F. Oaminal** (asr-000838)  
cybersecurity,

**[00:56:25.600] Rep. Sancho Fernando "Ando" F. Oaminal** (asr-000839)  
and data protection.

**[00:56:27.600] Rep. Sancho Fernando "Ando" F. Oaminal** (asr-000840)  
The DFA is also expanding the reach of the Foreign Service

**[00:56:31.040] Rep. Sancho Fernando "Ando" F. Oaminal** (asr-000841)  
through the opening of new posts in strategic locations,

**[00:56:34.360] Rep. Sancho Fernando "Ando" F. Oaminal** (asr-000842)  
including our embassies in Accra, Ghana, and Astana, Kazakhstan,

**[00:56:39.320] Rep. Sancho Fernando "Ando" F. Oaminal** (asr-000843)  
which are expected to open this year,

**[00:56:41.960] Rep. Sancho Fernando "Ando" F. Oaminal** (asr-000844)  
and our Consulate General in Miami, Florida,

**[00:56:45.240] Rep. Sancho Fernando "Ando" F. Oaminal** (asr-000845)  
which is scheduled for 2027.

**[00:56:47.740] Rep. Sancho Fernando "Ando" F. Oaminal** (asr-000846)  
These posts will help bring services closer to Filipino communities abroad

**[00:56:52.320] Rep. Sancho Fernando "Ando" F. Oaminal** (asr-000847)  
while strengthening the country's political and economic engagement.

**[00:56:55.600] Rep. Sancho Fernando "Ando" F. Oaminal** (asr-000848)  
Mr. Speaker, distinguished colleagues,

**[00:56:59.380] Rep. Sancho Fernando "Ando" F. Oaminal** (asr-000849)  
the DFA's proposed fiscal year 2027 budget will support its core operations

**[00:57:04.780] Rep. Sancho Fernando "Ando" F. Oaminal** (asr-000850)  
and respond to the Philippines' growing requirements in national security,

**[00:57:09.580] Rep. Sancho Fernando "Ando" F. Oaminal** (asr-000851)  
economic diplomacy, consular services,

**[00:57:12.740] Rep. Sancho Fernando "Ando" F. Oaminal** (asr-000852)  
assistance to overseas Filipinos,

**[00:57:14.840] Rep. Sancho Fernando "Ando" F. Oaminal** (asr-000853)  
and its organizational development.

**[00:57:17.500] Rep. Sancho Fernando "Ando" F. Oaminal** (asr-000854)  
Given the Department's important role in advancing Philippine interests on the global stage,

**[00:57:22.100] Rep. Sancho Fernando "Ando" F. Oaminal** (asr-000855)  
I respectfully urge this House to support the DFA's mission to improve the country's economic development,

**[00:57:25.600] Rep. Sancho Fernando "Ando" F. Oaminal** (asr-000856)  
and provide the approval of the proposed

**[00:57:26.860] Rep. Sancho Fernando "Ando" F. Oaminal** (asr-000857)  
fiscal year 2027 budget of the Department of Foreign Affairs

**[00:57:29.280] Rep. Sancho Fernando "Ando" F. Oaminal** (asr-000858)  
and its attached agencies.

**[00:57:31.920] Rep. Sancho Fernando "Ando" F. Oaminal** (asr-000859)  
Thank you very much, Mr. Speaker.

**[00:57:34.020] Deputy Speaker Yevgeny Vincente B. Emano** (asr-000860)  
Salamat kay Cong Ando. Majority Leader.

This is for 2027 fiscal year.

**[00:58:38.920] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000881)  
Am I right?

**[00:58:40.140] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000882)  
Is this correct?

**[00:58:41.580] Rep. Sancho Fernando "Ando" F. Oaminal** (asr-000883)  
Yes, Mr. Speaker.

**[00:58:43.920] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000884)  
Yet, records would show that turnout remains low during recent years.

**[00:58:51.900] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000885)  
Is this correct?

**[00:58:53.800] Rep. Sancho Fernando "Ando" F. Oaminal** (asr-000886)  
That's correct, Mr. Speaker.

**[00:58:55.900] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000887)  
May I know what's the reason behind?

**[00:58:58.540] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000888)  
How come the turnout remains so low from previous years?

**[00:59:02.200] Rep. Sancho Fernando "Ando" F. Oaminal** (asr-000889)  
Mr. Speaker, maraming factors to consider bakit medyo mababa yung turnout.

**[00:59:08.600] Rep. Sancho Fernando "Ando" F. Oaminal** (asr-000890)  
And a few of which are the mobility of overseas Filipinos.

**[00:59:13.200] Rep. Sancho Fernando "Ando" F. Oaminal** (asr-000891)  
Work relationships.

**[00:59:13.920] Rep. Sancho Fernando "Ando" F. Oaminal** (asr-000892)  
Related constraints.

**[00:59:15.240] Rep. Sancho Fernando "Ando" F. Oaminal** (asr-000893)  
Minsan, hindi po pinapayagan ng mga employees nila.

**[00:59:18.160] Rep. Sancho Fernando "Ando" F. Oaminal** (asr-000894)  
Country-specific restrictions and geographic dispersion.

**[00:59:21.880] Rep. Sancho Fernando "Ando" F. Oaminal** (asr-000895)  
Political engagement or lack of confidence.

**[00:59:25.660] Rep. Sancho Fernando "Ando" F. Oaminal** (asr-000896)  
And we have a limited awareness of the registration and the process.

**[00:59:29.260] Rep. Sancho Fernando "Ando" F. Oaminal** (asr-000897)  
Yung iba po nating kababayan, hirap po sila to participate kasi hindi po nila alam yung how to go about it and how to participate in the electoral procedure, Mr. Speaker.

**[00:59:42.320] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000898)  
Mr. Speaker, this is your sponsor.

**[00:59:45.600] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000899)  
You mentioned lack of confidence.

**[00:59:48.400] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000900)  
Can you elaborate what does it mean?

**[00:59:53.700] Rep. Sancho Fernando "Ando" F. Oaminal** (asr-000901)  
Mr. Speaker, lack of confidence.

**[00:59:57.820] Rep. Sancho Fernando "Ando" F. Oaminal** (asr-000902)  
Mr. Speaker, lack of confidence.

**[01:00:00.000] Rep. Sancho Fernando "Ando" F. Oaminal** (asr-000903)  
When I say lack of confidence po, Mr. Speaker, ibig sabihin po, yung mga workers po, they are not that confident yet in the process by which the elections is being done.

**[01:00:21.060] Rep. Sancho Fernando "Ando" F. Oaminal** (asr-000904)  
They need more awareness, Mr. Speaker, kaya po yung Department of Foreign Affairs are consistently undergoing trainings and information campaigns to educate better these intended participants of the electoral process, Mr. Speaker.

**[01:00:42.120] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000905)  
So in short, the lack of confidence, when you translate that to Tagalog, it means wala silang tiwala? Ganun ba yun?

**[01:00:50.080] Rep. Sancho Fernando "Ando" F. Oaminal** (asr-000906)  
Sa proseso?

**[01:00:51.060] Rep. Sancho Fernando "Ando" F. Oaminal** (asr-000907)  
So, it's not na wala po silang tiwala, Mr. Speaker, pero in a way din po, parang some of them feel that their votes do not necessarily make an impact, kaya hindi na lang po sila bumuboto.

**[01:01:09.280] Rep. Sancho Fernando "Ando" F. Oaminal** (asr-000908)  
So they need more awareness and more information campaigns po, Mr. Speaker. Kumbaga, kailangan po ipaintindi po sa kanila na every vote counts.

**[01:01:21.060] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000909)  
Thank you, Mr. Speaker, Distinguished Sponsor.

**[01:01:25.900] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000910)  
How much funds were allocated in the previous years for overseas absentee voting?

**[01:01:33.000] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000911)  
And in connection to that, could you explain the reasons for this low voter turnout and if this is isolated only in specific jurisdictions or is this prevalent in all countries where we have overseas voters?

**[01:01:51.060] Rep. Sancho Fernando "Ando" F. Oaminal** (asr-000912)  
Mr. Speaker, we are just gathering the data in terms of yung amount expended in the previous years.

**[01:02:01.200] Rep. Sancho Fernando "Ando" F. Oaminal** (asr-000913)  
But for the turnout, it seems to be consistent in the different regions of the world.

**[01:02:09.820] Rep. Sancho Fernando "Ando" F. Oaminal** (asr-000914)  
Sa highest po is only 24.16%, which is in the Asia and the Pacific, and the lowest is in North and Latin Americas, which is 7.9%.

**[01:02:21.060] Rep. Sancho Fernando "Ando" F. Oaminal** (asr-000915)  
And as I've mentioned earlier, Mr. Speaker, maraming factors po ang nag-contribute due to the low turnout.

**[01:02:28.920] Rep. Sancho Fernando "Ando" F. Oaminal** (asr-000916)  
But the department is already doubling its efforts, ensuring that in the next electoral process, this will greatly improve, Mr. Speaker.

**[01:02:40.440] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000917)  
Have you tried the COMLEC na magkaroon kayo ng consultation or technical working group when it comes to this?

**[01:02:47.860] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000918)  
At least, are they involved, COMLEC?

**[01:02:50.060] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000919)  
COMLEC?

**[01:02:51.060] Rep. Sancho Fernando "Ando" F. Oaminal** (asr-000920)  
Yes, definitely, Mr. Speaker. The COMLEC is very much involved in this procedure.

**[01:02:58.840] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000921)  
My follow-up question, just to put this in context, how many overseas registered voters do we have in 2025, if we have the figures on hand?

**[01:03:08.760] Rep. Sancho Fernando "Ando" F. Oaminal** (asr-000922)  
As of 2025, Mr. Speaker, there are 1,241,690 registered overseas voters, to be exact po.

**[01:03:20.600] Rep. Sancho Fernando "Ando" F. Oaminal** (asr-000923)  
Out of?

**[01:03:21.060] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000924)  
Out of this 1.2 million voters, how many had actually voted?

**[01:03:27.580] Rep. Sancho Fernando "Ando" F. Oaminal** (asr-000925)  
Out of the 1.2 million po, Mr. Speaker, 228,000 approximately were able to actually vote, which is equivalent to 18.4%, Mr. Speaker.

**[01:03:37.860] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000926)  
So, napakababa pala ng turnout natin.

**[01:03:41.460] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000927)  
Yes, Mr. Speaker.

**[01:03:42.640] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000928)  
Almost 18%, Mr. Speaker.

**[01:03:45.180] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000929)  
So, ano sa tingin mo ang reason neto?

**[01:03:49.740] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000930)  
Because you mentioned ago...

**[01:03:51.060] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000931)  
Lack of confidence, kailangan you have a massive information campaign.

**[01:03:57.040] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000932)  
Internet voting removed the physical distance problem, but turnout was still only about 18% among OBCS voters.

**[01:04:05.220] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000933)  
So, what does DFA's post-election assessment say?

**[01:04:09.880] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000934)  
Were the actual barriers pre-voting enrollment, digital literacy, authentication, awareness, trust, connectivity, or something else?

**[01:04:20.320] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000935)  
Or maybe?

**[01:04:21.060] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000936)  
Yung mga kababayan ba natin, natatakot sila because they're not documented?

**[01:04:27.780] Rep. Sancho Fernando "Ando" F. Oaminal** (asr-000937)  
Several factors mentioned by the good gentleman, Mr. Speaker, contributed po dun sa low turnout.

**[01:04:37.420] Rep. Sancho Fernando "Ando" F. Oaminal** (asr-000938)  
And yung digital literacy is very important din po.

**[01:04:42.080] Rep. Sancho Fernando "Ando" F. Oaminal** (asr-000939)  
Isa yun sa malaking reason bakit po hindi nakaboto.

**[01:04:50.080] Rep. Sancho Fernando "Ando" F. Oaminal** (asr-000940)  
Nakaboto yung iba nating kababayan kahit na gusto po nilang bumoto.

**[01:04:53.640] Rep. Sancho Fernando "Ando" F. Oaminal** (asr-000941)  
And that is why in the past several months and in the upcoming months, nagkaroon at magkakaroon po yung DFA ng overseas regional training for continuing overseas voter registration.

**[01:05:10.620] Rep. Sancho Fernando "Ando" F. Oaminal** (asr-000942)  
Kasama na din po dyan yung process by which on how to exercise their vote, Mr. Speaker.

**[01:05:17.640] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000943)  
Mr. Speaker, Distinguished Sponsor,

**[01:05:21.420] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000944)  
I understand that statutory division of responsibility, but DFA operates the foreign service post and undertook information registration activities.

**[01:05:34.160] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000945)  
I therefore want DFA's own expenditures and outputs separated from COMELEC.

**[01:05:40.300] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000946)  
Besides, Mr. Speaker, overseas registration for the succeeding election cycle,

**[01:05:47.640] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000947)  
already began on December 1, 2025, and is scheduled to continue through September 30, 2027 at embassies and consulates.

**[01:05:58.060] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000948)  
So I presume we'll be funding these activities again. Am I right?

**[01:06:04.400] Rep. Sancho Fernando "Ando" F. Oaminal** (asr-000949)  
Yes, Mr. Speaker. Nakahiwalay po talaga yung budget ng overseas voting under the DFA.

**[01:06:11.560] Rep. Sancho Fernando "Ando" F. Oaminal** (asr-000950)  
For 2026, nagkaroon po ng 44.4 million separately.

**[01:06:17.640] Rep. Sancho Fernando "Ando" F. Oaminal** (asr-000951)  
For that purpose.

**[01:06:19.860] Rep. Sancho Fernando "Ando" F. Oaminal** (asr-000952)  
And iba din po yung budget allocated by the COMELEC, which I do not have any record for.

**[01:06:27.700] Rep. Sancho Fernando "Ando" F. Oaminal** (asr-000953)  
This office does not have any record.

**[01:06:29.020] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000954)  
So yung sa COMELEC, so you have separate budget with COMELEC. Am I right?

**[01:06:33.420] Rep. Sancho Fernando "Ando" F. Oaminal** (asr-000955)  
Yes, Mr. Speaker.

**[01:06:34.320] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000956)  
So paano nyo nire-reconcile yun?

**[01:06:36.740] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000957)  
Wala bang duplicity?

**[01:06:38.600] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000958)  
Mga mamiya, nag-fund kayo, ganun din sila.

**[01:06:41.000] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000959)  
So there will be a separate, may kanya-kanya, baka yung jurisdiction, or how to go.

**[01:06:47.700] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000960)  
About it?

**[01:06:48.880] Rep. Sancho Fernando "Ando" F. Oaminal** (asr-000961)  
Mr. Speaker, as to the details of the budget, kasi part of which is the traveling expenses.

**[01:06:57.300] Rep. Sancho Fernando "Ando" F. Oaminal** (asr-000962)  
So yung mga officials po ng COMELEC, dun po nila sinacharge sa office nila sa DFA naman po.

**[01:07:02.580] Rep. Sancho Fernando "Ando" F. Oaminal** (asr-000963)  
Dito naman po under the DFA training expenses din po.

**[01:07:07.140] Rep. Sancho Fernando "Ando" F. Oaminal** (asr-000964)  
Training of, there's a counterpart of DFA and there's a counterpart of COMELEC.

**[01:07:11.360] Rep. Sancho Fernando "Ando" F. Oaminal** (asr-000965)  
So we assure the good gentleman that there is no duplicity.

**[01:07:17.640] Rep. Sancho Fernando "Ando" F. Oaminal** (asr-000966)  
Of the expenses, Mr. Chair.

**[01:07:21.360] Rep. Sancho Fernando "Ando" F. Oaminal** (asr-000967)  
Thank you, Mr. Sponsor.

**[01:07:23.320] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000968)  
Mr. Speaker, before we fund further preparatory activity in 2027,

**[01:07:29.160] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000969)  
when will Congress receive the costed proposal for the hybrid or revised overseas voting system,

**[01:07:37.240] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000970)  
including lessons learned from 2025?

**[01:07:40.360] Rep. Sancho Fernando "Ando" F. Oaminal** (asr-000971)  
I'm sorry, may we repeat the question, Mr. Speaker?

**[01:07:51.620] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000972)  
Again, before we fund further preparatory activity for 2027, right now, because asking for another fund for 2027 here in Congress,

**[01:08:03.580] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000973)  
kailan namin matatanggap yung costed proposal for the hybrid or revised overseas voting system,

**[01:08:09.880] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000974)  
including lessons learned from 2025?

**[01:08:10.340] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000975)  
Kailan namin matatanggap yung lessons learned from 2025?

**[01:08:12.780] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000976)  
Remember 2025, the turnout was only around 18%.

**[01:08:16.940] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000977)  
18% lang, Mr. Chair.

**[01:08:20.060] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000978)  
The actual votes, right?

**[01:08:22.500] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000979)  
So ngayon, 2027, yung costing nyo for the preparation for the hybrid or revised overseas voting system.

**[01:08:31.240] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000980)  
So anong gagawin natin na hindi natin maulit yung turnout natin na 18%?

**[01:08:38.460] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000981)  
Mr. Chair, part of...

**[01:08:39.860] Rep. Sancho Fernando "Ando" F. Oaminal** (asr-000982)  
Part po ng activities that the DFA will conduct is yung training and information dissemination po.

**[01:08:49.980] Rep. Sancho Fernando "Ando" F. Oaminal** (asr-000983)  
So yung pagpapaintindi po sa ating mga kababayan, how important their vote is.

**[01:08:56.580] Rep. Sancho Fernando "Ando" F. Oaminal** (asr-000984)  
So yun po yung nagdodouble efforts yung DFA and of course in partnership with COMELEC to educate our mga kababayan overseas po.

**[01:09:08.820] Rep. Sancho Fernando "Ando" F. Oaminal** (asr-000985)  
So that...

**[01:09:09.860] Rep. Sancho Fernando "Ando" F. Oaminal** (asr-000986)  
Yun po yung way na ma-increase po yung turnout natin, Mr. Chair.

**[01:09:15.720] Rep. Sancho Fernando "Ando" F. Oaminal** (asr-000987)  
And of course, better accessibility to the information needed through postings not only sa consular offices,

**[01:09:25.260] Rep. Sancho Fernando "Ando" F. Oaminal** (asr-000988)  
I mean sa foreign posts offices, but also through the direct communication with our recorded kababayans, Mr. Speaker.

**[01:09:37.000] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000989)  
As you know, Mr. Speaker, 2288 is around...

**[01:09:39.860] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000990)  
So do you think the 18% will translate maybe half, maybe 50% kaya ba natin by 2028?

**[01:09:49.880] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000991)  
Otherwise, Mr. Speaker, sayang lang yung pondong ibinibigay natin because we're funding our massive information campaign for our kababayan overseas to make sure na makaboto sila because every vote counts.

**[01:10:05.540] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000992)  
So do you see in the 20...

**[01:10:09.860] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000993)  
Or maybe the presidential election, tataas ang ating turnout vote?

**[01:10:17.840] Rep. Sancho Fernando "Ando" F. Oaminal** (asr-000994)  
That is the hope and the direction, Mr. Speaker, na mapapataas po yung rate.

**[01:10:24.460] Rep. Sancho Fernando "Ando" F. Oaminal** (asr-000995)  
And of course, we also are expecting that the COMELEC will double its efforts in increasing the turnout rate, Mr. Speaker.

**[01:10:36.720] Rep. Sancho Fernando "Ando" F. Oaminal** (asr-000996)  
But as far as the DFA is concerned...

**[01:10:39.860] Rep. Sancho Fernando "Ando" F. Oaminal** (asr-000997)  
In coordinating with the overseas Filipino voters, so they're doubling their efforts already po, Mr. Speaker.

**[01:10:47.840] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000998)  
Thank you for that, good sponsor.

**[01:10:50.880] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000999)  
Mr. Speaker, may I go to one of the crucial mandates of the department?

**[01:10:56.280] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-001000)  
Mr. Speaker, distinguished sponsor, can you confirm the report that DFA had already lodged 39 diplomatic protests against China in 2026?

**[01:11:08.120] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-001001)  
And 296 from 2022 through 2026?

**[01:11:14.620] Rep. Sancho Fernando "Ando" F. Oaminal** (asr-001002)  
Yes, that is correct, Mr. Speaker.

**[01:11:17.840] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-001003)  
The diplomacy program itself is seeking approximately 14.48 billion in 2027. Is this correct?

**[01:11:29.820] Rep. Sancho Fernando "Ando" F. Oaminal** (asr-001004)  
Yes, Mr. Speaker, that's correct.

**[01:11:31.340] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-001005)  
For the next fiscal year, or maybe for the past years, what performance framework does DFA use to determine...

**[01:11:39.860] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-001006)  
...whether these protests are producing the intended diplomatic outcome?

**[01:11:46.980] Rep. Sancho Fernando "Ando" F. Oaminal** (asr-001007)  
Mr. Speaker, we believe that these diplomatic protests, since these are legal documents, have in a way toned down the aggression of other countries against the Philippines, Mr. Speaker.

**[01:12:02.740] Rep. Sancho Fernando "Ando" F. Oaminal** (asr-001008)  
Makikita naman po natin na yung DFA, it is their role...

**[01:12:09.860] Rep. Sancho Fernando "Ando" F. Oaminal** (asr-001009)  
...is to diplomatically negotiate and communicate with other countries, Mr. Chair.

**[01:12:21.800] Rep. Sancho Fernando "Ando" F. Oaminal** (asr-001010)  
So, as we see na ngayon, while tensions are still there, pero medyo in a controlled level na po, then that is already an achievement on the part of the DFA, Mr. Chair, na yung mga inisyo na diplomatic notes are effective.

**[01:12:39.240] Rep. Sancho Fernando "Ando" F. Oaminal** (asr-001011)  
And in fact...

**[01:12:39.860] Rep. Sancho Fernando "Ando" F. Oaminal** (asr-001012)  
And in fact, many of which have been answered by China, Mr. Chair.

**[01:12:45.180] Rep. Sancho Fernando "Ando" F. Oaminal** (asr-001013)  
Yung iba, maramihan po nila sinagot. Yung iba po, isa-isa nila sinagot.

**[01:12:53.140] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-001014)  
Thank you, Mr. Speaker, Distinguished Sponsor.

**[01:12:55.800] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-001015)  
Is DFA tracking, for example, whether a protest receives a formal response, whether the complaint of conduct ceases or recurs, whether partner governments...

**[01:13:09.860] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-001016)  
...issues, supporting statements, whether operational behavior at sea changes, otherwise our protest will just be a mere formality with no outcome?

**[01:13:23.940] Rep. Sancho Fernando "Ando" F. Oaminal** (asr-001017)  
As mentioned, Mr. Chair, there is no one-on-one, one-to-one correspondence on our protests with China.

**[01:13:32.540] Rep. Sancho Fernando "Ando" F. Oaminal** (asr-001018)  
Yung reply nila, minsan, there are three or four diplomatic notes and they will only reply with one...

**[01:13:39.860] Rep. Sancho Fernando "Ando" F. Oaminal** (asr-001019)  
They will reply to that once, yung three or four diplomatic notes.

**[01:13:43.880] Rep. Sancho Fernando "Ando" F. Oaminal** (asr-001020)  
And we can see now, Mr. Chair, humuhupuan naman po yung tensyon as far as yung mga naririnig natin sa news before na there's attack against our fishermen, against our Coast Guard.

**[01:13:57.140] Rep. Sancho Fernando "Ando" F. Oaminal** (asr-001021)  
So ngayon, we are seeing na medyo nagto-tone down po, Mr. Chair, and we believe that this is greatly due also to the diplomatic notes issue.

**[01:14:09.860] Rep. Sancho Fernando "Ando" F. Oaminal** (asr-001022)  
by the Department of Foreign Affairs, Mr. Chair.

**[01:14:12.840] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-001023)  
Thank you, Mr. Speaker, Distinguished Sponsor.

**[01:14:15.920] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-001024)  
This becomes more relevant because on September 11, following a Philippine Coast Guard report that Chinese forces fired flares toward a Philippine aircraft near Zamora, Subi, and Panganiban Mischief Reefs.

**[01:14:34.320] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-001025)  
The FBI public called on China to cease its activities.

**[01:14:39.860] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-001026)  
at the futures and reiterated its position under UNCLOS and the 2016 Arbitral Award.

**[01:14:48.720] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-001027)  
Is this correct?

**[01:14:50.500] Rep. Sancho Fernando "Ando" F. Oaminal** (asr-001028)  
Yes, Mr. Chair, that's correct.

**[01:14:53.020] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-001029)  
So with respect to the September flare incident, did DFA lodge a diplomatic protest?

**[01:15:00.000] Rep. Sancho Fernando "Ando" F. Oaminal** (asr-001030)  
Mr. Chair, the DFA is about to send a diplomatic protest on that particular attack, Mr. Chair.

**[01:15:12.440] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-001031)  
So, it happened last week, September 11.

**[01:15:15.320] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-001032)  
Yes, Mr. Chair.

**[01:15:15.920] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-001033)  
So, isn't it after the incident, the following day, we should launch a protest?

**[01:15:21.540] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-001034)  
Hindi ba ganun ang SOP natin dyan?

**[01:15:24.700] Rep. Sancho Fernando "Ando" F. Oaminal** (asr-001035)  
Not immediately, Mr. Speaker.

**[01:15:26.280] Rep. Sancho Fernando "Ando" F. Oaminal** (asr-001036)

So, we're just going to wait.

**[01:15:42.040] Rep. Sancho Fernando "Ando" F. Oaminal** (asr-001038)

**[01:15:48.180] Rep. Sancho Fernando "Ando" F. Oaminal** (asr-001039)

**[01:15:49.940] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-001040)

**[01:15:53.020] Rep. Sancho Fernando "Ando" F. Oaminal** (asr-001041)

**[01:15:56.280] Rep. Sancho Fernando "Ando" F. Oaminal** (asr-001042)

**[01:15:59.900] Rep. Sancho Fernando "Ando" F. Oaminal** (asr-001043)

**[01:16:06.020] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-001044)

**[01:16:08.920] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-001045)  
I'll go to the funding for the Consular and Assistance to Nationals, yung tinatawag natin ATN program.

**[01:16:16.280] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-001046)

**[01:16:26.280] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-001047)

**[01:16:31.880] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-001048)

**[01:16:33.960] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-001049)

**[01:16:39.820] Rep. Sancho Fernando "Ando" F. Oaminal** (asr-001050)  
Yes, Mr. Speaker.

**[01:16:42.380] Rep. Sancho Fernando "Ando" F. Oaminal** (asr-001051)  
The Assistance to Nationals program, as for the Assistance to Nationals program,

**[01:16:48.480] Rep. Sancho Fernando "Ando" F. Oaminal** (asr-001052)  
there is a proposed funding for 2027.

**[01:16:56.280] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-001054)

**[01:17:02.100] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-001055)

**[01:17:10.540] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-001057)

**[01:17:14.660] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-001058)

**[01:17:17.300] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-001059)  
Remember yung absorptive capacity nyo, utilization rate, mababa,

**[01:17:23.420] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-001060)  
and then you're asking for more?

**[01:17:24.800] Rep. Sancho Fernando "Ando" F. Oaminal** (asr-001061)  
Mr. Speaker,

**[01:17:26.280] Rep. Sancho Fernando "Ando" F. Oaminal** (asr-001062)  
we are talking about the Assistance to Nationals program.

**[01:17:30.020] Rep. Sancho Fernando "Ando" F. Oaminal** (asr-001063)  
Mr. Speaker, the Assistance to Nationals program

**[01:17:34.740] Rep. Sancho Fernando "Ando" F. Oaminal** (asr-001064)

**[01:17:39.060] Rep. Sancho Fernando "Ando" F. Oaminal** (asr-001065)  
It does not necessarily mean that if the proposed budget is

**[01:17:42.800] Rep. Sancho Fernando "Ando" F. Oaminal** (asr-001066)  
for discussions purposes, 1 billion,

**[01:17:47.840] Rep. Sancho Fernando "Ando" F. Oaminal** (asr-001067)  
kailangan ubusin po yan on that fiscal year.

**[01:17:51.040] Rep. Sancho Fernando "Ando" F. Oaminal** (asr-001068)

**[01:17:56.280] Rep. Sancho Fernando "Ando" F. Oaminal** (asr-001069)  
From Filipinos overseas, Mr. Chair.

**[01:18:00.140] Rep. Sancho Fernando "Ando" F. Oaminal** (asr-001070)  
So, masasabi po natin, the lower the rate of utilization,

**[01:18:08.920] Rep. Sancho Fernando "Ando" F. Oaminal** (asr-001071)  
masasabi po natin, it's better for us kasi ibig sabihin lang po nun,

**[01:18:14.320] Rep. Sancho Fernando "Ando" F. Oaminal** (asr-001072)

**[01:18:17.360] Rep. Sancho Fernando "Ando" F. Oaminal** (asr-001073)  
and pumunta po sa atin, sa DFA, para humihingi po ng tulong, Mr. Chair.

**[01:18:21.580] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-001074)  
So, insured, Mr. Speaker, insured, that's part of insurance.

**[01:18:25.120] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-001075)  
Yun ba yung gusto nyo?

**[01:18:26.280] Rep. Sancho Fernando "Ando" F. Oaminal** (asr-001076)  
Yes, Mr. Speaker. It's like an insurance fund, Mr. Speaker.

**[01:18:29.360] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-001077)

**[01:18:32.740] Rep. Sancho Fernando "Ando" F. Oaminal** (asr-001078)  
Yes, Mr. Speaker. Kasi yung prior years po, mababalik na po sa National Treasury.

**[01:18:36.680] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-001079)

**[01:18:46.200] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-001080)

**[01:18:49.340] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-001081)  
Yes.

**[01:18:52.760] Rep. Sancho Fernando "Ando" F. Oaminal** (asr-001082)  
Mr. Speaker, yung nire-request po ng DFA,

**[01:18:56.280] Rep. Sancho Fernando "Ando" F. Oaminal** (asr-001083)  
na ATN fund is around 1 billion annually and around 200 million for the Legal Assistance Fund.

**[01:19:04.420] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-001084)  
Legal Assistance Fund.

**[01:19:06.520] Rep. Sancho Fernando "Ando" F. Oaminal** (asr-001085)  
Okay.

**[01:19:07.300] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-001086)

**[01:19:12.020] Rep. Sancho Fernando "Ando" F. Oaminal** (asr-001087)

**[01:19:26.280] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-001088)  
Yeah.

**[01:19:27.480] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-001089)

**[01:19:42.820] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-001090)

**[01:19:48.300] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-001091)  
Mr. Speaker,

**[01:19:55.150] Rep. Sancho Fernando "Ando" F. Oaminal** (asr-001093)  
we're just reviewing the breakdown.

**[01:19:56.280] Rep. Sancho Fernando "Ando" F. Oaminal** (asr-001094)  
Okay, Mr. Speaker.

**[01:20:16.420] Rep. Sancho Fernando "Ando" F. Oaminal** (asr-001095)

**[01:20:25.120] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-001096)  
Yes.

**[01:20:46.420] Rep. Sancho Fernando "Ando" F. Oaminal** (asr-001099)  
Mr. Speaker, 12.4 billion will go to the consular services, Mr. Speaker.

**[01:21:10.900] Rep. Sancho Fernando "Ando" F. Oaminal** (asr-001100)  
So, kasama na po yan yung passport production,

**[01:21:15.260] Rep. Sancho Fernando "Ando" F. Oaminal** (asr-001101)  
yung issuance of apostille certificates, among others, Mr. Speaker.

**[01:21:23.660] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-001102)  
Thank you, Mr. Speaker, good sponsor.

**[01:21:27.820] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-001103)  
In relation to the budget of ATN,

**[01:21:31.600] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-001104)  
DFA explained in the previous committee briefing that part of the law ATN utilization is attributable to the creation of the Department of Migrant Workers

**[01:21:41.500] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-001105)  
and the transfer of OFW-related assistance functions.

**[01:21:45.260] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-001106)  
Is this correct?

**[01:21:46.360] Rep. Sancho Fernando "Ando" F. Oaminal** (asr-001107)  
That's correct, Mr. Speaker, kasi yung DMW po, na-devolved na po doon yung ATN funds for overseas Filipino workers or for OFWs po.

**[01:21:59.540] Rep. Sancho Fernando "Ando" F. Oaminal** (asr-001108)  
What remains under the umbrella of the DFA are overseas Filipinos who are not working abroad,

**[01:22:13.900] Rep. Sancho Fernando "Ando" F. Oaminal** (asr-001109)  
such as tourists,

**[01:22:15.100] Rep. Sancho Fernando "Ando" F. Oaminal** (asr-001110)  
Students, dual citizens who in case of emergencies nangangailangan po ng tulong, Mr. Speaker.

**[01:22:24.060] Rep. Sancho Fernando "Ando" F. Oaminal** (asr-001111)  
And also in some areas where there are no DMW satellite offices yet, Mr. Speaker.

**[01:22:34.300] Rep. Sancho Fernando "Ando" F. Oaminal** (asr-001112)  
So yung DFA pa rin po yung tumutugon sa mga pangangailangan po ng mga OFWs in those particular areas, Mr. Speaker.

**[01:22:42.460] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-001113)  
Please walk me through.

Ibang DMW natin sa mga consular natin o embassy, meron ba silang satellite office inside ng ano nyo o wala?

**[01:23:00.700] Rep. Sancho Fernando "Ando" F. Oaminal** (asr-001116)

**[01:23:10.740] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-001117)  
Inside the embassy.

Ganun ba yun yung consular nyo? Ganun ba yun?

**[01:23:14.200] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-001119)  
It's separate.

**[01:23:16.540] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-001120)

**[01:23:22.700] Rep. Sancho Fernando "Ando" F. Oaminal** (asr-001121)  
Marami po sa kanila, Mr. Speaker, are together inside the embassy.

**[01:23:26.520] Rep. Sancho Fernando "Ando" F. Oaminal** (asr-001122)  
But there are also areas where DMW is renting or leasing their offices outside po.

**[01:23:33.460] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-001123)  
Do you think that the law utilization because of overlapping, meron ba yung overlapping with DMW when it comes to ATN?

**[01:23:39.640] Rep. Sancho Fernando "Ando" F. Oaminal** (asr-001124)  
Mr. Speaker, lalabas po na.

**[01:23:42.460] Rep. Sancho Fernando "Ando" F. Oaminal** (asr-001125)  
Overlapping in terms of yung line item but yung mga tao na kinikater, again, DMW caters to OFWs po, yung DFA, yung mga Filipinos who are overseas but are not necessarily workers and OFWs who are in areas without any DMW offices, Mr. Speaker.

**[01:24:06.300] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-001126)  
Thank you, Mr. Speaker, Distinguished Sponsor.

**[01:24:08.620] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-001127)  
I have more few questions but because my time is winding down.

**[01:24:12.460] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-001128)  
I just want to follow the strict rule of 30 minutes so this will be my last questions.

**[01:24:18.340] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-001129)  
Recently, our good Secretary of Foreign Affairs, Guibo Chidoro, attended an international forum abroad.

**[01:24:26.920] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-001130)  
Siguro ay napanood na natin ito lahat because this becomes viral not only here but also internationally.

**[01:24:34.920] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-001131)  
Sa video na ito, makikita natin na may nag-abot ng note habang si Secretary Guibo ay nagsasalita.

**[01:24:42.460] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-001132)  
At habang siya ay may sinasagot na tanong on stage, Secretary Guibo, upon reading the note, stopped mid-sentence at binasa niya ang nilalaman ng note na ito na nanggaling umano sa China, allegedly from China, Chinese Embassy representatives present in the forum.

**[01:25:04.580] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-001133)  
As we saw later in the photos, naglalaman ito ng pag-atake ng China sa 2016.

**[01:25:12.460] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-001134)  
South China Sea Arbitration Ruling.

**[01:25:16.640] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-001135)  
Inawag itong iligal at ang desisyon ng UNCLOS Arbitration Tribunal ay paglabag raw sa international law.

**[01:25:26.720] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-001136)  
Secretary Guibo called this stealthy passing of the note as actual coercion, bullying, and aggression, and a sign of disrespect towards South Korea as the host.

**[01:25:39.840] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-001137)  
Thank you very much.

**[01:25:42.460] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-001138)  
Mr. Speaker, this is your sponsor. May we ask the official position of the Department of Foreign Affairs on this incident?

**[01:25:53.500] Rep. Sancho Fernando "Ando" F. Oaminal** (asr-001139)  
Mr. Speaker, the Department of Foreign Affairs respects the statements and views of the good Secretary of the Department of National Defense.

**[01:26:02.880] Rep. Sancho Fernando "Ando" F. Oaminal** (asr-001140)  
However, it maintains that it will continue to deal with these aggressions diplomatically, Mr. Speaker.

**[01:26:10.800] Rep. Sancho Fernando "Ando" F. Oaminal** (asr-001141)  
Thank you.

**[01:26:12.460] Rep. Sancho Fernando "Ando" F. Oaminal** (asr-001142)  
Mr. Speaker, are you going to file also a protest or you will lodge a protest?

**[01:26:17.400] Rep. Sancho Fernando "Ando" F. Oaminal** (asr-001143)  
Mr. Speaker, hinihintay pa po ng opisina yung buong detalye po from the incident, Mr. Speaker.

**[01:26:28.720] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-001144)  
So eventually, are you going to lodge a protest?

**[01:26:32.020] Rep. Sancho Fernando "Ando" F. Oaminal** (asr-001145)  
It will depend upon the evaluation po, Mr. Speaker, on our DFA officials.

**[01:26:39.040] Rep. Sancho Fernando "Ando" F. Oaminal** (asr-001146)  
If it is necessary to issue a diplomatic...

**[01:26:42.460] Rep. Sancho Fernando "Ando" F. Oaminal** (asr-001147)  
A diplomatic note on that matter, Mr. Speaker.

**[01:26:44.500] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-001148)  
So in short, yung evaluation nyo, is this an act of aggression yung ginawa?

**[01:26:49.200] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-001149)  
Because during a forum, may biglang aabot sa'yo, that's seldom, peculiar na makikita nyo.

**[01:26:58.180] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-001150)  
So I think this is something that we have to file a protest, no?

**[01:27:03.280] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-001151)  
Kasi hindi maganda yung ginawa.

**[01:27:05.280] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-001152)  
No less than, we were insulted, you know, because Secretaries, the alter ego of our President,

**[01:27:12.460] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-001153)  
yung nakakaya itong ginawa.

**[01:27:15.580] Rep. Sancho Fernando "Ando" F. Oaminal** (asr-001154)  
Yes, Mr. Speaker.

**[01:27:16.980] Rep. Sancho Fernando "Ando" F. Oaminal** (asr-001155)  
But the DFA maintains its stand that differences in position are better addressed through established diplomatic channels

**[01:27:24.860] Rep. Sancho Fernando "Ando" F. Oaminal** (asr-001156)  
and in a manner consistent with the courtesy and professionalism expected in official international engagements, Mr. Speaker.

**[01:27:33.420] Rep. Sancho Fernando "Ando" F. Oaminal** (asr-001157)  
Thank you, Mr. Speaker. I have no more questions.

**[01:27:38.180] Deputy Speaker Yasser Alonto Balindong** (asr-001158)  
Majority Leader.

**[01:27:40.140] Rep. Patricia C. Calderon** (asr-001159)  
Mr. Speaker, next to interpolate.

**[01:27:42.460] Rep. Patricia C. Calderon** (asr-001160)  
The sponsor, I move to recognize Representative Rene Luisco of Kabataan Partylist.

**[01:27:48.860] Deputy Speaker Yasser Alonto Balindong** (asr-001161)  
The Honorable Rene Luisco is recognized to interpellate the sponsor.

**[01:27:54.440] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-001162)  
Thank you, Mr. Speaker.

**[01:27:56.340] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-001163)  
And good morning to the distinguished sponsor and, of course, to the department.

**[01:28:01.960] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-001164)  
My first question is to clarify whether, to clarify the mandate of the DFA.

**[01:28:09.380] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-001165)  
Please correct me if I'm wrong.

**[01:28:11.440] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-001166)  
Tama po ba that the DFA...

**[01:28:12.460] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-001167)  
The DFA acts as the primary coordinator, advisor, and processing agency for the negotiation, ratification, and entry into force of treaties in the Philippines,

**[01:28:22.900] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-001168)  
supposedly determining whether an international agreement qualifies as an executive agreement or a treaty requiring legislative concurrence

**[01:28:31.000] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-001169)  
and drafting and coordinating closely with its offices on the drafts of the ratification of such treaties.

**[01:28:41.000] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-001170)  
I'm sure that the DFA...

**[01:28:42.980] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-001171)  
has many mandates.

**[01:28:44.340] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-001172)  
But may I request from the sponsor to clarify if this is accurate.

**[01:28:51.020] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-001173)  
Madam, Mr. Speaker, good sponsor.

**[01:28:53.220] Rep. Sancho Fernando "Ando" F. Oaminal** (asr-001174)  
Yes, Mr. Speaker, that is correct.

**[01:28:55.440] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-001175)  
Mr. Speaker, good sponsor, I ask this because I'd like to clarify our commitments in relation to the Basel Convention.

**[01:29:04.320] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-001176)  
We ratified the Basel Convention already in 1993, a few years after we signed it in 1989.

**[01:29:11.120] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-001177)  
But on the 5th of

**[01:29:12.460] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-001178)  
December 2019, the

**[01:29:14.460] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-001179)  
Basel Ban Amendment became

**[01:29:16.340] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-001180)  
international law and was adopted

**[01:29:18.580] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-001181)  
by the parties to the Basel

**[01:29:20.360] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-001182)  
Convention back

**[01:29:22.460] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-001183)  
in 1995. May we

**[01:29:24.600] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-001184)  
ask for an update on the

**[01:29:26.200] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-001185)  
ratification of the Basel Ban Amendment?

**[01:29:42.460] Rep. Sancho Fernando "Ando" F. Oaminal** (asr-001186)  
Mr. Speaker,

**[01:29:47.500] Rep. Sancho Fernando "Ando" F. Oaminal** (asr-001187)  
the Department is currently

**[01:29:49.060] Rep. Sancho Fernando "Ando" F. Oaminal** (asr-001188)  
checking on the status of the

**[01:29:50.700] Rep. Sancho Fernando "Ando" F. Oaminal** (asr-001189)  
and updates of the ratification.

**[01:29:53.840] Rep. Sancho Fernando "Ando" F. Oaminal** (asr-001190)  
But it

**[01:29:55.080] Rep. Sancho Fernando "Ando" F. Oaminal** (asr-001191)  
is

**[01:29:58.340] Rep. Sancho Fernando "Ando" F. Oaminal** (asr-001192)  
under the

**[01:30:00.000] Rep. Sancho Fernando "Ando" F. Oaminal** (asr-001193)  
under the view of the DFA that this is under the Department of Environment and National Resources, Mr. Speaker?

**[01:30:11.860] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-001194)  
Yes, Madam Speaker. Mr. Speaker, good sponsor.

**[01:30:15.200] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-001195)  
We also asked the DNR on the update on the ratification of the Basel ban amendment.

**[01:30:22.700] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-001196)  
In 2020, they said it's in the works and six years later, this year,

**[01:30:28.140] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-001197)  
we are given essentially the same update.

**[01:30:31.860] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-001198)  
So, given that there's almost no movement as to how we are compliant with this international agreement that we entered into,

**[01:30:42.600] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-001199)  
what can the DFA do to ensure that action and follow-through happens

**[01:30:48.000] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-001200)  
so that this agreement has translation in ratification?

**[01:30:54.260] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-001201)  
Mr. Speaker, good sponsor.

**[01:30:55.280] Rep. Sancho Fernando "Ando" F. Oaminal** (asr-001202)  
Mr. Speaker, upon checking, the DFA has ratified.

**[01:30:58.140] Rep. Sancho Fernando "Ando" F. Oaminal** (asr-001203)  
The DFA has ratified the main agreement but not the protocols, Mr. Speaker.

**[01:31:02.380] Rep. Sancho Fernando "Ando" F. Oaminal** (asr-001204)

**[01:31:10.360] Rep. Sancho Fernando "Ando" F. Oaminal** (asr-001205)  
And the DFA will coordinate with the DNR as to the status of the protocols.

**[01:31:22.440] Rep. Sancho Fernando "Ando" F. Oaminal** (asr-001207)  
Yes, that's correct, Mr. Speaker.

**[01:31:23.660] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-001208)  
Okay. Mr. Speaker, good sponsor, we'd like to request a report.

**[01:31:28.140] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-001209)
