**[01:31:37.480] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-001210)  
and is waiting on the protocols which I understand is with the DNR. Is this correct?

**[01:31:44.060] Rep. Sancho Fernando "Ando" F. Oaminal** (asr-001211)  
Yes, Mr. Speaker, that is correct.

**[01:31:45.780] Rep. Sancho Fernando "Ando" F. Oaminal** (asr-001212)  
And the DFA would willingly submit a report to the Office of the Good Congressman whenever it is ready, Mr. Speaker.

**[01:31:54.920] Rep. Sancho Fernando "Ando" F. Oaminal** (asr-001213)  
Even if there is no...

**[01:31:58.140] Rep. Sancho Fernando "Ando" F. Oaminal** (asr-001214)  
defined, definitive update yet on the protocols.

**[01:32:03.320] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-001215)  
I raise this, Mr. Speaker, good sponsor because under the Basel Convention,

**[01:32:08.660] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-001216)  
which we already ratified and it's already enacted into law,

**[01:32:12.320] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-001217)  
state parties like the Philippines cannot trade waste with non-parties

**[01:32:17.640] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-001218)  
unless there is a bilateral agreement with that non-party

**[01:32:22.500] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-001219)  
which has terms and protections equal or better than that provided in the Basel Convention.

**[01:32:28.140] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-001220)  
And in the past year, however, there's been more than 250 shipments of electronic waste

**[01:32:36.040] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-001221)  
from the United States imported by the Philippines.

**[01:32:40.040] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-001222)  
And the United States is a non-party to the Basel Convention which makes these imports illegal.

**[01:32:46.920] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-001223)  
Is the DFA aware of this, Mr. Speaker, good sponsor?

**[01:32:51.020] Rep. Sancho Fernando "Ando" F. Oaminal** (asr-001224)  
Mr. Speaker, the DFA is not aware of the information being...

**[01:32:58.140] Rep. Sancho Fernando "Ando" F. Oaminal** (asr-001225)  
presented by the good congressman.

**[01:33:01.120] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-001226)  
Mr. Speaker, good sponsor.

**[01:33:03.040] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-001227)  
We are sharing this because this is a violation of our commitments to the international community of states

**[01:33:11.480] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-001228)  
which we say, you know, that when we'd like to regulate the trade of waste,

**[01:33:16.700] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-001229)  
that should happen amongst parties and should there be trade with non-parties,

**[01:33:24.220] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-001230)  
that should be the subject of an agreement.

**[01:33:28.140] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-001231)

**[01:33:35.640] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-001232)

**[01:33:44.960] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-001233)

**[01:33:54.000] Rep. Sancho Fernando "Ando" F. Oaminal** (asr-001234)

**[01:33:58.140] Rep. Sancho Fernando "Ando" F. Oaminal** (asr-001235)

**[01:34:03.800] Rep. Sancho Fernando "Ando" F. Oaminal** (asr-001236)

**[01:34:11.300] Rep. Sancho Fernando "Ando" F. Oaminal** (asr-001237)

**[01:34:19.300] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-001238)

**[01:34:30.700] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-001241)

**[01:34:39.000] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-001242)

**[01:34:42.140] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-001243)

**[01:34:56.020] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-001247)

**[01:35:01.760] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-001250)

**[01:35:06.020] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-001251)

**[01:35:08.180] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-001252)

**[01:35:11.360] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-001253)

**[01:35:13.380] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-001254)

**[01:35:22.500] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-001256)

**[01:35:24.160] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-001257)

**[01:35:28.140] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-001258)

**[01:35:31.180] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-001259)

**[01:35:33.040] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-001260)

**[01:35:34.840] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-001261)  
any member state of the OECD, European Union, or Liechtenstein

**[01:35:40.480] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-001262)

**[01:35:43.880] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-001263)

**[01:35:56.180] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-001267)

**[02:12:18.880] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002038)

**[02:12:23.660] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002039)  
Sa mga usapang diplomatiko, meron ba ang DFA na hakbang upang masigurado na kapag ang mga Filipino seafarers ay protektado sa blacklisting,

**[02:12:35.480] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002040)  
kapag ginamit nila ang right to refuse sailing?

**[02:12:39.960] Rep. Sancho Fernando "Ando" F. Oaminal** (asr-002041)  
Yes, Mr. Speaker, tuloy-tuloy po ang communication ng DFA with other countries,

**[02:12:48.880] Rep. Sancho Fernando "Ando" F. Oaminal** (asr-002042)  
on how to protect the welfare of all, not just the seafarers, but of all overseas Filipinos working abroad, Mr. Speaker.

**[02:12:59.880] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002043)  
Maraming salamat. At ang panawagan natin, palakasin natin yung mga mekanismo para protektahan talaga sa blacklisting itong mga seafarers.

**[02:13:07.260] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002044)  
Kasi ang nangyayari daw kapag sila ay meron ng record na sila ay inexercise ang kanilang right to refuse sailing,

**[02:13:15.960] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002045)  
pagbalik po nila dito sa Pilipinas, pag-aalis na sila ulit.

**[02:13:18.880] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002046)  
So, nagkakagulatan na lang na bawal na sila, umalis o na-blacklist na sila, o hindi na po sila makapag-readme ng kanilang mga kontrata.

**[02:13:28.380] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002047)  
Na ilapit na rin natin ito sa DMW at naway makasama din po natin ang DFA sa pagpapalakas ng proteksyon, pagtataguyod ng karapatan ng ating Filipino seafarers.

**[02:13:39.460] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002048)  
Maraming salamat, Mr. Speaker. Maraming salamat sa ating distinguished sponsor.

**[02:13:43.380] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002049)  
At would the distinguished sponsor and the DFA commit to a dialogue?

**[02:13:48.880] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002050)  
And to strengthen the protections and ensure that the voices of the Filipino seafarers are heard on this important matter?

**[02:13:58.220] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002051)  
Maraming salamat.

**[02:13:59.040] Deputy Speaker Yevgeny Vincente B. Emano** (asr-002052)  
Thank you very much, Honorable Elago, Majority Leader.

**[02:14:02.140] Rep. Patricia C. Calderon** (asr-002053)

**[02:15:00.000] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002061)

**[02:15:20.180] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002064)

**[02:15:22.920] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002065)

**[02:15:30.480] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002066)

**[02:16:22.480] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002077)

**[02:16:23.920] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002078)

**[02:16:37.320] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002084)

**[02:16:44.720] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002088)

**[02:16:48.040] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002089)

**[02:17:01.200] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002091)

**[02:17:06.220] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002092)  
Pinsala sa kalusugan, pinsala sa ating environment.

**[02:17:18.380] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002095)

Sa buong kapuluan.

Yung usok mula sa

**[02:17:50.200] Rep. Sancho Fernando "Ando" F. Oaminal** (asr-002110)

**[02:17:52.580] Rep. Sancho Fernando "Ando" F. Oaminal** (asr-002111)

**[02:18:00.480] Rep. Sancho Fernando "Ando" F. Oaminal** (asr-002115)

**[02:18:10.960] Rep. Sancho Fernando "Ando" F. Oaminal** (asr-002120)

**[02:18:28.480] Rep. Sancho Fernando "Ando" F. Oaminal** (asr-002129)  
Mr. Speaker.

Yes, indeed it is, and we'll get to that.

Pero, yung dahilan, for the record,

**[02:18:35.780] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002132)  
Mr. Speaker,

**[02:18:36.840] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002133)  
alam naman nito, this is

**[02:18:40.280] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002135)  
the wildfires are, ang importante

**[02:18:42.420] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002136)  
may establish dito, dahil sa tao yan.

**[02:18:45.440] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002137)  
So, in other words, hindi ito

aksidente, hindi

**[02:18:48.260] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002139)  
ito due to natural

**[02:18:50.560] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002140)

**[02:18:52.920] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002141)

**[02:18:54.620] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002142)  
Specifically, the palm oil

**[02:18:56.740] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002143)

**[02:18:59.580] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002144)  
Every year,

**[02:19:06.480] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002148)  
para makapagtanim uli

**[02:19:07.920] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002149)  
ng

**[02:19:08.620] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002150)

**[02:19:11.440] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002151)  
ng mga puno na

**[02:19:16.800] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002153)

**[02:19:18.260] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002154)  
sa maraming mga

**[02:19:19.860] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002155)

**[02:19:22.760] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002156)

**[02:19:24.000] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002157)  
yung palm oil.

**[02:19:25.980] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002158)  
At isa sa mga pinakamalaking

**[02:19:27.940] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002159)

**[02:19:28.760] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002160)

**[02:19:33.060] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002161)

**[02:19:33.880] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002162)  
So, sinusunog po, parang sa atin,

**[02:19:35.860] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002163)

**[02:19:37.860] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002164)

**[02:19:41.260] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002165)

particular sa mga isla

ng Borneo

**[02:19:45.960] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002168)  
at Sumatra,

**[02:19:48.260] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002169)  
yung Borneo,

**[02:19:50.700] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002170)  
yung, sa particular,

yung hati ng

yung Kalimantan,

**[02:19:57.720] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002174)  
tsaka yung Sumatra.

**[02:19:59.640] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002175)  
Doon po nangyayari. At dahil po sa

habagat,

**[02:20:04.000] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002177)  
southwest, ayun, no, mula sa

**[02:20:05.900] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002178)  
southwest, ay nadadala

**[02:20:08.280] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002179)  
dito ang usok.

**[02:20:10.060] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002180)  
At,

**[02:20:10.380] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002181)  
alam nyo po ba kung hanggang

**[02:20:13.940] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002182)  
kailan daw magpapatuloy

**[02:20:16.100] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002183)  
ang haze na ito?

**[02:20:17.980] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002184)  
Ito.

**[02:20:18.260] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002185)  
Dapat alam ng DFA.

**[02:20:21.400] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002186)  
And I'll get to why

**[02:20:23.000] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002187)  
later.

**[02:20:28.200] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002188)  
Mr. Speaker,

**[02:20:30.460] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002189)  
mas alam po ng DNR

**[02:20:32.120] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002190)  
yung sagot kung hanggang kailan po ito

**[02:20:34.740] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002191)  
maramdaman dito sa

**[02:20:37.040] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002192)  
ating bansa.

**[02:20:38.680] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002193)  
Well, unfortunately,

**[02:20:40.820] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002194)  
hindi po katanggap-tanggap yun.

**[02:20:42.200] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002195)  
Dahil, why do I say so?

**[02:20:44.080] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002196)  
Because, the DFA,

**[02:20:46.740] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002197)  
of course,

**[02:20:48.260] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002198)  
would know merong kasunduan

**[02:20:50.340] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002199)  
sa ASEAN

**[02:20:51.380] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002200)  
kaugnay sa haze.

**[02:20:54.400] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002201)  
Hindi po ba?

**[02:20:55.640] Rep. Sancho Fernando "Ando" F. Oaminal** (asr-002202)  
Yes, that's correct.

**[02:20:56.600] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002203)  
Ano po yung kasunduan na ito?

**[02:20:58.180] Rep. Sancho Fernando "Ando" F. Oaminal** (asr-002204)  
Mr. Speaker, in 2002,

**[02:21:00.820] Rep. Sancho Fernando "Ando" F. Oaminal** (asr-002205)  
nagkaroon ng ASEAN

**[02:21:02.040] Rep. Sancho Fernando "Ando" F. Oaminal** (asr-002206)  
Agreement on Transboundary

**[02:21:04.460] Rep. Sancho Fernando "Ando" F. Oaminal** (asr-002207)  
Haze Pollution.

**[02:21:06.140] Rep. Sancho Fernando "Ando" F. Oaminal** (asr-002208)  
Correct.

**[02:21:08.260] Rep. Sancho Fernando "Ando" F. Oaminal** (asr-002209)  
And, it created

**[02:21:10.320] Rep. Sancho Fernando "Ando" F. Oaminal** (asr-002210)  
the ASEAN

**[02:21:12.380] Rep. Sancho Fernando "Ando" F. Oaminal** (asr-002211)  
Coordinating

**[02:21:13.880] Rep. Sancho Fernando "Ando" F. Oaminal** (asr-002212)  
Council for Trans

**[02:21:16.360] Rep. Sancho Fernando "Ando" F. Oaminal** (asr-002213)  
Boundary Haze Pollution.

**[02:21:18.260] Rep. Sancho Fernando "Ando" F. Oaminal** (asr-002214)  
Control, Mr. Speaker.

**[02:21:20.540] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002215)  
In fact, hindi po ba

**[02:21:22.160] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002216)  
sa Manila pa pinirmahan yun?

**[02:21:23.860] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002217)  
Yung pangalawang agreement na yan?

**[02:21:30.580] Rep. Sancho Fernando "Ando" F. Oaminal** (asr-002218)  
They're reviewing

**[02:21:31.720] Rep. Sancho Fernando "Ando" F. Oaminal** (asr-002219)  
the records, Mr. Speaker.

**[02:21:34.420] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002220)  
Sa Manila po.

**[02:21:36.080] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002221)  
Okay. Yung pangalawa, the one

**[02:21:38.120] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002222)  
creating the...

**[02:21:41.260] Rep. Sancho Fernando "Ando" F. Oaminal** (asr-002223)  
Mr. Speaker,

**[02:21:43.560] Rep. Sancho Fernando "Ando" F. Oaminal** (asr-002224)  
it was signed in

**[02:21:46.020] Rep. Sancho Fernando "Ando" F. Oaminal** (asr-002225)  
Malaysia in the...

**[02:21:48.260] Rep. Sancho Fernando "Ando" F. Oaminal** (asr-002226)  
10th of June

**[02:21:50.180] Rep. Sancho Fernando "Ando" F. Oaminal** (asr-002227)  
2002. Yung agreement po,

**[02:21:52.200] Rep. Sancho Fernando "Ando" F. Oaminal** (asr-002228)  
Mr. Speaker.

**[02:21:53.360] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002229)  
Yes, yung agreement. The mother agreement

**[02:21:55.980] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002230)  
in 2002. But anyway,

**[02:21:58.480] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002231)  
sige po.

**[02:21:59.700] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002232)  
So, merong agreement.

**[02:22:01.760] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002233)

**[02:53:49.440] Rep. Alexandria "Queenie" Gonzales** (asr-003137)

**[02:53:57.160] Deputy Speaker Yevgeny Vincente B. Emano** (asr-003138)

**[02:54:01.800] Deputy Speaker Yevgeny Vincente B. Emano** (asr-003139)

**[02:54:05.280] Rep. Lolita T. Javier** (asr-003140)  
Mr. Speaker, distinguished members of the House of Representatives,

**[02:54:11.840] Rep. Lolita T. Javier** (asr-003141)  
ladies and gentlemen,

**[02:54:13.380] Rep. Lolita T. Javier** (asr-003142)  
maupay nga aga haatan nga tanan.

**[02:54:17.340] Rep. Lolita T. Javier** (asr-003143)  
It is my privilege to sponsor the proposed budget of the Movie Television Review and Classification Board

**[02:54:25.180] Rep. Lolita T. Javier** (asr-003144)  
for Fiscal Year 2020.

**[02:54:27.160] Rep. Lolita T. Javier** (asr-003145)  
MTRCB, by virtue of Presidential Decree No. 1986,

**[02:54:36.080] Rep. Lolita T. Javier** (asr-003146)  
is mandated to review and classify film and television programs as to audience suitability.

**[02:54:45.260] Rep. Lolita T. Javier** (asr-003147)  
The MTRCB's strategic direction focuses on enhancing efficiency,

**[02:54:52.800] Rep. Lolita T. Javier** (asr-003148)  
modernizing operations,

**[02:54:55.680] Rep. Lolita T. Javier** (asr-003149)  
engaging the public,

**[02:54:57.160] Rep. Lolita T. Javier** (asr-003150)  
and ensuring regulatory effectiveness

**[02:54:59.700] Rep. Lolita T. Javier** (asr-003151)  
to meet the evolving needs of the media industry

**[02:55:03.980] Rep. Lolita T. Javier** (asr-003152)  
and safeguard the interest and welfare of our viewers.

**[02:55:10.440] Rep. Lolita T. Javier** (asr-003153)  
Being a self-sustaining agency,

**[02:55:14.000] Rep. Lolita T. Javier** (asr-003154)  
the MTRCB is allowed to utilize its income to fund its MOUE and capital outlay.

**[02:55:22.840] Rep. Lolita T. Javier** (asr-003155)  
Hence, only the amount of $49,000,

**[02:55:27.160] Rep. Lolita T. Javier** (asr-003156)  
$9,987 million for personal services is being asked for approval by Congress today.

**[02:55:39.000] Rep. Lolita T. Javier** (asr-003157)  
Mr. Speaker,

**[02:55:40.920] Rep. Lolita T. Javier** (asr-003158)  
dear colleagues,

**[02:55:41.880] Rep. Lolita T. Javier** (asr-003159)  
this representation is now ready for your interpolations

**[02:55:46.320] Rep. Lolita T. Javier** (asr-003160)  
and I thank you in advance for your usual support in the approval of the budget for MTRCB.

**[02:55:54.880] Rep. Lolita T. Javier** (asr-003161)  
Thank you very much, Majority Leader.

**[02:56:02.280] Rep. Alexandria "Queenie" Gonzales** (asr-003162)  
Mr. Speaker, I move to recognize Representative Presley C. De Jesus

**[02:56:07.880] Rep. Alexandria "Queenie" Gonzales** (asr-003163)  
from Partylist, Philreca,

**[02:56:09.900] Rep. Alexandria "Queenie" Gonzales** (asr-003164)  
to interpolate the sponsor on the proposed budget.

**[02:56:12.520] Deputy Speaker Yevgeny Vincente B. Emano** (asr-003165)  
The gentleman from Philreca, Honorable Presley De Jesus,

**[02:56:16.540] Deputy Speaker Yevgeny Vincente B. Emano** (asr-003166)  
is recognized to interpolate the sponsor.

**[02:56:20.160] Deputy Speaker Yevgeny Vincente B. Emano** (asr-003167)

**[02:56:21.160] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003168)  
Thank you, Mr. Speaker.

**[02:56:22.960] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003169)  
And, of course, to our MTRCB family and to our distinguished sponsor.

**[02:56:29.440] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003170)  
My first question, Mr. Speaker,

**[02:56:32.520] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003171)  
the MTRCB continues to operate principally under PD No. 1986,

**[02:56:40.860] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003172)  
which was issued in 1985

**[02:56:43.040] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003173)  
before streaming services, video-on-demand platforms,

**[02:56:49.040] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003174)  
social media,

**[02:56:51.200] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003175)  
and user-generated content,

**[02:56:52.960] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003176)  
and content became major sources of entertainment.

**[02:56:57.380] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003177)  
While conventional films and television programs

**[02:57:00.680] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003178)  
are subject to MTRCB review and classification,

**[02:57:07.120] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003179)  
the Board has acknowledged that its current authority

**[02:57:10.940] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003180)  
does not expressly extend to platforms such as Netflix,

**[02:57:19.180] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003181)  
Disney,

**[02:57:20.600] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003182)  
HBO Max,

**[02:57:21.480] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003183)  
and TV.

**[02:57:26.500] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003186)  
In September 2026,

**[02:57:29.800] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003187)  
the MTRCB stated that

**[02:57:31.760] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003188)  
it was prepared to regulate streaming services

**[02:57:35.660] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003189)  
if Congress expands its jurisdiction.

**[02:57:41.160] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003190)  
My question, Mr. Speaker, distinguished sponsor,

**[02:57:44.980] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003191)  
given the public's increasing reliance on streaming services,

**[02:57:48.640] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003192)  
does the MTRCB believe that presidential decree numbering,

**[02:57:52.960] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003193)  
No. 1986, remains adequate or should Congress now modernize its mandate?

**[02:58:01.320] Rep. Lolita T. Javier** (asr-003194)  
Mr. Speaker,

**[02:58:04.120] Rep. Lolita T. Javier** (asr-003195)  
it is correct that the MTRCB should remain

**[02:58:10.840] Rep. Lolita T. Javier** (asr-003196)  
and that we should expand the scope of the MTRCB

**[02:58:16.240] Rep. Lolita T. Javier** (asr-003197)  
so that all platforms or content

**[02:58:21.080] Rep. Lolita T. Javier** (asr-003198)  
can be addressed.

**[02:58:22.920] Rep. Lolita T. Javier** (asr-003199)  
Because as of now,

**[02:58:25.060] Rep. Lolita T. Javier** (asr-003200)  
the presidential decree numbering No. 1986

**[02:58:30.600] Rep. Lolita T. Javier** (asr-003201)  
is only concerned with not all.

**[02:58:33.840] Rep. Lolita T. Javier** (asr-003202)  
So, we should have a new House Bill

**[02:58:40.040] Rep. Lolita T. Javier** (asr-003203)  
to expand the scope of the MTRCB, Mr. Chair.

**[02:58:47.000] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003204)  
Mr. Speaker,

**[02:58:49.360] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003205)  
you know what? Because of the new platform po kasi,

**[02:58:51.640] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003206)  
lahat ho tayo

**[02:58:52.760] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003207)  
hindi na nagpupunta ng sinehan,

**[02:58:54.800] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003208)  
bihira na.

**[02:58:56.240] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003209)  
Of course, I remember vividly, Mr. Speaker,

**[02:58:59.520] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003210)  
back then when I was in high school, college,

**[02:59:02.040] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003211)  
ano, panonood pa ni

**[02:59:02.280] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003212)  
Eddie Garcia

**[02:59:03.600] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003213)  
noong mga araw-araw nasa sinehan ako.

**[02:59:06.840] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003214)  
I'm a Sharon Cuneta fan.

**[02:59:09.400] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003215)  
Lahat ng pelikula niya,

**[02:59:10.460] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003216)  
from The Heart,

**[02:59:11.840] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003217)  
hanggang ano, pinanonood namin.

**[02:59:13.440] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003218)  
Pero ngayon,

**[02:59:14.780] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003219)  
sorry, pero ngayon,

**[02:59:18.760] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003220)  
tuwing Christmas na lang tayo

**[02:59:21.000] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003221)  
nakakapanood, no?

**[02:59:21.680] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003222)  
So it's a dying industry

**[02:59:22.760] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003223)  
na dapat

**[02:59:24.060] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003224)  
natin

**[02:59:25.020] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003225)  
palakasin.

**[02:59:25.560] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003227)  
That's why, Mr. Speaker,

**[02:59:28.460] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003228)  
Madam Sponsor,

**[02:59:29.340] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003229)  
noon pa man,

**[02:59:30.300] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003230)  
I've been advocating

**[02:59:31.320] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003231)  
na dapat palakasin natin

**[02:59:33.520] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003232)  
ang ating local industry,

**[02:59:37.000] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003233)  
lalo na itong MTRCB,

**[02:59:39.280] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003234)  
because MTRCB,

**[02:59:40.920] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003235)  
Mr. Speaker,

**[02:59:41.700] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003236)  
hawak lang niya

**[02:59:42.520] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003237)  
TV and television,

**[02:59:47.140] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003238)  
TV and, of course, the movie, no?

**[02:59:50.540] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003239)  
Like in Korea,

**[02:59:52.040] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003240)  
meron sinatawag,

**[02:59:52.760] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003241)  
ng Korean Media

**[02:59:54.380] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003242)  
Rating Board

**[02:59:55.960] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003243)  
kung saan, pati concert

**[02:59:58.960] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003244)  
no

**[03:00:00.000] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003245)  
ay dumaadan sa kanila at meron silang kita rito.

**[03:00:04.520] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003246)  
So dito po ang MTRCB, if they rely only on a meager budget po,

**[03:00:11.540] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003247)  
so paano nila mapapatupad ang kanilang mandato?

**[03:00:15.540] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003248)  
Lalo ngayon, wala na po nanonood sa sinihan.

**[03:00:21.940] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003249)  
Birang-birang na po.

**[03:00:23.640] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003250)  
So ano ba ang pwedeng gawin ng MTRCB?

**[03:00:27.440] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003251)  
What particular gaps in the present law prevent the MTRCB from responding to complaints involving streaming content?

**[03:00:39.000] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003252)  
Under PD 1986, kaya ba nyo?

**[03:00:42.100] Rep. Lolita T. Javier** (asr-003253)  
Balik din po tayo.

**[03:00:43.720] Rep. Lolita T. Javier** (asr-003254)  
Isa lang yung sagot dyan.

**[03:00:45.560] Rep. Lolita T. Javier** (asr-003255)  
Dahil nga po sa decree ng 1986 na nanatiling movie and television lang po ang sakop nila.

**[03:00:53.780] Rep. Lolita T. Javier** (asr-003256)  
Kaya hinihikayat ko po ang Kongreso,

**[03:00:55.780] Rep. Lolita T. Javier** (asr-003257)  
na magtulungan tayo upang masuportahan at mapalawak ang mithiin po ng MTRCB

**[03:01:04.660] Rep. Lolita T. Javier** (asr-003258)  
na maging sakop din po nila yung iba-ibang platform para po magkaroon tayo

**[03:01:13.160] Rep. Lolita T. Javier** (asr-003259)  
ng pinakamaayos at dekalidad na mga palabas.

**[03:01:22.920] Rep. Lolita T. Javier** (asr-003260)  
At kaya naman po nilang...

**[03:01:25.780] Rep. Lolita T. Javier** (asr-003261)  
Tugunan ang MTRCB lahat, hindi lang movies, hindi lang television, kahit ho yung Netflix, kayang-kaya po nila yan.

**[03:01:33.660] Rep. Lolita T. Javier** (asr-003262)  
And I have faith na talagang gagawin ito dahil naniniwala po ang MTRCB sa sining nating mga Pilipino.

**[03:01:42.460] Rep. Lolita T. Javier** (asr-003263)  
Mr. Chair.

**[03:01:43.460] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003264)  
Thank you, Mrs. Speaker.

**[03:01:45.460] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003265)  
My follow-up question, Madam Sponsor.

**[03:01:47.460] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003266)  
Does the MTRCB recommend simply amending Presidential Decree No. 1986?

**[03:01:55.780] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003267)  
Enacting an entirely new MTRCB Charter or creating a separate law governing online audiovisual services?

**[03:02:05.460] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003268)  
Kailangan ko ba natin pala...

**[03:02:08.460] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003269)  
We all know that we're going to make sure that ang PD 1986 eh parang kulang sa ngipin, no?

**[03:02:18.460] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003270)  
So do we need a separate law para ito sa mga online audiovisual services?

**[03:02:25.780] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003271)  
Ay kailangan masama under ng MTRCB?

**[03:02:29.780] Rep. Lolita T. Javier** (asr-003272)  
Yes, Mr. Chair.

**[03:02:31.780] Rep. Lolita T. Javier** (asr-003273)  
Tama po kayo.

**[03:02:33.780] Rep. Lolita T. Javier** (asr-003274)  
Gusto ko po doon sa sinasabi nyo na kailangan ng panibagong batas para dito.

**[03:02:40.780] Rep. Lolita T. Javier** (asr-003275)  
Para po tama kayo na itong ngipin na sinasabi nyo na parang dalawa lang yung nasasakupan.

**[03:02:50.780] Rep. Lolita T. Javier** (asr-003276)  
So kung magkakaroon tayo ng bagong batas,

**[03:02:53.780] Rep. Lolita T. Javier** (asr-003277)  
sa pamamigitan ng support natin dito,

**[03:02:55.780] Rep. Lolita T. Javier** (asr-003278)  
sa House of Representatives,

**[03:02:57.780] Rep. Lolita T. Javier** (asr-003279)  
ay mapapalawak po natin ang saklaw, ang sakop po ng ahensya.

**[03:03:02.780] Rep. Lolita T. Javier** (asr-003280)  
At maging content regulatory board po ang tawag doon.

**[03:03:08.780] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003281)  
So ito ho bang sample?

**[03:03:10.780] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003282)  
Netflix, HBO, Disney, at yung paborito ni Kong Jet, yung Viva Max.

**[03:03:19.780] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003283)  
Eh nakakuha ho ba kayo ng ano dito?

**[03:03:22.780] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003284)  
Kahit pa paano ng fees?

**[03:03:24.780] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003285)  
Share po pag ito po ino-online o nag-streaming sa ating bansa?

**[03:03:31.780] Rep. Lolita T. Javier** (asr-003286)  
Wala po.

**[03:03:33.780] Rep. Lolita T. Javier** (asr-003287)  
Ang MTRCB po ay talagang tinitinglan lang nila yung video and classification.

**[03:03:41.780] Rep. Lolita T. Javier** (asr-003288)  
Kaya nga hinihikayat ko tayo dito na magtulungan para nga po magkaroon tayo ng panibagong batas.

**[03:03:50.780] Rep. Lolita T. Javier** (asr-003289)  
Para masakop nila, masaklaw nila.

**[03:03:52.780] Rep. Lolita T. Javier** (asr-003290)  
Yung lahat ng pinangalanan niyo kanina.

**[03:03:56.780] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003291)  
So in short, Mrs. Speaker, no?

**[03:03:59.780] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003292)  
Ah, ang MTRCB wala talagang ipin pagdating sa mga streaming neto.

**[03:04:05.780] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003293)  
Kasi ngayon, no, halos wala na nagpupunta ng siniyan, no?

**[03:04:09.780] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003294)  
Or bihira na manood ng TV, free TV.

**[03:04:13.780] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003295)  
So ano ang gusto niyong gawin na batas para maisama to?

**[03:04:18.780] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003296)  
Kasi ang number one dito, importante rito, itong mga kabataan.

**[03:04:21.780] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003297)  
Kasi dito, kung hindi niyo maka-classify, napakarami na ito.

**[03:04:26.780] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003298)  
I think you need AI para ma-

**[03:04:29.780] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003299)  
Ilan ho ba ang nagka-classify sa MTRCB pagdating po dito sa mga streaming na ganito?

**[03:04:35.780] Rep. Lolita T. Javier** (asr-003300)  
Ilan ba tayo?

**[03:04:39.780] Rep. Lolita T. Javier** (asr-003301)  
Valley 31 po ang board members na tumitingin sa mga nag-review.

**[03:04:48.780] Rep. Lolita T. Javier** (asr-003302)  
Kaya nga po, Mr. Chair,

**[03:04:51.780] Rep. Lolita T. Javier** (asr-003303)  
kailangan po natin masuportahan para mas madami pa silang empleyado.

**[03:04:58.780] Rep. Lolita T. Javier** (asr-003304)  
At kung gagawa tayo ng panibagong batas, mas malaki ang saklaw na ma-review po nila.

**[03:05:06.780] Rep. Lolita T. Javier** (asr-003305)  
Hindi na lang yung television and movies.

**[03:05:11.780] Rep. Lolita T. Javier** (asr-003306)  
Pati na rin po yung Vivamox, yung Netflix, kung ano pa man yung mga other social media platforms, Mr. Chair.

**[03:05:20.780] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003307)  
Ano mo, Mr. Chair, Mr. Speaker, nakakalunos naman ang MTRCB.

**[03:05:27.780] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003308)

**[03:05:40.780] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003309)

**[03:05:45.780] Rep. Lolita T. Javier** (asr-003310)

**[03:05:49.780] Rep. Lolita T. Javier** (asr-003311)  
Pero bagkos dahil sa marami silang nire-review, sila po ay self-generating agency.

**[03:05:56.780] Rep. Lolita T. Javier** (asr-003312)

**[03:06:00.780] Rep. Lolita T. Javier** (asr-003313)

**[03:06:10.780] Rep. Lolita T. Javier** (asr-003314)

**[03:06:17.780] Rep. Lolita T. Javier** (asr-003315)

**[03:06:19.780] Rep. Lolita T. Javier** (asr-003316)

**[03:06:28.780] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003318)

**[03:06:36.780] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003319)

**[03:06:46.780] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003320)

**[03:06:48.780] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003321)

**[03:06:53.780] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003322)

**[03:07:01.780] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003323)

**[03:07:05.780] Rep. Lolita T. Javier** (asr-003324)

**[03:07:16.780] Rep. Lolita T. Javier** (asr-003325)

Thank you, Madam Sponsor.

**[03:07:24.780] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003327)

**[03:07:36.780] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003328)

**[03:07:45.780] Rep. Lolita T. Javier** (asr-003331)

**[03:07:56.780] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003332)

**[03:08:03.780] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003333)

**[03:08:04.780] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003334)

**[03:08:11.780] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003335)  
So if we file a bill,

**[03:08:13.780] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003336)  
para pag naisama to, ano sa tingin mo ang magiging priority niyo o functions niyo dito?

**[03:08:21.780] Rep. Lolita T. Javier** (asr-003337)

**[03:08:35.780] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003338)

**[03:08:43.780] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003339)

**[03:08:47.780] Rep. Lolita T. Javier** (asr-003340)

**[03:08:52.780] Rep. Lolita T. Javier** (asr-003342)

**[03:08:54.780] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003343)

**[03:09:00.780] Rep. Lolita T. Javier** (asr-003344)

**[03:09:11.780] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003345)

**[03:09:19.780] Rep. Lolita T. Javier** (asr-003348)

**[03:09:48.780] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003353)

**[03:10:00.780] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003354)

**[03:10:37.780] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003364)

**[03:10:39.780] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003365)

**[03:10:45.680] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003366)

**[03:11:02.980] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003368)

**[03:11:11.080] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003369)

**[03:11:15.520] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003370)

**[03:11:22.660] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003371)

**[03:11:30.480] Rep. Lolita T. Javier** (asr-003372)  
Bago po natin mailagay on air,

**[03:11:32.980] Rep. Lolita T. Javier** (asr-003373)  
nire-review po yan ng MTRCB.

**[03:11:36.720] Rep. Lolita T. Javier** (asr-003374)  
From the start, talagang nire-review nila yun.

**[03:11:40.700] Rep. Lolita T. Javier** (asr-003375)  
Mula noong 2025, pinalakas po ng MTRCB ang responsabling panunood campaign

**[03:11:48.440] Rep. Lolita T. Javier** (asr-003376)  
sa pamamagitan ng PROTECT framework.

**[03:11:52.060] Rep. Lolita T. Javier** (asr-003377)  
Ang PROTECT framework po ay

**[03:11:54.360] Rep. Lolita T. Javier** (asr-003378)  
ang P, provide guidance on age-appropriate content.

**[03:12:00.700] Rep. Lolita T. Javier** (asr-003379)  
Yung R, regulate.

**[03:12:02.980] Rep. Lolita T. Javier** (asr-003380)  
A, create screen time and set healthy limits.

**[03:12:07.160] Rep. Lolita T. Javier** (asr-003381)  
O, observe co-viewing and family discussions.

**[03:12:12.220] Rep. Lolita T. Javier** (asr-003382)  
T, teach medial literacy.

**[03:12:15.720] Rep. Lolita T. Javier** (asr-003383)  
And E, educate children on the impact of media.

**[03:12:20.140] Rep. Lolita T. Javier** (asr-003384)  
And C, create a family viewing plan.

**[03:12:24.480] Rep. Lolita T. Javier** (asr-003385)  
T, take the lead by setting a good example in content consumption.

**[03:12:30.480] Rep. Lolita T. Javier** (asr-003386)  
So, yung PROTECT,

**[03:12:33.140] Rep. Lolita T. Javier** (asr-003387)  
framework po,

**[03:12:34.920] Rep. Lolita T. Javier** (asr-003388)  
Mr. Chair,

**[03:12:36.680] Rep. Lolita T. Javier** (asr-003389)

**[03:12:39.760] Rep. Lolita T. Javier** (asr-003390)  
May surveys,

**[03:12:41.600] Rep. Lolita T. Javier** (asr-003391)

**[03:12:44.420] Rep. Lolita T. Javier** (asr-003392)  
Tapos, meron din post-engagement surveys

**[03:12:47.480] Rep. Lolita T. Javier** (asr-003393)

**[03:12:51.040] Rep. Lolita T. Javier** (asr-003394)  
Yung impact assessments sa mga komunidad

**[03:12:56.560] Rep. Lolita T. Javier** (asr-003396)

**[03:45:13.480] Rep. Alyssa Michaela "Mica" M. Gonzales** (asr-004203)

**[03:45:21.000] Deputy Speaker Yasser Alonto Balindong** (asr-004204)

**[03:45:34.480] Deputy Speaker Yasser Alonto Balindong** (asr-004206)

**[03:45:36.480] Rep. Alyssa Michaela "Mica" M. Gonzales** (asr-004207)  
Mr. Speaker, I move to recognize Representative Vice Chair Dolita T. Javier,

**[03:45:42.600] Rep. Alyssa Michaela "Mica" M. Gonzales** (asr-004208)  
Vice Chairperson of the Committee on Appropriations,

**[03:45:45.300] Rep. Alyssa Michaela "Mica" M. Gonzales** (asr-004209)  
to answer questions on the proposed budget of the Film Development Council of the Philippines.

**[03:45:51.000] Deputy Speaker Yasser Alonto Balindong** (asr-004210)  
The Honorable Vice Chair Dolita Javier is recognized.

**[03:45:56.080] Rep. Lolita T. Javier** (asr-004211)  
Mr. Speaker, Distinguished Members of the House of Representatives,

**[03:46:01.860] Rep. Lolita T. Javier** (asr-004212)  
Ladies and Gentlemen, Maupay Nga Kulop Haton Nga Tanan,

**[03:46:06.620] Rep. Lolita T. Javier** (asr-004213)  
It is my privilege to sponsor the proposed budget of the Film Development Council of the Philippines for Fiscal Year 2027.

**[03:46:16.300] Rep. Lolita T. Javier** (asr-004214)  
The FDCP, which was created by virtue of Republic Touch,

**[03:46:21.000] Rep. Lolita T. Javier** (asr-004215)  
Act No. 9167,

**[03:46:24.340] Rep. Lolita T. Javier** (asr-004216)  
encourages the creation of quality films and content,

**[03:46:29.420] Rep. Lolita T. Javier** (asr-004217)  
from development to production,

**[03:46:32.360] Rep. Lolita T. Javier** (asr-004218)  
exhibition and distribution.

**[03:46:35.180] Rep. Lolita T. Javier** (asr-004219)  
The FDCP also conducts film-related events

**[03:46:40.800] Rep. Lolita T. Javier** (asr-004220)  
that enhance the skills of Filipino talents

**[03:46:44.900] Rep. Lolita T. Javier** (asr-004221)  
and amplify economic and cultural opportunities for film industry,

**[03:46:51.000] Rep. Lolita T. Javier** (asr-004222)  
as well as the participation of the FDCP's stakeholders.

**[03:46:54.940] Rep. Lolita T. Javier** (asr-004223)  
In addition,

**[03:46:56.240] Rep. Lolita T. Javier** (asr-004224)  
the FDCP leads the film markets and film festivals

**[03:47:00.580] Rep. Lolita T. Javier** (asr-004225)  
to promote and position Philippine cinema to be globally competitive.

**[03:47:06.340] Rep. Lolita T. Javier** (asr-004226)  
The Council also works to preserve and protect films

**[03:47:12.220] Rep. Lolita T. Javier** (asr-004227)  
as part of the country's national and cultural heritage

**[03:47:17.260] Rep. Lolita T. Javier** (asr-004228)  
through film archiving.

**[03:47:19.660] Rep. Lolita T. Javier** (asr-004229)  
For Fiscal Year 2027,

**[03:47:20.940] Rep. Lolita T. Javier** (asr-004230)  
the FDCP proposed a budget of P311,743 million.

**[03:47:28.940] Rep. Lolita T. Javier** (asr-004231)  
It is P55.606 million,

**[03:47:32.240] Rep. Lolita T. Javier** (asr-004232)  
or 15.14% lower compared to the Fiscal Year 2026 budget of P367.349 million.

**[03:47:44.340] Rep. Lolita T. Javier** (asr-004233)  
Mr. Speaker, my dear colleagues,

**[03:47:46.860] Rep. Lolita T. Javier** (asr-004234)  
I solicit your kind support for the approval

**[03:47:50.300] Rep. Lolita T. Javier** (asr-004235)  
of the FDCP's budget for the Fiscal Year 2026.

**[03:47:50.920] Rep. Lolita T. Javier** (asr-004236)  
I also request the approval of the budget

**[03:47:51.620] Rep. Lolita T. Javier** (asr-004237)  
of the Film Development Council of the Philippines.

**[03:47:55.300] Rep. Lolita T. Javier** (asr-004238)

Salamaton

**[03:48:01.440] Rep. Lolita T. Javier** (asr-004240)  
tanan.

**[03:48:03.620] Rep. Lolita T. Javier** (asr-004241)

Honorable Journey Jet Nisay,

**[03:48:22.800] Rep. Jernie Jett V. Nisay** (asr-004249)  
Mr. Speaker, Madam Sponsor,

**[03:48:25.620] Rep. Jernie Jett V. Nisay** (asr-004250)  
at sa atin pong Film Development Council of the Philippines,

**[03:48:28.540] Rep. Jernie Jett V. Nisay** (asr-004251)

**[03:48:30.360] Rep. Jernie Jett V. Nisay** (asr-004252)  
Una pong katanungan para sa budget reduction and industry support,

**[03:48:35.180] Rep. Jernie Jett V. Nisay** (asr-004253)  
Mr. Speaker, Madam Sponsor,

**[03:48:37.800] Rep. Jernie Jett V. Nisay** (asr-004254)  
Para po sa Film Development Council of the Philippines,

**[03:48:41.380] Rep. Jernie Jett V. Nisay** (asr-004255)  
bilang ahensya na tumutulong sa paggawa,

**[03:48:45.060] Rep. Jernie Jett V. Nisay** (asr-004256)  
pag-promote at pagpreserba ng pelikulang Pilipino,

**[03:48:48.200] Rep. Jernie Jett V. Nisay** (asr-004257)  
ay kailangang malinaw kung

**[03:48:50.200] Rep. Jernie Jett V. Nisay** (asr-004258)  
paano mapanatili ang serbisyo

**[03:48:52.420] Rep. Jernie Jett V. Nisay** (asr-004259)  
at oportunidad

**[03:48:53.760] Rep. Jernie Jett V. Nisay** (asr-004260)  
para sa filmmakers at film workers.

**[03:48:57.820] Rep. Jernie Jett V. Nisay** (asr-004261)  
At sa proposed 2027 budget

**[03:49:00.380] Rep. Jernie Jett V. Nisay** (asr-004262)  
nila na umaabot po ng 300.7 million,

**[03:49:04.880] Rep. Jernie Jett V. Nisay** (asr-004263)  
paano tinitiyak

**[03:49:05.960] Rep. Jernie Jett V. Nisay** (asr-004264)  
ng Film Development Council

**[03:49:08.000] Rep. Jernie Jett V. Nisay** (asr-004265)  
ang suporta nito sa Pilipino filmmakers,

**[03:49:11.800] Rep. Jernie Jett V. Nisay** (asr-004266)  
film workers,

**[03:49:12.840] Rep. Jernie Jett V. Nisay** (asr-004267)  
at sa development ng ating local film industry?

**[03:49:17.000] Rep. Lolita T. Javier** (asr-004268)  
Mr. Chair,

**[03:49:20.200] Rep. Lolita T. Javier** (asr-004280)  
FDCP po ay malawak ang pangarap para po sa ating sining, sa ating bansa.

**[03:49:29.520] Rep. Lolita T. Javier** (asr-004281)  
Meron po tayong mga scholars na mga kabataan all over the Philippines,

**[03:49:35.420] Rep. Lolita T. Javier** (asr-004282)  
filmmakers nationwide.

**[03:49:37.920] Rep. Lolita T. Javier** (asr-004283)  
Mga scholars po sila.

**[03:49:39.560] Rep. Lolita T. Javier** (asr-004284)  
Minsan nakakaawa nga po kasi as of kwento ni Director Joy,

**[03:49:47.400] Rep. Lolita T. Javier** (asr-004285)  
yung budget na binibigay lang minsan ay P50,000 para sa mga filmmakers natin.

**[03:49:56.160] Rep. Lolita T. Javier** (asr-004286)  
Kung hindi natin susuportahan, mawawalan po tayo ng mga bagong idea

**[03:50:02.520] Rep. Lolita T. Javier** (asr-004287)  
kung paano po gumawa ng pindikalidad na movie sa ating bansa.

**[03:50:07.500] Rep. Lolita T. Javier** (asr-004288)  
At hindi po natin, para sa ahensya na ito,

**[03:50:13.180] Rep. Lolita T. Javier** (asr-004289)  
hindi po natin pwedeng hayaan na mawala yun sa ating,

**[03:50:17.400] Rep. Lolita T. Javier** (asr-004290)  
dahil ang ibang bansa po ay niyayakap po nila talaga

**[03:50:22.600] Rep. Lolita T. Javier** (asr-004291)  
ang mga talento ng kanilang mga artista.

**[03:50:27.640] Rep. Lolita T. Javier** (asr-004292)  
Dito po sa atin, meron tayong mga scholars nga po nationwide

**[03:50:34.360] Rep. Lolita T. Javier** (asr-004293)  
na sinusuportahan po natin.

**[03:50:36.800] Rep. Lolita T. Javier** (asr-004294)  
Maliban pa po doon, ang prayer po talaga ng FDCP

**[03:50:41.860] Rep. Lolita T. Javier** (asr-004295)  
ay magkaroon po tayo ng film archiving

**[03:50:46.260] Rep. Lolita T. Javier** (asr-004296)  
na kung saan,

**[03:50:47.400] Rep. Lolita T. Javier** (asr-004297)  
nawawala na po ang kasaysayan ng pelikulang Pilipino.

**[03:50:53.400] Rep. Lolita T. Javier** (asr-004298)  
Meron po tayong mga lumang movies na nakukuha pa po sa ibang bansa.

**[03:50:59.400] Rep. Lolita T. Javier** (asr-004299)  
Imaginin po natin, sarili nating mga pelikula nung mga 1930s,

**[03:51:03.400] Rep. Lolita T. Javier** (asr-004300)  
mga early 1900s, na wala na po sa ating bansa.

**[03:51:08.400] Rep. Lolita T. Javier** (asr-004301)  
Nasa ibang bansa pa po natin nakukuha.

**[03:51:10.400] Rep. Lolita T. Javier** (asr-004302)  
At hindi po ganun kamura para po i-restore natin yung mga old movies.

**[03:51:16.400] Rep. Lolita T. Javier** (asr-004303)  
At hindi po ganun kamura para po i-restore natin yung mga old movies.

**[03:51:17.400] Rep. Lolita T. Javier** (asr-004304)  
Ang isang pelikula po na luma ay, it depends po kung gaano kalawak yung sira.

**[03:51:26.400] Rep. Lolita T. Javier** (asr-004305)  
Minsan po, ang isang movie ay nagkakahalaga ng 2 million para ma-restore po ito.

**[03:51:33.400] Rep. Lolita T. Javier** (asr-004306)  
Kung hindi naman ganun kalaki ang damage, umaabot pa rin po ng isang million.

**[03:51:40.400] Rep. Lolita T. Javier** (asr-004307)  
So million po talaga ang halaga ng bawat isang pelikula

**[03:51:45.400] Rep. Lolita T. Javier** (asr-004308)  
para maitago natin, mailagay natin sa archive yung kasaysayan ng pelikulang Pilipino, Mr. Chair.

**[03:51:55.400] Rep. Jernie Jett V. Nisay** (asr-004309)  
Maraming salamat, Madam Sponsor.

**[03:51:58.400] Rep. Jernie Jett V. Nisay** (asr-004310)  
Ang susunod ko pong katanungan ay may kinalaman sa Film Development Council Grants.

**[03:52:03.400] Rep. Jernie Jett V. Nisay** (asr-004311)  
Mr. Speaker, Madam Sponsor, sa 2027, target ng Film Development Council na 30% ng films na bibigyan ng incentives o grants

**[03:52:13.400] Rep. Jernie Jett V. Nisay** (asr-004312)  
ay maibigay based on merit.

**[03:52:14.400] Rep. Jernie Jett V. Nisay** (asr-004313)  
Habang 50% naman ng award-winning producers, directors, at actors ay bibigyan ng priority sa pag-avail ng incentives o grants.

**[03:52:24.400] Rep. Jernie Jett V. Nisay** (asr-004314)

**[03:52:37.400] Rep. Lolita T. Javier** (asr-004316)

**[03:52:49.400] Rep. Lolita T. Javier** (asr-004318)

**[03:53:04.400] Rep. Jernie Jett V. Nisay** (asr-004319)

**[03:54:13.400] Rep. Lolita T. Javier** (asr-004325)

**[03:54:43.400] Rep. Lolita T. Javier** (asr-004326)  
and archiving. Through this program, the government reinforces its commitment to nurturing quality Filipino films and promoting the growth of Philippine cinema. Mr. Chair.

**[04:01:34.340] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-004379)

**[04:01:43.460] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-004380)  
So, of course, while we commend the FDCP for increasing the Create PH Films

**[04:01:48.960] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-004381)  
project development grant from 300,000 to 500,000, we must look at the

**[04:01:54.260] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-004382)  
operational reality on the ground.

**[04:01:56.400] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-004383)  
Under current auditing guidelines, grant distributions are heavily reliant on tranche liquidations and post-expense reimbursements. Madali lang po 'yun for established production houses. Kaya po nilang i-outlay 'yung 500,000 to 1 million upfront while waiting for government tranche releases. 'Yun nga lang po, for young emerging independent storytellers without large capital reserves, this upfront expense requirement is a massive barrier to entry. Paano po ba, ano pong ginagawa ng FDCP upang ma-address itong cash flow bottleneck para ma-ensure na 'yung mga maaawardan ng grants hindi po maihinto sa early production dahil wala pong immediate operational capital? Ito po kasi 'yung kinonsulta natin sa ating mga young filmmakers na issue kung bakit hindi po sila makapagsimula in the first place.

**[04:03:58.820] Rep. Lolita T. Javier** (asr-004431)

838,000.

Yes.

**[04:22:33.800] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004652)

well,

normally,

pero,

**[04:22:44.820] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004663)

**[04:22:46.980] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004665)  
This time around though,

**[04:22:48.860] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004666)  
na obliga tayo

**[04:22:49.960] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004667)  
dahil

**[04:22:50.600] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004668)  
no less

**[04:22:51.120] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004669)  
than the President

**[04:22:51.880] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004670)  
mentioned

**[04:22:53.240] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004671)  
the Philippines Space Agency

**[04:22:54.740] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004672)  
in his SONA.

**[04:22:56.320] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004673)  
Di ba?

**[04:22:56.620] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004674)  
Isa sa mga

**[04:22:57.200] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004675)  
highlights

**[04:22:58.220] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004676)  
nung SONA

**[04:22:58.900] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004677)  
ay,

**[04:22:59.140] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004678)  
Sinabi niya, by 2027, the Philippines will launch its first ever sounding rocket in Cagayan in 2027.

**[04:23:16.040] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004679)  
Yun po ang gusto nating alamin.

**[04:23:19.280] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004680)  
At dun ba gagamitin yung budget na ito?

**[04:23:22.380] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004681)  
Particularly the P739.9 million for research and development, policy development, and capacity building.

**[04:23:31.840] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004682)  
Could you tell us more about this program of VILSA, yung rocket?

**[04:23:39.660] Rep. John Geesnell "Baba" Yap** (asr-004683)  
Yes, Mr. Speaker. Only a very small portion of that will be used for the launch of the sounding rocket.

**[04:23:45.700] Rep. John Geesnell "Baba" Yap** (asr-004684)  
But to give you an update on the pronouncement of the President last sauna,

**[04:23:51.860] Rep. John Geesnell "Baba" Yap** (asr-004685)  
last August 4, official project kick-off with the VILSA, DICT, CESA, Ascend International Gateway,

**[04:23:59.840] Rep. John Geesnell "Baba" Yap** (asr-004686)  
Perigee Aerospace Incorporated, and Perigee Manila,

**[04:24:03.160] Rep. John Geesnell "Baba" Yap** (asr-004687)  
affirming its commitment towards implementing the test launch of the sounding rocket within the first quarter of 2027.

**[04:24:11.100] Rep. John Geesnell "Baba" Yap** (asr-004688)  
And last August 11, VILSA engineers left for Korea to undertake 10 weeks of technical and hands-on training on building the sounding rocket.

**[04:24:19.700] Rep. John Geesnell "Baba" Yap** (asr-004689)  
And up to this date, the flight...

**[04:24:21.860] Rep. John Geesnell "Baba" Yap** (asr-004690)  
The flight model of the rocket is being assembled with tests to be undertaken.

**[04:24:26.180] Rep. John Geesnell "Baba" Yap** (asr-004691)  
The rocket is set to be completed and shipped to the Philippines in October on track for the scheduled launch next year, Mr. Speaker.

**[04:24:33.760] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004692)  
Okay, so nabanggit niyo po, VILSA involved with other agencies, including a private company called Perigee.

**[04:24:43.460] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004693)  
Korean firm po ba ito?

**[04:24:45.980] Rep. John Geesnell "Baba" Yap** (asr-004694)  
Yes, it is a Korean firm, Mr. Speaker.

**[04:24:48.240] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004695)  
At ito yung kapartner ng VILSA.

**[04:24:51.860] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004696)  
Sa pag-develop at pag-manufacture ng rocket, tama po ba?

**[04:24:56.120] Rep. John Geesnell "Baba" Yap** (asr-004697)  
Tama, Mr. Speaker.

**[04:24:57.300] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004698)  
Okay. At tinatawag yung rocket, tinatawag na sounding rocket.

**[04:25:05.120] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004699)  
So pwede po bang mapaliwanag, ano yung sabihin ng sounding rocket?

**[04:25:16.440] Rep. John Geesnell "Baba" Yap** (asr-004700)  
It will be a test launch, Mr. Speaker, but a sounding rocket can be used to

**[04:25:21.860] Rep. John Geesnell "Baba" Yap** (asr-004701)  
study weather and atmosphere conditions, radiation, space physics, communications, and new technologies.

**[04:25:27.840] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004702)  
Okay, so ang intindi ko po, yung sounding rocket, parang siyang mas maliit na rocket, mainly for research purposes, and sounding rockets are suborbital.

**[04:25:38.200] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004703)  
Actually, hindi po pa aabot sa outer space technically, but its main use will be for research, scientific purposes.

**[04:25:48.620] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004704)  
Okay, so nasa ganung level po.

**[04:25:51.860] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004705)  
So our concern po natin ay, okay, meron bang aspeto ng technology transfer at sinasabi, pag-develop ng industriya,

**[04:26:05.980] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004706)  
ang ayaw po natin mangyari, ito na naman, tag-assemble lang tayo, pero in the end, pag umalis yung kumpanya, in this case, Perigee, wala na naman tayong sariling industriya.

**[04:26:17.280] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004707)  
Ano po ba ang perspektiba ng VILSA?

**[04:26:21.860] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004708)  
As far as, at least as far as the Acting Director General is concerned, I had the opportunity to speak with her before this interpellation, Mr. Speaker, si Acting Director General Gay Jane P. Perez, physics professor po yan sa UP, at genuine rocket scientist.

**[04:26:46.860] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004709)  
So, ano po ba ang perspective ng VILSA?

**[04:26:51.860] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004710)  
Dito sa space industry, kumbaga.

**[04:26:56.980] Rep. John Geesnell "Baba" Yap** (asr-004711)  
Mr. Speaker, meron tayong technology transfer. In fact, meron na mga trainings ongoing ngayon.

**[04:27:03.380] Rep. John Geesnell "Baba" Yap** (asr-004712)  
But the test launch shall be the next step in building capabilities that directly affect the country's economy, resilience, and long-term national interest.

**[04:27:12.080] Rep. John Geesnell "Baba" Yap** (asr-004713)  
Yung objective is not simply to launch a rocket, it is to build the people, technology, regulatory framework, safety procedure, and infrastructure

**[04:27:20.700] Rep. John Geesnell "Baba" Yap** (asr-004714)  
that will allow the people to be able to fly.

**[04:27:21.700] Rep. John Geesnell "Baba" Yap** (asr-004715)  
that will allow the people to be able to fly.

**[04:27:21.820] Rep. John Geesnell "Baba" Yap** (asr-004716)  
that will allow the people to be able to fly.

**[04:27:21.860] Rep. John Geesnell "Baba" Yap** (asr-004717)  
That will allow the Philippines to develop its own launch capability.

**[04:27:25.480] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004718)  
Okay, allow the Philippines to develop its own launch capability.

**[04:27:30.580] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004719)  
We will pursue that a little bit, in a little bit.

**[04:27:33.900] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004720)  
Nabanggit nyo yung maliit na bahagi lang yung para sa launch.

**[04:27:38.580] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004721)  
Magkano po yung gagastusin for the launch?

**[04:27:46.300] Rep. John Geesnell "Baba" Yap** (asr-004722)  
This year, Mr. Speaker, it will be a hundred million.

**[04:27:49.140] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004723)  
Hundred million of the 739 million.

**[04:27:51.860] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004724)  
Okay.

**[04:27:53.260] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004725)  
For this year. How about for next year?

**[04:27:56.380] Rep. John Geesnell "Baba" Yap** (asr-004726)  
For next year, it will just be six million. Over six million.

**[04:27:59.720] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004727)  
Okay. So, one hundred million this year, and then six million next year.

**[04:28:02.860] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004728)  
So, the rest of the budget, nabanggit nyo po, will be for developing and launching another satellite.

**[04:28:09.280] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004729)  
Would you tell us more about this satellite? Mula?

**[04:28:15.940] Rep. John Geesnell "Baba" Yap** (asr-004730)  
Yes, it will be used in developing the Mula 2 satellite, Mr. Chair.

**[04:28:20.860] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004731)  
Para saan po yung Mula?

**[04:28:21.860] Rep. John Geesnell "Baba" Yap** (asr-004732)  
Yung Mula 2 satellite natin, isang multi-spectral unit for land assessment, satellite, earth observation satellite,

**[04:28:32.560] Rep. John Geesnell "Baba" Yap** (asr-004733)  
being developed by FILSA to generate satellite data to benefit applications in agriculture, environmental protection and monitoring,

**[04:28:40.840] Rep. John Geesnell "Baba" Yap** (asr-004734)  
natural resource management, disaster risk reduction, and national security, among others.

**[04:28:46.200] Rep. John Geesnell "Baba" Yap** (asr-004735)  
Mula shall have a 5M multi-spectral imager, allowing the satellite...

**[04:28:51.860] Rep. John Geesnell "Baba" Yap** (asr-004736)  
to capture up to 139,000 square kilometers of satellite images per day.

**[04:28:59.520] Rep. John Geesnell "Baba" Yap** (asr-004737)  
The development of Mula was started by DOST in 2021, and was continued in 2023 by FILSA.

**[04:29:06.940] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004738)  
Okay. Thank you for that. So, mainly, imaging satellite, ganun po ba?

**[04:29:10.900] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004739)  
At gagamitin para sa, among others, agrikultura?

**[04:29:14.720] Rep. John Geesnell "Baba" Yap** (asr-004740)  
Yes, Mr. Speaker.

**[04:29:15.540] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004741)  
Ganun.

**[04:29:16.300] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004742)  
However, you did also mention national security.

**[04:29:19.720] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004743)  
So, baka pwede na nating isegway.

**[04:29:21.500] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004744)  
Bakit?

**[04:29:21.860] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004745)  
Ano po may national security?

**[04:29:25.480] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004746)  
Could you just explain? Ano yung role sa national security?

**[04:29:31.360] Rep. John Geesnell "Baba" Yap** (asr-004747)  
It is included in the Philippine Space Act, Mr. Chair.

**[04:29:36.020] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004748)  
Okay. And as I understand, in the Philippine Space Council, ay co-chair po. Tama po ba?

**[04:29:43.520] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004749)  
Is it DOST and BND Secretary?

**[04:29:48.880] Rep. John Geesnell "Baba" Yap** (asr-004750)  
Co-Vice Chair ang DOST, Mr. Speaker.

**[04:29:50.940] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004751)  
Ah, co-Vice Chair.

**[04:29:51.860] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004752)  
Ang chair po ay ang...

**[04:29:56.330] Rep. John Geesnell "Baba" Yap** (asr-004752-split-2)  
Our president is the

**[04:29:57.670] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004752-split-3)  
Pangulo mismo.

**[04:29:58.380] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004753)  
Okay.

**[04:29:59.180] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004754)  
So,

**[04:30:00.000] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004755)  
well, historically po, ang space sector, aerospace industry, including outer space,

**[04:30:11.600] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004756)  
historically, magpipit na magkaugnay naman talaga yung militar at sibilyan.

**[04:30:16.500] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004757)  
Diba?

**[04:30:17.600] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004758)  
At so, I suppose, kaya ganun.

**[04:30:22.500] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004759)  
Pero ngayon, napapanahon, at in fact, sa gera,

**[04:30:28.080] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004760)  
ang tinuturing na stratehikong lugar, ang kalawakan.

**[04:30:35.940] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004761)  
So, control of outer space means control of, I mean, you know,

**[04:30:43.420] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004762)  
it's a requisite for control over everything below it.

**[04:30:47.820] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004763)  
Parang ganun.

**[04:30:49.100] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004764)  
So, ngayon, ang tanong, dahil may national security aspect dito,

**[04:30:55.140] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004765)  
and this involves manufacturing,

**[04:30:58.080] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004766)  
and development of certain advanced technologies.

**[04:31:02.900] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004767)  
At ang pangunahing inisyatiba ng Malacanang,

**[04:31:07.740] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004768)  
ng Marcos Administration, kaugnay dyan ngayon,

**[04:31:10.300] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004769)  
ay yung Pax Silica.

**[04:31:12.760] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004770)  
So, may involvement ba ang FILSA at ang programa nila,

**[04:31:18.700] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004771)  
ang mga programa nila sa Pax Silica?

**[04:31:23.300] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004772)  
Dahil yung Pax Silica,

**[04:31:25.820] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004773)  
ang pangunahing concern ng USG,

**[04:31:28.080] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004774)  
dyan is the Defense Industrial Base of the United States.

**[04:31:31.660] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004775)  
And, inevitably, kasama doon, yung rockets, yung satellites,

**[04:31:36.340] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004776)  
and missiles.

**[04:31:39.760] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004777)  
Iyan ang ginagawa ng FILSA.

**[04:31:41.100] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004778)  
So, involved po ba ang FILSA sa Pax Silica?

**[04:31:44.140] Rep. John Geesnell "Baba" Yap** (asr-004779)  
Mr. Speaker, FILSA is not involved or connected with Pax Silica.

**[04:31:48.920] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004780)  
Not involved.

**[04:31:51.300] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004781)  
Will it ever be involved?

**[04:31:53.800] Rep. John Geesnell "Baba" Yap** (asr-004782)  
No.

**[04:31:58.080] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004783)  
Or, will it never be involved?

**[04:32:02.340] Rep. John Geesnell "Baba" Yap** (asr-004784)  
The agency has no knowledge about it, Mr. Chair.

**[04:32:05.740] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004785)  
No knowledge about it.

**[04:32:07.560] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004786)  
However, may lupa kayo sa New Clark City.

**[04:32:12.060] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004787)  
Diba?

**[04:32:13.340] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004788)  
And, in fact, yung lupa nyo ay gagamitin para sa Pax Silica.

**[04:32:17.460] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004789)  
Tama po ba?

**[04:32:20.120] Rep. John Geesnell "Baba" Yap** (asr-004790)  
Yes, Mr. Chair.

**[04:32:21.300] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004791)  
O, tama.

**[04:32:21.800] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004792)  
So, may involvement.

**[04:32:24.060] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004793)  
Kahit sa ganong level, ano po mangyayari sa inyong lupa?

**[04:32:28.080] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004794)  
And, yet, you say you're not involved.

**[04:32:31.480] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004795)  
Paano yun?

**[04:32:32.280] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004796)  
Please clarify.

**[04:32:33.700] Rep. John Geesnell "Baba" Yap** (asr-004797)  
The agency will relocate, Mr. Chair.

**[04:32:36.440] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004798)  
The agency will relocate.

**[04:32:38.260] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004799)  
So, gagamitin ang lupa nyo para sa Pax Silica sa New Clark City,

**[04:32:44.080] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004800)  
pero magre-relocate kayo.

**[04:32:48.140] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004801)  
Okay.

**[04:32:49.660] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004802)  
Tama po?

**[04:32:51.040] Rep. John Geesnell "Baba" Yap** (asr-004803)  
Yes, Mr. Chair.

**[04:32:51.780] Rep. John Geesnell "Baba" Yap** (asr-004804)  
BCDA will relocate the land, Mr. Chair.

**[04:32:58.080] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004805)  
So, okay.

**[04:33:00.280] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004806)  
Now, you also mentioned CESA earlier as involved in the rocket launch.

**[04:33:07.000] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004807)  
At sa kagayan magaganap yung launch.

**[04:33:09.460] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004808)  
In fact, saan po ang target ng first test launch ng sounding rocket

**[04:33:16.100] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004809)  
in the first quarter of 2027?

**[04:33:20.040] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004810)  
Yung pinagmalaki ng Pangulo, saan mangyayari yung launch test?

**[04:33:25.520] Rep. John Geesnell "Baba" Yap** (asr-004811)  
It will be in Cagayan, Mr. Chair.

**[04:33:27.320] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004812)  
Where?

**[04:33:27.760] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004813)  
In Cagayan?

**[04:33:28.080] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004814)  
Cagayan, specifically, Mr. Speaker.

**[04:33:32.120] Rep. John Geesnell "Baba" Yap** (asr-004815)  
In Lalo Airport.

**[04:33:33.560] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004816)  
Lalo Airport.

**[04:33:35.120] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004817)  
Okay.

**[04:33:35.600] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004818)  
Lalo Airport, which also happens to be an EDCA site.

**[04:33:41.240] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004819)  
Tama po ba?

**[04:33:42.940] Rep. John Geesnell "Baba" Yap** (asr-004820)  
Yes, Mr. Chair.

**[04:33:43.980] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004821)  
Yes, it's an EDCA site.

**[04:33:45.220] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004822)  
Of all places in the Philippines, bakit po sa EDCA site gaganapin yung test launch?

**[04:33:52.800] Rep. John Geesnell "Baba" Yap** (asr-004823)  
Mr. Chair, because of the location, aside from the geographic advantages of being in

**[04:33:57.760] Rep. John Geesnell "Baba" Yap** (asr-004824)  
the country's eastern seaboard, Cagayan has existing infrastructure in place to execute the test launch of a sounding rocket.

**[04:34:06.820] Rep. John Geesnell "Baba" Yap** (asr-004825)  
Furthermore, the area has an institutional and economic zone framework administered by the Cagayan Economic Zone Authority.

**[04:34:15.140] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004826)  
Okay.

**[04:34:17.720] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004827)  
So, dahil EDCA site po yan, obviously, nandyan po ang presensya ng U.S. military.

**[04:34:25.480] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004828)  
It is actively being used.

**[04:34:27.760] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004829)  
By the U.S. military on a rotational basis.

**[04:34:31.040] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004830)  
Ang alam po natin, yung mga helicopters ng U.S., yan ang ginagamit ng lunsaran.

**[04:34:38.800] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004831)  
At nagpapatrolya sila hanggang sa Batanes, sa Nueva Ecija, and parts of the South China Sea, using Lalo as their operating base.

**[04:34:52.800] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004832)  
So, magkakaroon kayo ng launch doon.

**[04:34:55.820] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004833)  
May involvement po ba?

**[04:34:57.020] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004834)  
Ang U.S. military sa proyektong ito?

**[04:35:01.540] Rep. John Geesnell "Baba" Yap** (asr-004835)  
Walang involvement, Mr. Chair.

**[04:35:03.360] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004836)  
Wala pong anumang involvement, yun po ang sinasabi natin.

**[04:35:06.940] Rep. John Geesnell "Baba" Yap** (asr-004837)  
Yes.

**[04:35:07.400] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004838)  
Sa ngayon, wala pong involvement?

**[04:35:09.560] Rep. John Geesnell "Baba" Yap** (asr-004839)  
Sabi po sa agency.

**[04:35:11.100] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004840)  
Okay.

**[04:35:13.080] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004841)  
Maraming salamat po.

**[04:35:14.280] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004842)  
At least, nagkaroon tayo ng pagkakataon to publicly discuss ano ba talaga yung plano ng Philippine Space Agency.

**[04:35:24.400] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004843)  
Ano ba itong binanggit ng Pangulo sa SONA?

**[04:35:27.020] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004844)  
At nakikita po natin na, if not now, ay kasama rin po sa proyekto yung mga issue ng national security.

**[04:35:36.940] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004845)  
At nakikita na rin po natin, kahit pa pano, ay nadidikit sa usapin ng Paxilica at saka ng U.S. military presence ang proyektong ito.

**[04:35:51.720] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004846)  
Kaya babantayan po natin ang further developments dito.

**[04:35:55.380] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004847)  
At the same time, of course,

**[04:35:57.020] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004848)  
mahalaga ng mag-develop ng sariling industriya ang mga Pilipino.

**[04:36:02.860] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004849)  
Kailangan tiyakin natin na ito ay maglilingkod sa interest ng mga Pilipino.

**[04:36:07.700] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004850)  
Maraming salamat po.

**[04:36:09.300] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004851)  
And I look forward to further discussions with Philza on this matter.

**[04:36:14.420] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004852)  
Maraming salamat, Mr. Speaker.

**[04:36:15.980] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004853)  
Thank you to the sponsor.

**[04:36:17.420] Deputy Speaker Yevgeny Vincente B. Emano** (asr-004854)  
Thank you, Honorable Tinio, Majority Leader.

**[04:36:20.820] Rep. Eduardo R. Rama Jr.** (asr-004855)  
Mr. Speaker, next to interpolate the sponsor, I move to recognize the representative,

**[04:36:27.020] Rep. Eduardo R. Rama Jr.** (asr-004856)  
Jernie Jett Nisay of Pusong Pinoy Party-list.

**[04:36:32.480] Deputy Speaker Yevgeny Vincente B. Emano** (asr-004857)  
Gentleman from Pusong Pinoy Party-list, Representative Jernie Jett Nisay.

**[04:36:38.200] Deputy Speaker Yevgeny Vincente B. Emano** (asr-004858)  
Sir, you are recognized to interpolate the sponsor.

**[04:36:41.920] Deputy Speaker Yevgeny Vincente B. Emano** (asr-004859)  
You have the floor.

**[04:36:43.020] Rep. Jernie Jett V. Nisay** (asr-004860)  
Sir Speaker, Sir Sponsor, ilang mga katanungan lamang po.

**[04:36:47.860] Rep. Jernie Jett V. Nisay** (asr-004861)  
Asunod ng mga katanungan ng ating congressman Tino.

**[04:36:51.100] Rep. Jernie Jett V. Nisay** (asr-004862)  
Regarding ho sa rocket launch sa Pilipinas,

**[04:36:53.780] Rep. Jernie Jett V. Nisay** (asr-004863)  
ano naman po ang nakikita ng Pilza?

**[04:36:56.100] Rep. Jernie Jett V. Nisay** (asr-004864)  
Na pangunahing benepisyo at potential risk ng pagkakaroon ng rocket launch capability sa ating bansa.

**[04:37:03.340] Rep. Jernie Jett V. Nisay** (asr-004865)  
Lalo't sa isang banda ay maaari itong mag-develop ng Pilipino expertise, research, aerospace jobs,

**[04:37:10.340] Rep. Jernie Jett V. Nisay** (asr-004866)  
at future commercial space activities.

**[04:37:13.700] Rep. Jernie Jett V. Nisay** (asr-004867)  
Pero sa kabilang banda, ano naman po ang mga concerns, safety,

**[04:37:17.800] Rep. Jernie Jett V. Nisay** (asr-004868)  
concerns sa safety, environmental impact, air and maritime traffic,

**[04:37:21.980] Rep. Jernie Jett V. Nisay** (asr-004869)  
at possible rocket debris na kailangang pagandaan?

**[04:37:26.100] Rep. John Geesnell "Baba" Yap** (asr-004870)  
Mr. Speaker, the test launch is another step towards building capabilities

**[04:37:30.180] Rep. John Geesnell "Baba" Yap** (asr-004871)  
that directly affect the country's economy, resilience, and long-term national interest

**[04:37:35.180] Rep. John Geesnell "Baba" Yap** (asr-004872)  
as a space-faring and space-capable nation.

**[04:37:38.920] Rep. John Geesnell "Baba" Yap** (asr-004873)  
The objective is not only to simply just launch a rocket,

**[04:37:42.080] Rep. John Geesnell "Baba" Yap** (asr-004874)  
it is to build the people, technology, regulatory framework, safety procedures, and infrastructure

**[04:37:48.140] Rep. John Geesnell "Baba" Yap** (asr-004875)  
that will allow the Philippines to develop its own space launch capability.

**[04:37:56.100] Rep. Jernie Jett V. Nisay** (asr-004876)  
Mr. Sponsor, additional question lamang po.

**[04:37:59.540] Rep. Jernie Jett V. Nisay** (asr-004877)

**[04:49:27.180] Deputy Speaker Yevgeny Vincente B. Emano** (asr-005023)

**[04:49:53.920] Rep. Eduardo R. Rama Jr.** (asr-005027)

**[04:49:57.240] Rep. Eduardo R. Rama Jr.** (asr-005028)

**[04:49:59.100] Rep. Eduardo R. Rama Jr.** (asr-005029)

**[04:50:00.680] Deputy Speaker Yevgeny Vincente B. Emano** (asr-005030)

**[04:50:07.380] Deputy Speaker Yevgeny Vincente B. Emano** (asr-005031)

**[04:50:12.520] Deputy Speaker Yevgeny Vincente B. Emano** (asr-005032)  
Majority Leader.

**[04:50:14.560] Deputy Speaker Yevgeny Vincente B. Emano** (asr-005033)  
I'm sorry.

**[04:50:16.280] Rep. John Geesnell "Baba" Yap** (asr-005034)  
Mr. Speaker, distinguished colleagues, CCC family, led by Secretary Robert Borje,

**[04:50:22.100] Rep. John Geesnell "Baba" Yap** (asr-005035)  
Mayong hapon sa tanan.

**[04:50:23.920] Rep. John Geesnell "Baba" Yap** (asr-005036)  
It is my privilege to sponsor the proposed Fiscal Year 2027 budget of the Climate Change Commission, or CCC.

**[04:50:33.040] Rep. John Geesnell "Baba" Yap** (asr-005037)  
The government's lead policy-making body tasked to coordinate, monitor, and evaluate our programs and action plans on climate change

**[04:50:42.000] Rep. John Geesnell "Baba" Yap** (asr-005038)  
and ensure the mainstreaming of climate change into national, sectoral, and local development plans and programs and investment programming.

**[04:50:51.040] Rep. John Geesnell "Baba" Yap** (asr-005039)  
Climate change is no longer a distant threat.

**[04:50:53.920] Rep. John Geesnell "Baba" Yap** (asr-005040)  
Intensifying tropical cyclones, sea-level rise, heavy rainfall events, and increasing temperatures already affect the lives and livelihoods of millions of Filipinos.

**[04:51:05.920] Rep. John Geesnell "Baba" Yap** (asr-005041)  
From damage to agriculture and infrastructure to escalating economic costs, the burden of climate change continues to grow, falling most heavily on vulnerable communities and sectors.

**[04:51:20.080] Rep. John Geesnell "Baba" Yap** (asr-005042)  
This reality underscores the need for sustained and sustainable development.

**[04:51:23.900] Rep. John Geesnell "Baba" Yap** (asr-005043)  
The Commission's role is not only to develop climate policies, but to ensure that these are translated into risk-informed planning, investments, and programs that respond to the needs of communities and sectors.

**[04:51:45.320] Rep. John Geesnell "Baba" Yap** (asr-005044)  
The CCC also plays an important role in advancing the country's climate finance agenda and in strengthening the use of

**[04:51:53.900] Rep. John Geesnell "Baba" Yap** (asr-005045)  
climate risk information to support evidence-based decision-making.

**[04:51:57.740] Rep. John Geesnell "Baba" Yap** (asr-005046)  
For fiscal year 2027, the proposed budget of the Commission amounts to Php 183,482,000.

**[04:52:06.820] Rep. John Geesnell "Baba" Yap** (asr-005047)  
It is Php 132,801,000, or 41.99% lower compared to the fiscal year 2026 budget.

**[04:52:17.620] Rep. John Geesnell "Baba" Yap** (asr-005048)  
Mr. Speaker, this budget is not merely an operational expense.

**[04:52:22.720] Rep. John Geesnell "Baba" Yap** (asr-005049)  
It is an investment in building more resilient

**[04:52:23.900] Rep. John Geesnell "Baba" Yap** (asr-005050)  
communities, advancing climate-responsive planning, safeguarding infrastructure and livelihoods, and ultimately securing the lives, well-being and sustainable future of every Filipino. I therefore respectfully seek the support of this august chamber for the approval of the fiscal year 2027 budget of the Climate Change Commission. This representation is now ready for interpellation. Thank you.

**[04:52:50.660] Deputy Speaker Yevgeny Vincente B. Emano** (asr-005050-split-2)  
Thank you, Majority Leader.

**[04:52:53.900] Rep. Eduardo R. Rama Jr.** (asr-005051)
