Ilang katanungan lamang po mula sa kababaihan kabalikatan para sa kapakanan.

**[04:53:23.900] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-005055)  
At kaularan ng bayan, nabanggit niyo po sa inyong opening statement, Mr. Speaker, Mr. Sponsor,

**[04:53:32.160] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-005056)  
na the Climate Change Commission was created to lead, coordinate, monitor, and evaluate climate change programs across government.

**[04:53:41.960] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-005057)  
This is a very powerful mandate, but the question is whether it is being exercised with enough authority.

**[04:53:49.080] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-005058)  
For fiscal year 2027, which agencies, programs,

**[04:53:53.900] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-005059)  
and climate tag expenditures will be the subject to actual review and monitoring by the Commission.

**[04:54:44.260] Rep. John Geesnell "Baba" Yap** (asr-005061)  
Mr. Speaker, as of this moment, we have 202 NGAs, 98 GOCCs, and 119 SUCs.

**[04:54:52.420] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-005062)  
Thank you, Mr. Speaker.

**[04:54:53.900] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-005066)  
Thank you, Mr. Speaker, Mr. Sponsor.

**[04:54:56.240] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-005067)  
For instance, if an agency labels a program climate tagged, but it cannot show a clear climate rational or measurable target or credible risk basis,

**[04:55:11.360] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-005068)  
ano po ang ginagawa ng komisyon?

**[04:55:14.080] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-005069)  
Meron po bang otoridad ng komisyon na i-question or mag-recommend ng revision or i-flag yung mga items na iyon sa Department?

**[04:55:23.880] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-005070)  
Department of Budget and Management or Kongreso po.

**[04:55:41.260] Rep. John Geesnell "Baba" Yap** (asr-005071)  
Mr. Speaker, yan ay part ng Quality Assurance ng Climate Change Commission,

**[04:55:47.540] Rep. John Geesnell "Baba" Yap** (asr-005072)  
and meron silang mga reviews, and then if makapasa, ay okay na sila, Mr. Speaker.

**[04:55:52.660] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-005073)  
Um, ilan po yung na-review exactly? May datos po ba tayo kung ilang mga climate tag programs ang na-review na ng komisyon na-reject or na-correct in recent budget cycle?

**[04:56:22.660] Rep. John Geesnell "Baba" Yap** (asr-005074)  
Mr. Speaker, as of the moment, total na NGAs ay 202, ang completed ng process ay 148, with a compliance rate of 73.27%.

**[04:56:44.980] Rep. John Geesnell "Baba" Yap** (asr-005075)  
For GOCC, 98 ang total completed CCET process, 57, with a compliance rate of 58.27%.

**[04:56:52.660] Rep. John Geesnell "Baba" Yap** (asr-005076)  
With the SUCs, 119. Completed CCET process, 102. Compliance rate of 85.71%, with a total of compliance rate ng 73.27%, Mr. Speaker.

**[04:57:08.180] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-005077)  
Thank you, Mr. Speaker, Mr. Sponsor.

**[04:57:10.940] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-005078)  
Um, alam naman po natin, isa sa mga problema, actually po pala dalawa, um, dalawa sa pinakamalaking problema natin dito.

**[04:57:19.280] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-005079)  
Um, isa na dun po yung institutional order.

**[04:57:22.660] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-005080)  
Overlap. Ano po?

**[04:57:24.680] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-005081)  
Um, paano po kino-coordinate ng komisyon at na-avoid yung institutional overlap ng functions, especially with like DENR, um, with the NDRRM, with the DOF and other agencies na involved din sa climate policy, no?

**[04:57:45.760] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-005082)  
Um, disaster risk reduction.

**[04:57:47.920] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-005083)  
So, ano po ba talaga, anong role ng komisyon?

**[04:57:52.660] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-005084)  
Sa coordination, what evidence shows that coordination is producing unified action rather than yung fragmentation pong na meron tayo?

**[04:58:02.940] Rep. John Geesnell "Baba" Yap** (asr-005085)  
Mr. Speaker, meron namang interagency meetings with the different agencies together with the Commission, Climate Change Commission.

**[04:58:10.000] Rep. John Geesnell "Baba" Yap** (asr-005086)  
So, walang overlapping of duties and responsibilities, Mr. Speaker.

**[04:58:14.000] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-005087)  
Okay, thank you, Mr. Speaker, Mr. Sponsor.

**[04:58:17.000] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-005088)  
Um, pagdating po sa climate finance, this remains as actually...

**[04:58:22.660] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-005089)  
...one of the problems, no?

**[04:58:24.880] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-005090)  
Um, sinasabi po nila, um, the absence of a climate finance unit is very challenging, kaya nagkakaroon po tayo ng fragmented, minsan underutilized and difficult to access, particularly for LGUs.

**[04:58:40.660] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-005091)  
Um, wala po kasi tayong centralized institutional mechanism for climate finance, kaya nalilimit yung abilidad ng ating bansa to effectively mobilize...

**[04:58:52.660] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-005092)  
...coordinate and deploy both domestic and international resources.

**[04:58:58.480] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-005093)  
Um, while the CCC is mandated to coordinate climate policy, it lacks a dedicated operational arm focused exclusively on climate finance.

**[04:59:10.000] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-005094)  
Ano po ang posisyon ng CCC on the creation of a climate finance unit under the CCC?

**[04:59:16.760] Rep. John Geesnell "Baba" Yap** (asr-005095)  
Mr. Speaker, meron na silang development partners coordinating group para ma-discipline.

**[04:59:22.660] Rep. John Geesnell "Baba" Yap** (asr-005096)  
Pag-discuss nila doon kung ano yung mga kailangan gawin para sa Climate Change Commission para, once again, ma-disseminate yung mga information at anong mga kailangan gawin, especially with the LGUs.

**[04:59:34.040] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-005097)  
Um, yung pinupunto ko po dito, Mr. Speaker, um, Mr. Sponsor, kasi yung iba po, diba, parang dire-diretsyo, iba-diretsyo na sa LGU.

**[04:59:43.880] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-005098)  
May fragmentation talaga, halimbawa may financing, diretsyo sa LGU, yung iba-diretsyo dito.

**[04:59:50.520] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-005099)  
Wala talaga siyang centralized...

**[04:59:52.660] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-005100)  
...centralized agency who monitors the financing that comes in para magkaroon ng isang comprehensive...

**[05:00:00.000] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-005101)  
or integrated na pagtingin doon sa mga programa o sa mga proyekto na dapat na paprioritize for financing.

**[05:00:09.180] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-005102)  
How does the Climate Change Commission deal with that problem?

**[05:00:15.460] Rep. John Geesnell "Baba" Yap** (asr-005103)  
As I mentioned earlier, Mr. Speaker, because of the interagency meeting, doon nila nabibigay yung mga gusto nila,

**[05:00:22.960] Rep. John Geesnell "Baba" Yap** (asr-005104)  
ano mga kailangan na kukuha nila para once again walang overlapping with the different agencies

**[05:00:27.860] Rep. John Geesnell "Baba" Yap** (asr-005105)  
and iba-iba naman yung mga mandates nila with the different agencies, Mr. Speaker.

**[05:00:34.000] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-005106)  
How about po, Mr. Speaker, Mr. Sponsor, yung pag-access ng mga international grants?

**[05:00:40.400] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-005107)  
Ano po ba ang mga challenges or mga balakid sometimes that we are not able to access grants from abroad?

**[05:00:49.420] Rep. John Geesnell "Baba" Yap** (asr-005108)  
That remains to be one of the biggest challenges of the Commission, Mr. Speaker.

**[05:00:54.020] Rep. John Geesnell "Baba" Yap** (asr-005109)  
That is why our Secretary-Holmes,

**[05:00:57.860] Rep. John Geesnell "Baba" Yap** (asr-005110)  
is also working hard with the different agencies to be able to access some of the finances, international finances, Mr. Speaker.

**[05:01:04.420] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-005111)  
Hindi po kaya yung fragmentation na ito is also a contributing factor and our difficulty to access international financing

**[05:01:12.600] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-005112)  
kasi po, di ba, hindi coordinated, hindi integrated.

**[05:01:16.720] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-005113)  
So medyo mas matagal, mas minsan, baka po, nagkakaroon ng pagkakataon na deadline na hindi natin na ibibigay ng dokumento,

**[05:01:25.900] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-005114)  
medyo madaming dokumentong kailangan.

**[05:01:27.860] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-005115)  
Hindi po ba ito isa sa mga dahilan din kung bakit nagkakaroon tayo ng problema doon?

**[05:01:36.220] Rep. John Geesnell "Baba" Yap** (asr-005116)  
Isa po yan sa mga dahilan, Mr. Speaker, pero the Commission is now working on the capacity building para sa NGAs

**[05:01:43.260] Rep. John Geesnell "Baba" Yap** (asr-005117)  
para ma-improvise, ma-coordinate ang mga different mga agencies, Mr. Speaker.

**[05:01:51.940] Rep. John Geesnell "Baba" Yap** (asr-005118)  
And under the Government Optimization Law,

**[05:01:57.860] Rep. John Geesnell "Baba" Yap** (asr-005119)  
yung Climate Finance Unit requested to be established, is requested to be established, Mr. Speaker.

**[05:02:05.980] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-005120)  
That was my question a while ago, the climate, I asked about your position on creating a Climate Finance Unit.

**[05:02:14.680] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-005121)  
So, in fact, I think the answer was you have, there's already a coordinating committee.

**[05:02:23.220] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-005122)  
Ito pong sinasabing nyo ngayon, ano po yung Climate Finance Unit?

**[05:02:26.820] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-005123)  
So there is...

**[05:02:27.860] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-005124)  
An existing Climate Finance Unit in the Commission right now?

**[05:02:31.680] Rep. John Geesnell "Baba" Yap** (asr-005125)  
It is still a proposal, Mr. Speaker.

**[05:02:33.960] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-005126)  
Okay. Thank you.

**[05:02:35.820] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-005127)  
So the Commission is supporting the creation of that unit?

**[05:02:41.540] Rep. John Geesnell "Baba" Yap** (asr-005128)  
Yes, Mr. Speaker.

**[05:02:42.300] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-005129)  
I was leading to that question a while ago, Mr. Speaker, Mr. Sponsor.

**[05:02:50.000] Rep. John Geesnell "Baba" Yap** (asr-005130)  
Yes, Mr. Speaker. In fact, the Commission is also requesting your support, the support of everybody,

**[05:02:56.880] Rep. John Geesnell "Baba" Yap** (asr-005131)  
especially in the 20th Congress, Mr. Speaker.

**[05:02:59.820] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-005132)  
Mr. Speaker, Mr. Sponsor, I'd like to inform the Sponsor and the CCC that, in fact,

**[05:03:05.520] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-005133)  
this representation filed House Bill 8918, creating the Climate Finance Unit in the CCC.

**[05:03:14.300] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-005134)  
Okay. Next question ko naman po, Mr. Speaker, Mr. Sponsor.

**[05:03:18.960] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-005135)  
Nasabi nyo rin po kanina, every year, communities are hit by stronger typhoons, deeper floods, longer dry spells, extreme heat,

**[05:03:26.360] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-005136)  
coastal erosion, crop losses, water insecurity, and displacement.

**[05:03:31.800] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-005137)  
Against this backdrop po, how can the Commission justify its 2027 proposal as adequate,

**[05:03:39.380] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-005138)  
considering na, sabi nyo rin po, bumaba po yung proposed budget for 2027?

**[05:03:45.900] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-005139)  
Ano po, dito sa funding gap na ito, ano po ang mga maaapektuhan na programa at proyekto ng CCC?

**[05:03:53.600] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-005140)  
Alin ang hindi mapopondohan?

**[05:03:55.780] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-005141)  
Alin ang makukompromiso?

**[05:03:57.380] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-005142)  
Alin ang mapaaapektuhan po?

**[05:04:08.120] Rep. John Geesnell "Baba" Yap** (asr-005143)  
Mr. Speaker, ang Commission naman ay nag-request na mas mataas na budget, but they also understand the budget.

**[05:04:18.860] Rep. John Geesnell "Baba" Yap** (asr-005144)  
Ang maapektuhan ng mga programs nila, a capacity building for LGU, mga data,

**[05:04:25.780] Rep. John Geesnell "Baba" Yap** (asr-005145)  
gathering, at sa iba pang mga programs, Mr. Speaker.

**[05:04:30.220] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-005146)  
Doon sa capacity building po, alam ko isang challenge dyan eh, kaya hindi makapag-access.

**[05:04:36.320] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-005147)  
Halimbawa nga yung mga LGU sa People's Survival Fund, kasi nahihirapan po sila doon sa documentation,

**[05:04:42.820] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-005148)  
sa sobrang dami po ng dokumentong hinihingi, ultimo po paggawa ng project proposal, hirap sila.

**[05:04:50.340] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-005149)  
So kasama po ba dyan sa capacity building yan?

**[05:04:52.920] Rep. John Geesnell "Baba" Yap** (asr-005150)  
Yes, kasama, Mr. Speaker.

**[05:04:55.780] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-005151)  
Magsalamat po.

**[05:04:57.720] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-005152)  
On climate disasters and losses naman po, meron po bang estimate ang Commission yung annual economic and social cost ng climate impact sa Pilipinas?

**[05:05:09.580] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-005153)  
Meron po tayong datos tungkol dyan?

**[05:05:16.980] Rep. John Geesnell "Baba" Yap** (asr-005154)  
The Commission has a data, Mr. Speaker, but please allow me to...

**[05:05:23.220] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-005155)  
But anyways, anyway, Mr. Speaker.

**[05:05:25.780] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-005156)  
Mr. Speaker, Mr. Sponsor, ang gusto ko lamang po ito nung tungkol dyan, in general, yung data pong yan,

**[05:05:33.680] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-005157)  
kung lagi po nating pinupondohan lamang yung ating recovery,

**[05:05:41.440] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-005158)  
hindi pa kaya dapat mas pagtuuna natin ng pansin yung prevention before disasters?

**[05:05:47.040] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-005159)  
Ganoon naman po ang ginagawa ng Commission, hindi po ba?

**[05:05:50.100] Rep. John Geesnell "Baba" Yap** (asr-005160)  
Thank you, Mr. Speaker.

**[05:05:54.660] Rep. John Geesnell "Baba" Yap** (asr-005161)  
Mr. Speaker, meron naman po and we agree.

**[05:05:55.780] Rep. John Geesnell "Baba" Yap** (asr-005162)  
We agree to that.

**[05:05:56.680] Rep. John Geesnell "Baba" Yap** (asr-005163)  
That's why we have the National Adaptation Plan at saka predictive governance of our Honorable President Bongbao Marcos.

**[05:06:09.560] Rep. John Geesnell "Baba" Yap** (asr-005164)  
Mr. Speaker.

**[05:06:11.160] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-005165)  
Thank you, Mr. Speaker, Mr. Sponsor.

**[05:06:13.520] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-005166)  
Pagdating naman po sa mga vulnerable sectors, paano nasisiguro po ng Commission na yung ating mga magsasaka, mangingisda, yung mga informal settler families,

**[05:06:24.020] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-005167)  
mga IPs?

**[05:06:25.780] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-005168)  
Children, senior citizen, PWDs at mga kababaihan ay hindi lamang mga tinatawag nating generic beneficiaries,

**[05:06:36.240] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-005169)  
kundi tinatrato sila na specific groups na may specific climate risk at dapat may specific budget need din na nailalaan.

**[05:06:47.520] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-005170)  
Meron po ba tayong nakalaan sa pagpupondo ng ganyan sa budget ng 2027?

**[05:06:54.440] Rep. John Geesnell "Baba" Yap** (asr-005171)  
Mr. Speaker.

**[05:06:55.780] Rep. John Geesnell "Baba" Yap** (asr-005172)  
Meron pong nakalaan dyan.

**[05:06:57.000] Rep. John Geesnell "Baba" Yap** (asr-005173)  
In fact, it is included sa NAP localization, saka sa NDC localization.

**[05:07:02.240] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-005174)  
Maraming salamat po.

**[05:07:03.800] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-005175)  
Pagdating naman po sa food and water security, paano nagko-coordinate ang Commission sa agriculture, fisheries, irrigation, water, and LGU para i-address yung mga problema sa tagtuyot, saltwater intrusion, crop failure, fishery decline, water shortages?

**[05:07:23.900] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-005176)  
If districts are already affected?

**[05:07:25.780] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-005177)  
If they are already affecting livelihood and prices, why should climate adaptation remain a peripheral item instead of a central budgetary priority?

**[05:07:38.160] Rep. John Geesnell "Baba" Yap** (asr-005178)  
Kasama din yung water natin para sa interagency meeting para ma-advance yung national adaptation plan at saka in coordination with the different LGUs.

**[05:07:48.660] Rep. John Geesnell "Baba" Yap** (asr-005179)  
Meron namang mga series of meetings together with the Commission and the LGUs.

**[05:07:52.740] Rep. John Geesnell "Baba" Yap** (asr-005180)  
Mr. Speaker.

**[05:07:54.900] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-005181)  
Maraming salamat.

**[05:07:55.780] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-005182)  
Maraming salamat po, Mr. Speaker, Mr. Sponsor.

**[05:07:58.620] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-005183)  
Panghuli na lamang po sigurong katanungan on loss and damage.

**[05:08:03.480] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-005184)  
What is the Commission doing to help the Philippine access international finance for climate-related losses without creating new debt burdens?

**[05:08:13.420] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-005185)  
Are we actively preparing project pipelines and evidence-based claims?

**[05:08:17.640] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-005186)  
Or are we simply waiting for international mechanisms to mature while communities continue to absorb the damage?

**[05:08:25.780] Rep. John Geesnell "Baba" Yap** (asr-005187)  
Yes, Mr. Speaker.

**[05:08:27.540] Rep. John Geesnell "Baba" Yap** (asr-005188)  
The Commission is currently having a negotiation and kasalipan yan sa development plan ng Commission.

**[05:08:33.500] Rep. John Geesnell "Baba" Yap** (asr-005189)  
Mr. Speaker.

**[05:08:34.740] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-005190)  
Thank you, Mr. Speaker, Mr. Sponsor.

**[05:08:37.560] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-005191)  
In closing, the issue before us is not whether climate change is real nor whether the Climate Change Commission has an important mandate.

**[05:08:46.220] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-005192)  
Those questions have already been settled by law, by science, and by the lived experiences of our people.

**[05:08:52.520] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-005193)  
The issue before Congress is whether the FY2020...

**[05:08:55.780] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-005194)  
...FY2027 budget gives the Commission the resources, discipline, and accountability mechanisms needed to turn climate policy into protection on the ground.

**[05:09:06.460] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-005195)  
The KBKP supports the provision of adequate funding for the Commission, but at the same time challenges it to demonstrate that their budget will not merely sustain an office,

**[05:09:22.660] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-005196)  
but strengthen the country's capacity to survive.

**[05:09:25.800] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-005198)  
adapt and protect the most vulnerable communities from accelerating climate crisis.

**[05:09:32.820] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-005199)  
Maraming salamat po, Mr. Speaker, Mr. Sponsor, and to the Climate Change Commission.

**[05:09:37.780] Deputy Speaker Yevgeny Vincente B. Emano** (asr-005200)  
Thank you, Honorable Montez, Majority Leader.

**[05:09:41.640] Rep. Eduardo R. Rama Jr.** (asr-005201)  
Mr. Speaker, next to interpolate the sponsor, I move to recognize Representative Renee Louise Co of Kabataan Party List.

**[05:09:51.500] Deputy Speaker Yevgeny Vincente B. Emano** (asr-005202)  
The Honorable Renee Louise Co of Kabataan Party List.

**[05:09:55.340] Deputy Speaker Yevgeny Vincente B. Emano** (asr-005203)  
is recognized to interpolate

**[05:09:57.580] Deputy Speaker Yevgeny Vincente B. Emano** (asr-005204)  
the sponsor. Ma'am, you have the floor.

**[05:10:00.600] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-005205)  
Thank you, Mr.

**[05:10:01.740] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-005206)  
Speaker, and good afternoon

**[05:10:03.600] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-005207)  
to our distinguished sponsor and the

**[05:10:05.460] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-005208)  
Climate Change Commission.

**[05:10:08.080] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-005209)  
Mr. Speaker, good sponsor,

**[05:10:09.760] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-005210)  
we are aware of the existence

**[05:10:11.800] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-005211)  
of a Climate Change

**[05:10:13.320] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-005212)  
Task Force, a body

**[05:10:15.600] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-005213)  
presenting as official

**[05:10:17.160] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-005214)  
under Commissioner Albert De La Cruz.

Honorable Jernie Jett Nisay.

**[05:23:03.020] Rep. Jernie Jett V. Nisay** (asr-005404)  
Mr. Sponsor, isang katanungan na lamang po bago ho natin i-terminate.

**[05:23:08.320] Rep. Jernie Jett V. Nisay** (asr-005405)  
Mr. Speaker, Mr. Sponsor, ayon po sa latest pag-asa outlook,

**[05:23:11.980] Rep. Jernie Jett V. Nisay** (asr-005406)  
ang El Niño ay maaaring magpatuloy hanggang first half of 2027

**[05:23:16.100] Rep. Jernie Jett V. Nisay** (asr-005407)  
at maaaring umabot sa very strong state na maaaring magdulot ng mas matinding init at below normal rainfall

**[05:23:23.500] Rep. Jernie Jett V. Nisay** (asr-005408)  
sa ilang bahagi ng ating bansa.

**[05:23:26.760] Rep. Jernie Jett V. Nisay** (asr-005409)  
Pero nakita rin po natin nitong mga nakaraang buwan na marami pa rin pong magkaroon ng episodes

**[05:23:32.280] Rep. Jernie Jett V. Nisay** (asr-005410)  
ng matinding ulan at malalakas na bagyo.

**[05:23:35.060] Rep. Jernie Jett V. Nisay** (asr-005411)  
Sa ganitong pabago-bagong climate conditions, paano po minomonitor ng CCC

**[05:23:40.220] Rep. Jernie Jett V. Nisay** (asr-005412)  
kung ang mga LGU ay handa sa magkaibang climate risk

**[05:23:43.680] Rep. Jernie Jett V. Nisay** (asr-005413)  
mula sa water shortage at agricultural losses hanggang sa flooding and storm impacts.

**[05:23:53.500] Rep. John Geesnell "Baba" Yap** (asr-005414)  
What the Commission has is the LC-CAP of the different LGUs

**[05:23:59.300] Rep. John Geesnell "Baba" Yap** (asr-005415)  
and the CCC is monitoring kung na-implement na ba ang mga LC-CAPs nila, Mr. Speaker.

**[05:24:13.560] Rep. John Geesnell "Baba" Yap** (asr-005416)  
Mr. Speaker, LC-CAP means Local Climate Change Action Plan, Mr. Speaker.

**[05:24:21.340] Rep. Jernie Jett V. Nisay** (asr-005417)  
Mr. Speaker, Mr. Sponsor,

**[05:24:24.020] Rep. Jernie Jett V. Nisay** (asr-005419)  
nais ko na lang pong ipahayag ang aking suporta sa Climate Change Commission

**[05:24:27.880] Rep. Jernie Jett V. Nisay** (asr-005420)  
at sa mahalagang gampanin nila sa paghanda ng ating bansa sa mga epekto ng climate change.

**[05:24:34.020] Rep. Jernie Jett V. Nisay** (asr-005421)  
Sa uli, ang climate action ay hindi lamang tungkol sa environmental policies.

**[05:24:39.300] Rep. Jernie Jett V. Nisay** (asr-005422)  
Direktang nakaka-apekto ito sa pagkain at mga presyo, kabuhayan, kalusugan, tahanan, infrastruktura at siguridad ng ating mga komunidad.

**[05:24:50.280] Rep. Jernie Jett V. Nisay** (asr-005423)  
Kaya mahalaga ang patuloy na tungkulin ng CCC,

**[05:24:52.840] Rep. Jernie Jett V. Nisay** (asr-005424)  
upang matulungan ng ating mga ahensya, LGUs at mga komunidad na maisalin ang climate policies at risk assessments

**[05:25:01.000] Rep. Jernie Jett V. Nisay** (asr-005425)  
sa konkretong paghanda at mga programang tumutugon sa mga pangailangan ng mamayan.

**[05:25:06.500] Rep. Jernie Jett V. Nisay** (asr-005426)  
Patuloy nating suportahan ang mga akbang na magpapalakas sa climate resilience ng ating mga komunidad,

**[05:25:11.920] Rep. Jernie Jett V. Nisay** (asr-005427)  
lalo na habang mas magiging hindi inaasahan ang epekto ng matinding init, tagtuyot, malakas na ulan at bagyo.

**[05:25:19.000] Rep. Jernie Jett V. Nisay** (asr-005428)  
Maraming salamat po, Mr. Speaker, Mr. Sponsor.

**[05:35:12.780] Deputy Speaker Yasser Alonto Balindong** (asr-005499)

**[05:35:18.660] Rep. Ed Christopher S. Go** (asr-005500)  
Mr. Speaker, it is my privilege to sponsor the proposed budget of the Commission.

**[05:35:22.800] Rep. Ed Christopher S. Go** (asr-005501)  
ng Wikang Filipino for fiscal year 2027. The Komisyon sa Wikang Filipino carries the important mandate of preserving and promoting the Filipino language, as well as our other indigenous languages across the archipelago, languages that embody the soul of our people. The Commission's approved budget allocation for fiscal year 2027 is around 120 million, but the Commission has identified an additional 27.5 million

**[05:35:49.780] Rep. Ed Christopher S. Go** (asr-005502)  
For priority programs which are as follows.

**[05:35:52.740] Rep. Ed Christopher S. Go** (asr-005503)  
Pagbuo ng otografiya para sa sampung matinding nanganganib na wika, pagtatayo ng limang bantayog wika, and implementation of the Filipino Sign Language Visual Communication Assessment for the Deaf.

**[05:36:06.620] Rep. Ed Christopher S. Go** (asr-005504)  
Mr. Speaker, the Commission ng Wikang Filipino wishes that its budget allocation will be augmented for the proper implementation of its priority programs.

**[05:36:15.660] Rep. Ed Christopher S. Go** (asr-005505)  
These programs are important in ensuring that our languages remain alive, accessible, and responsive to the needs of our people.

**[05:36:21.920] Rep. Ed Christopher S. Go** (asr-005506)  
We are hopeful that the Commission's proposed additional funding will be given great consideration.

**[05:36:27.800] Rep. Ed Christopher S. Go** (asr-005507)

**[05:36:31.160] Deputy Speaker Yasser Alonto Balindong** (asr-005508)  
Majority Leader.

Ang tatanong natin ay tungkol po sa FSL unit na ginawa sa Commission sa Wikang Filipino, na ang pagbuo nito ay may basihan sa enactment ng Republic Act No. 11106, or the Filipino Sign Language Act.

**[05:37:19.160] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-005517)  
Okay.

**[05:37:19.820] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-005518)  
By recognizing FSL as the...

**[05:37:21.920] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-005519)  
National and Official Sign Language of the country, it is a groundbreaking law even from an international perspective.

**[05:37:28.420] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-005520)  
It is also comprehensive in its scope of six language domains of education, justice, health, workplace, media, and all other public transactions, services, and facilities.

**[05:37:42.000] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-005521)  
It is likewise an unusual law, which accepts that the visual, spatial language used by Filipinos is true and legitimate.

**[05:37:51.920] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-005523)

ang appropriated and

**[05:41:44.800] Rep. Ed Christopher S. Go** (asr-005597)

**[05:41:46.220] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-005598)

**[05:41:50.700] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-005600)

**[05:42:01.080] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-005601)

**[05:42:10.480] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-005602)

**[05:42:20.700] Rep. Ed Christopher S. Go** (asr-005604)

**[05:42:25.020] Rep. Ed Christopher S. Go** (asr-005605)

**[05:42:29.160] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-005606)

**[05:42:43.380] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-005607)  
Wala rin po bang pondo dito?

**[05:43:21.800] Rep. Ed Christopher S. Go** (asr-005622)

**[05:43:24.160] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-005623)

**[05:43:29.900] Rep. Ed Christopher S. Go** (asr-005624)  
Yes po, Mr. Speaker.

**[05:43:30.920] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-005625)

**[05:43:47.700] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-005626)

**[05:44:00.920] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-005627)

**[05:44:18.540] Rep. Ed Christopher S. Go** (asr-005628)

**[05:44:27.760] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-005629)

This is for addressing the needs of deaf children and youth in the BARM, historically na bababa talaga yung pondo dito, pero dumadami din po yung poverty incidents, education crisis sa children in BARM.

**[05:45:00.000] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-005633)

**[05:45:17.820] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-005635)  
Katuwang ng Council for the Welfare of Children.

Mr. Speaker, good sponsor.

Ang pambansang wikang Filipino man yan, o ang marami nating wikang katutubo, maging ang Filipino Sign Language.

Mr. Speaker, next to interpellate, I move to recognize Representative Antonio Tinio of ACT Teachers Partylist.

Maraming salamat, Mr. Speaker.

**[05:47:53.360] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005655)  
Ilang mga katanungan po tungkol sa budget ng Commission sa Wikang Filipino.

**[05:48:00.560] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005656)  
Sa budget, sa General Appropriations Bill, nakalagay dito Commission on the Filipino Language.

**[05:48:08.880] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005657)  
115.714 million pesos ang nihinging budget. Napakaliit na budget.

**[05:48:18.680] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005658)  
Sa isang komisyon na napakahalaga ng mandato, yan ang pagpapaunlad, pagpapalago, pagpapalaganap sa gamit ng Pilipino bilang pambansang wika at mga wikang Pilipino.

**[05:48:38.800] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005659)  
Ang realidad po, isang multilingual na lipunan ng ating bansa.

**[05:48:45.800] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005660)  
Ngunit, tila hindi pa kinikilala

**[05:48:48.680] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005661)  
at umaangkop ang mga institusyon ng ating lipunan sa realidad na ito.

**[05:48:56.420] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005662)  
Mula sa mga paaralan, sa mass media, at unang-una na ang gobyerno. Multilingual tayo.

**[05:49:06.560] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005663)  
So, dapat umangkop ang gobyerno at ang budget para dito.

**[05:49:15.480] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005664)  
So, tingnan po.

**[05:49:18.680] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005665)  
Tingnan po natin yung budget, 115 million pesos, at isa ang programa, Filipino and Other Philippine Languages Development Program.

**[05:49:39.220] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005666)  
Pwede po bang paliwanag ng sponsor?

**[05:49:43.520] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005667)  
Ano-ano po ba ang mga programa at ano ang ginagawa?

**[05:49:48.680] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005668)  
Nang KWF para isulong ka ang kanyang mandato kaugnay sa wikang Pilipino at mga wika ng Pilipinas.

**[05:50:01.220] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005669)  
Ano-ano yung ginagawa nila under this program?

**[05:50:12.720] Rep. Ed Christopher S. Go** (asr-005670)  
Mr. Speaker, pagbuo po ng otografiya, pagbuo po ng mga glosaryo,

**[05:50:18.680] Rep. Ed Christopher S. Go** (asr-005671)  
diksyonaryo, pananaliksik, aral, at sa etno-linguistic communities.

**[05:50:28.560] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005672)  
Okay. Anong mga wika po ang sinusuportahan ng Komisyon sa Wikang Pilipino? Aling mga wika yan?

**[05:50:39.380] Rep. Ed Christopher S. Go** (asr-005673)  
Meron po tayong 135 po na wika.

**[05:50:42.920] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005674)  
Opo, 135. Pero, gustuhin man natin.

**[05:50:48.680] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005675)  
Magpakatotoo tayo, hindi natututukan ng KWF ang lahat ng wikang yan.

**[05:50:57.120] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005676)  
So, yung mga programa, lalo na para sa proposed budget, ang actual na budget ay 85.3 million pesos lamang.

**[05:51:09.820] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005677)  
Ano ang mga kongkretong gagawin yan sa 2027?

**[05:51:14.920] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005678)  
Thank you very much.

**[05:51:18.680] Rep. Ed Christopher S. Go** (asr-005679)  
Ang pinag-protectan po ay 10 wika.

**[05:51:20.600] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005680)

**[05:51:23.200] Rep. Ed Christopher S. Go** (asr-005681)  
Ang pinakamalubhang nanganganib.

**[05:51:25.220] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005682)  
Ano-ano po yun?

**[05:51:40.140] Rep. Ed Christopher S. Go** (asr-005683)  
Mr. Speaker, noted on this, we will let the agency submit to the Committee for Media Compliance.

**[05:51:46.180] Rep. Ed Christopher S. Go** (asr-005684)  
Isusumiti na lang po namin.

**[05:51:47.280] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005685)  
Ay, isusumiti po. Okay.

**[05:51:48.680] Rep. Ed Christopher S. Go** (asr-005686)  
Pero, ito po.

**[05:51:53.600] Rep. Ed Christopher S. Go** (asr-005687)  
Isa po ay buniking binukignon, bukidnon, mahagat, amatikanon, inata, inete, kabalyanon, kaluyanen, karulano, malaynon, at orohanon.

**[05:52:13.000] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005688)  
So, mukhang ang focus po na binabanggit nyo ngayon ay yung mga endangered.

**[05:52:18.680] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005689)  
Ang pinag-protecting na wika, ganun po ba?

**[05:52:21.240] Rep. Ed Christopher S. Go** (asr-005690)  
Tama po.

**[05:52:22.260] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005691)  
At karamihan ay nasa Mindanao.

**[05:52:25.380] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005692)  
Mga wika ng indigenous groups, tama po ba?

**[05:52:31.560] Rep. Ed Christopher S. Go** (asr-005693)  
Tama po.

**[05:52:32.220] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005694)  
Okay. Gusto lang po natin malaman, ano yung particular na budget at programa kung meron man ang Commission sa Wikang Filipino para sa Filipino Sign Language?

**[05:52:48.680] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005695)  
So, nabanggit po ito ng unang nag-interpelate, si representative ko, nais ko pong banggitin din, kaya po ako nagtatanong dahil isang, ako po ang principal author ng Republic Act 11106, Filipino Sign Language Act.

**[05:53:09.520] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005696)  
At minamandato po doon, ang lead agency dito ay ang Commission sa Wikang Filipino.

**[05:53:14.940] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005697)  
So, magkano ang ilalaan?

**[05:53:18.680] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005698)  
Ang pondo ng KWF para sa implementasyon ng Filipino Sign Language Act. Kung meron man.

**[05:53:37.140] Rep. Ed Christopher S. Go** (asr-005699)  
4.7 million po for 2027.

**[05:53:40.940] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005700)  
4.7 million pesos. At para saan po yan? Ano po ang specific na gamit dito?

**[05:53:48.680] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005701)  
Ang makalit na halaga na po. Para saan po yan?

**[05:53:53.300] Rep. Ed Christopher S. Go** (asr-005702)  
For the interpreting systems po, Mr. Speaker.

**[05:54:00.160] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005703)  
Interpreting. So, FSL, Filipino Sign Language Interpreting.

**[05:54:06.340] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005704)  
Okay.

**[05:54:06.620] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005705)  
Okay.

**[05:54:07.140] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005706)  
Thank you.

**[05:54:12.340] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005707)  
Sapat na po ba yan? Ang pondo na yan ay sapat na para ma-fulfill ang managin?

**[05:54:18.680] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005708)  
Andato sa KWF?

**[05:54:22.780] Rep. Ed Christopher S. Go** (asr-005709)  
Mr. Speaker, hindi po ito sapat. Ang tutuusin. Pero pinakakasa po ng Commission.

**[05:54:28.260] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005710)

**[05:54:33.620] Rep. Ed Christopher S. Go** (asr-005711)  
27 million po, Mr. Speaker.

**[05:54:35.880] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005712)  
27 million.

**[05:54:38.540] Rep. Ed Christopher S. Go** (asr-005713)  
Yes po, Mr. Speaker.

**[05:54:39.100] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005714)  
27 million ang kailangan. 4 million lang ang nakalaan.

**[05:54:46.920] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005716)  
Handa po ba

**[05:54:48.680] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005717)  
At nagsubmit po ba ang FSL ng Kaukulang Request for Institutional Amendment sa Committee on Appropriations para mapunuan ito?

**[05:55:01.740] Rep. Ed Christopher S. Go** (asr-005718)  
Yes po, Mr. Speaker.

**[05:55:02.700] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005719)  
So, may submission po. So, in other words, maaaring matalakay ito sa iko-convene na BARC.

**[05:55:09.840] Rep. Ed Christopher S. Go** (asr-005720)  
Yes, Mr. Speaker.

**[05:55:10.320] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005721)  
Budget Amendments Review Subcommittee ng Committee on Appropriations.

**[05:55:14.980] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005722)  
So, okay. Asahan niyo po na susuportahan po natin.

**[05:55:17.500] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005723)  
For as long as na-submit niyo yung

**[05:55:18.680] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005724)  
institutional amendment, ay susuportahan po natin ito.

**[05:55:22.980] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005725)  
Mabanggit ko na rin po na bilang bahagi ng implementation ng Filipino Sign Language Act,

**[05:55:29.680] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005726)

**[05:55:36.500] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005727)  
Pero mga job order or contract of service.

**[05:55:41.540] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005728)  
Tama po ba? Do you confirm this?

**[05:55:44.220] Rep. Ed Christopher S. Go** (asr-005729)  
Yes, Mr. Speaker.

**[05:55:45.040] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005730)  
Okay.

**[05:55:48.680] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005731)  
Wala po bang proposal ang KWF para mabigyan ng plantilla positions,

**[05:55:58.160] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005732)

**[05:56:04.000] Rep. Ed Christopher S. Go** (asr-005733)  
Obviously, meron po, Mr. Speaker.

**[05:56:06.400] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005734)  
Kailangan. Para ma-implement yung batas, kailangan.

**[05:56:08.940] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005735)  
So, meron po. Kasama po ba dun sa inyong proposal na 27 million ang plantilla positions para sa dalawang ito?

**[05:56:18.680] Rep. Ed Christopher S. Go** (asr-005736)  
Yes, Mr. Speaker.

**[05:56:20.180] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005737)  
Yes. Okay. So, salamat po kung gayon.

**[05:56:24.560] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005738)  
At asahan po ninyo na susuportahan natin ang mungkahing Institutional Amendments for additional 27 million.

**[05:56:35.040] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005739)  
Napakaliit na halaga na lang po ito para sa Filipino deaf, mga kababayan nating bingi sa buong bansa.

**[05:56:45.960] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005740)  
At now, let me move on.

**[05:56:48.680] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005741)  
Let me move on to another topic.

**[05:56:50.500] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005742)  
Ito po ay sumulat po sa amin at kahit po sa, alam ko, kahit sa commission kay KWF Chair,

**[05:57:02.000] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005743)  
Attorney Marites Barrios-Taran, ang isang professor ng Filipino sa UP.

**[05:57:08.700] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005744)  
Sa totoo lang, naging kasamahan ko po sa Departamento ng Filipino at Panitikan ng Pilipinas.

**[05:57:16.580] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005745)  
Ang professor na ito, si Romel.

**[05:57:18.680] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005746)  
Romel Rodriguez, Professor Romel Rodriguez, omeng.

**[05:57:22.360] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005747)  
Ilala ko po siya bilang omeng dahil matagal po kaming nagkasama, nagturo kami sa Filipino Department for a long time.

**[05:57:32.840] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005748)  
Noong 2022, siya po ay author ng manuskritong kalatas, mga kwentong bayan at kwentong buhay na ginawa sa ilalim ng KWF.

**[05:57:47.740] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005749)  
Pero noong Agosto 2022, naglabas po ng memo, ang KWF, tinawag na subersibo at may red tagging pa sa author at sa akdang ito, kasama ng iba pa.

**[05:58:05.880] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005750)  
So total limang authors at mga akda nila ang dineklarang subersibo ng KWF, kaya pinigilan ang paglabas, ang publication.

**[05:58:17.740] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005751)  
Subsequently, naging issue po, dinala po kahit dito sa Kongreso during the budget deliberations, at winit draw yung memo na iyon ng KWF.

**[05:58:28.420] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005752)  
Gayunpaman, mula noon, 2022 hanggang ngayon, ay hindi na nalabas yung libro.

**[05:58:36.820] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005753)  
Ngayon, ang isang author po na ito, si omeng, si Professor Romel Rodriguez, ay nagtatanong po.

**[05:58:44.620] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005754)  
Pinaghirapan, tinrabaho, at magandang kalidad.

**[05:58:47.740] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005755)  
Ito po natin, si Professor Rodriguez, alam natin, isa sa mga batikang manunulat, awarded, at scholar, scholar, talag-tunay, genuine scholar, tagapagsulong ng wikang Filipino.

**[05:59:01.280] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005756)  
So ngayon, nag-aapila po siya. Ano na nangyari sa kanyang libro, bakit hindi pa rin nilalabas?

**[05:59:08.200] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005757)  
And in fact, humihingi po ng dialogue with the KWF.

**[05:59:11.680] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005758)  
Meron po bang update ang komisyon sa kasong ito?

**[05:59:16.500] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005759)  
Bakit nakaipit?

**[05:59:17.740] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005760)  
Bakit nakaipit pa rin yung libro?

**[05:59:20.680] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005761)  
Ano ito? Hindi katanggap-tanggap. Kaya nga pinabawi yung memo eh, dahil censorship po ito.

**[05:59:26.640] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005762)  
And red tagging, wala namang basis para tawaging subersibo o red tagging.

**[05:59:36.140] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005763)  
Ang akda, red tag, ang akda.

**[05:59:39.000] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-005764)  
Ano po ang update dito?

**[05:59:41.540] Rep. Ed Christopher S. Go** (asr-005765)  
Mr. Speaker, KWF will act on this after going through the ISO.

**[05:59:47.740] Rep. Ed Christopher S. Go** (asr-005766)

**[06:31:17.340] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-006171)  
produce science-based information on water availability, water quality, groundwater, and climate risks.

**[06:31:36.260] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-006176)

**[06:32:10.840] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-006182)

**[06:32:25.300] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-006187)

**[06:32:28.040] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-006188)

**[06:32:49.320] Deputy Speaker Jose C. Alvarez** (asr-006191)  
Mr. Speaker, distinguished colleague.

**[06:32:51.260] Deputy Speaker Jose C. Alvarez** (asr-006192)

**[06:32:57.340] Deputy Speaker Jose C. Alvarez** (asr-006193)

**[06:32:59.300] Deputy Speaker Jose C. Alvarez** (asr-006194)

**[06:33:10.140] Deputy Speaker Jose C. Alvarez** (asr-006195)

**[06:33:14.020] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-006196)

**[06:33:27.320] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-006198)

**[06:33:57.320] Deputy Speaker Jose C. Alvarez** (asr-006205)  
Mr. Speaker, distinguished colleague.

**[06:34:05.040] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-006208)

**[06:34:09.440] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-006209)

**[06:34:11.800] Deputy Speaker Jose C. Alvarez** (asr-006210)

**[06:34:17.540] Deputy Speaker Jose C. Alvarez** (asr-006211)

**[06:34:24.440] Deputy Speaker Jose C. Alvarez** (asr-006213)

**[06:34:27.320] Deputy Speaker Jose C. Alvarez** (asr-006214)

Mr. Speaker, distinguished colleague.

**[06:34:43.140] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-006219)

**[06:34:57.320] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-006221)

**[06:35:19.220] Deputy Speaker Jose C. Alvarez** (asr-006224)  
Ang hindi nila mapiperforma yun, katulad nung Pampanga Basin na yan, all the way to the north.

**[06:35:24.940] Deputy Speaker Jose C. Alvarez** (asr-006225)  
Eh, there is no such thing.

**[06:35:27.320] Deputy Speaker Jose C. Alvarez** (asr-006226)

**[06:35:29.840] Deputy Speaker Jose C. Alvarez** (asr-006227)

**[06:35:34.740] Deputy Speaker Jose C. Alvarez** (asr-006228)

**[06:35:40.080] Deputy Speaker Jose C. Alvarez** (asr-006229)

**[06:35:47.040] Deputy Speaker Jose C. Alvarez** (asr-006230)

**[06:35:52.280] Deputy Speaker Jose C. Alvarez** (asr-006231)

**[06:35:56.460] Deputy Speaker Jose C. Alvarez** (asr-006232)  
Mr. Speaker, distinguished colleague.

**[06:35:58.980] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-006233)  
Thank you, Mr. Speaker.

**[06:36:01.180] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-006234)

**[06:38:54.020] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-006283)  
kapag ito ay within or...

**[06:39:07.320] Deputy Speaker Jose C. Alvarez** (asr-006288)  
eh, yung malawakang pagtanim sa dalawang kanti o kansi.

**[06:39:12.960] Deputy Speaker Jose C. Alvarez** (asr-006289)

**[06:39:15.900] Deputy Speaker Jose C. Alvarez** (asr-006290)

**[06:39:20.220] Deputy Speaker Jose C. Alvarez** (asr-006291)

**[06:39:26.340] Deputy Speaker Jose C. Alvarez** (asr-006292)

**[06:39:27.020] Deputy Speaker Jose C. Alvarez** (asr-006293)

**[06:39:33.080] Deputy Speaker Jose C. Alvarez** (asr-006294)  
Mr. Speaker, distinguished colleague, yan po ang procedure.

**[06:39:36.820] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-006295)

**[06:39:40.560] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-006296)

**[06:39:51.800] Deputy Speaker Jose C. Alvarez** (asr-006298)

**[06:39:54.460] Deputy Speaker Jose C. Alvarez** (asr-006299)

**[06:40:07.520] Deputy Speaker Jose C. Alvarez** (asr-006304)

**[06:40:10.780] Deputy Speaker Jose C. Alvarez** (asr-006305)

**[06:40:18.020] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-006307)

**[06:40:28.600] Deputy Speaker Jose C. Alvarez** (asr-006309)

**[06:40:38.840] Deputy Speaker Jose C. Alvarez** (asr-006312)

Meron,

**[06:40:42.520] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-006313-split-2)

**[06:41:22.380] Deputy Speaker Jose C. Alvarez** (asr-006320)

**[06:41:31.700] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-006323)

**[06:41:40.940] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-006324)

Ang Executive Order No. 79, dated July 7 of 2012,

**[06:41:54.860] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-006326)

Mayroon?

**[06:42:51.860] Deputy Speaker Jose C. Alvarez** (asr-006337)

**[06:43:30.220] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-006343-split-2)  
Yung MAPA.

**[06:43:31.780] Deputy Speaker Jose C. Alvarez** (asr-006344)

**[06:43:38.440] Deputy Speaker Jose C. Alvarez** (asr-006345)

**[06:43:41.280] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-006346)

**[06:43:41.960] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-006347)

Dali lang.

**[06:44:18.440] Deputy Speaker Jose C. Alvarez** (asr-006356)

**[06:44:28.780] Deputy Speaker Jose C. Alvarez** (asr-006361)  
Mr. Speaker, distinguished colleague.

**[06:44:31.160] Deputy Speaker Jose C. Alvarez** (asr-006362)  
Yung strengthening doon sa, ayun, para magpatuloy, maliit man ito, nakakahiya.

**[06:44:38.900] Deputy Speaker Jose C. Alvarez** (asr-006363)

**[06:44:46.540] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-006364)  
Tama po.

**[06:44:47.140] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-006365)  
Tingin ko po, ito'y kulang din po, no?

**[06:44:50.020] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-006366)  
Oo.

**[06:44:50.380] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-006367)  
para mapagpatuloy nila yung patungkol sa budget na ito para masupervise nila yung pag-audit sa mga...

**[06:45:00.000] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-006368)  
Anyway, Mr. Speaker, Mr. Sponsor, kaugnay pa rin po sa mining, ano ang status of the mining in Sibuyan Island owned by Altai Minerals Development Corporation, nakarating po sa aking opisina na ito ay temporarily nahinto dahil sa barikada ng mga mamamayan at mismo ang LGU at local residents ang nagpapatawag na i-cancel na ang mining contract.

**[06:45:30.000] Deputy Speaker Jose C. Alvarez** (asr-006369)  
Mr. Speaker, di ba yung Sibuyan Island, maliit lang nga yun eh. So dapat talaga i-suspendihin. As of now, suspended yun, Mr. Speaker, I'm distinguished colleague.

**[06:45:40.760] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-006370)  
May plano po ba ang DNR na i-resume ito?

**[06:45:44.460] Deputy Speaker Jose C. Alvarez** (asr-006371)  
Wala pa, Mr. Speaker.

**[06:45:45.880] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-006372)  
Maraming salamat po.

**[06:45:47.200] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-006373)  
Hinding-hindi dapat maging kapalit ng mining ang karapatan at mabuting kalagayan ng mga komunidad.

**[06:45:53.880] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-006374)  
Ang kahit anong economic activity sa ating bansa ay dapat nakaayon sa utos ng Saligang Batas.

**[06:46:00.000] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-006375)  
Na bawat Pilipino ay may karapatan sa isang balanced at healthful ecology.

**[06:46:06.520] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-006376)  
Maraming salamat, Mr. Sponsor, sa pagsagot sa mga tanong na iyan.

**[06:46:11.640] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-006377)  
Ngayon po, nais ko naman pong balikan ang issue patungkol sa Financial or Technical Assistance Agreements o FTAA.

**[06:46:20.700] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-006378)  
Noong budget briefing po, na-raise ko ang tungkol sa renewal ng FTAAs.

**[06:46:26.100] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-006379)  
Can the DNR explain their role in the FTAA?

**[06:46:30.000] Deputy Speaker Jose C. Alvarez** (asr-006380)  
Isa lang, Mr. Speaker, distinguished colleague. Sa Region 1, yung Altamina Exploration and Resources, Iron Ore, Gold and Vanadium Resources (Philippines), Inc.

**[06:46:45.800] Deputy Speaker Jose C. Alvarez** (asr-006381)  
Sa Region 2, yung OceanaGold.

**[06:46:49.940] Deputy Speaker Jose C. Alvarez** (asr-006382)  
Sa MIMAROPA, sa amin, yung Agusan Petroleum and Minerals Corporation, pero hindi pa ito pinayagan.

**[06:46:57.080] Deputy Speaker Jose C. Alvarez** (asr-006383)  
Region 12, Sagittarius Mines.

**[06:46:58.840] Deputy Speaker Jose C. Alvarez** (asr-006384)  
Lahat ito.

**[06:47:00.000] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-006385)  
So, Mr. Speaker, distinguished colleague, ano po kaya ang role ng DNR dito sa FTAAs?

**[06:47:06.040] Deputy Speaker Jose C. Alvarez** (asr-006386)  
Nagmo-monitor lang, Mr. Speaker, ng compliance.

**[06:47:08.220] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-006387)  
Monitoring. Salamat po.

**[06:47:09.980] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-006388)  
Kinoconfirm din po sa budget hearing na DNR na merong allocated budget para sa monitoring at enforcement ng FTAAs.

**[06:47:19.460] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-006389)  
Distinguished Sponsor, paano ginagamit ng DNR ang halagang ito para palakasin ang monitoring at enforcement ng FTAAs?

**[06:47:29.080] Deputy Speaker Jose C. Alvarez** (asr-006390)  
Mr. Speaker, distinguished colleague, siyempre, kung may pera sila, they have to travel kasi they cannot do the monitoring dito lang sila sa Manila.

**[06:47:36.500] Deputy Speaker Jose C. Alvarez** (asr-006391)  
Pumunta katulad sa Cebu yan, they have to go there.

**[06:47:39.060] Deputy Speaker Jose C. Alvarez** (asr-006392)  
Katulad doon sa Humonhon, kailangan pumunta sila doon.

**[06:47:42.320] Deputy Speaker Jose C. Alvarez** (asr-006393)  
Ang daming mina.

**[06:47:43.860] Deputy Speaker Jose C. Alvarez** (asr-006394)  
Sa Palawan nga, meron kaming mining moratorium for 50 years, pinirmahan nila.

**[06:47:49.060] Deputy Speaker Jose C. Alvarez** (asr-006395)  
So, wala na kaming bagong ina-accept ng mga application.

**[06:47:52.960] Deputy Speaker Jose C. Alvarez** (asr-006396)  
Kaya yung ibang mga probinsya, sabihin mo, Mr. Speaker, gumawa din ng mining moratorium.

**[06:47:59.080] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-006397)  
Maraming salamat po, Mr. Sponsor.

**[06:48:03.420] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-006398)  
Iko-confirm ko lang din po na sakaling may nahuli kayo dito na lumalabag sa FTAAs po during your monitoring, ano po yung ginagawa nila?

**[06:48:15.600] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-006399)  
Distinguished sponsor.

**[06:48:20.080] Deputy Speaker Jose C. Alvarez** (asr-006400)  
Kung nag-ooperate pa yan, Mr. Speaker, suspended immediately.

**[06:48:25.220] Deputy Speaker Jose C. Alvarez** (asr-006401)  
Pagkatapos, pag nag-violet pa talaga, eh talagang hindi lang suspended.

**[06:48:29.080] Deputy Speaker Jose C. Alvarez** (asr-006402)  
Yan, multahan yan.

**[06:48:31.040] Deputy Speaker Jose C. Alvarez** (asr-006403)  
Kung hindi naman madala sa multa, eh ang DNR, baka ilibing na lang nila yun doon sa gamitin yung bakho, Mr. Speaker.

**[06:48:39.300] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-006404)  
Kinakasuhan din po ba natin?

**[06:48:41.160] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-006405)  
Eh, due process pala. I'm sorry.

**[06:48:43.540] Deputy Speaker Jose C. Alvarez** (asr-006406)  
Hindi pala pwede yung bakho gagamitin doon pag hindi sumunod sa DNR.

**[06:48:50.120] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-006407)  
So, kinakasuhan po natin sakaling lumabag?

**[06:48:52.760] Deputy Speaker Jose C. Alvarez** (asr-006408)  
Kaso, multa.

**[06:48:53.700] Deputy Speaker Jose C. Alvarez** (asr-006409)  
Hindi pa talaga pumayag sa multa.

**[06:48:57.080] Deputy Speaker Jose C. Alvarez** (asr-006410)  
Bakho.

**[06:48:57.480] Deputy Speaker Jose C. Alvarez** (asr-006411)  
Eh, lalo na kung hindi.

**[06:48:59.080] Deputy Speaker Jose C. Alvarez** (asr-006412)  
Kaya naman, Pilipino citizen, eh alam mo na kung anong parosa, Mr. Speaker, Distinguished Colleague.

**[06:49:04.220] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-006413)  
Tama po.

**[06:49:05.040] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-006414)  
Kasama rin po ba rito ang pagsuri kung compliant ang isang FTAA proposal sa mga requirements

**[06:49:12.160] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-006415)  
gaya ng Environmental Compliance Certificate or FPIC bago ito ma-approve?

**[06:49:18.120] Deputy Speaker Jose C. Alvarez** (asr-006416)  
Tama, Mr. Speaker, Distinguished Colleague. You're very correct.

**[06:49:20.800] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-006417)  
Na-e-ensure ba ng DNR na ang mga renewal ay di nagiging automatic at sumasailalim pa rin

**[06:49:28.440] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-006418)  
sa pagpasa ng mga requirements?

**[06:49:30.560] Deputy Speaker Jose C. Alvarez** (asr-006419)  
Mr. Speaker, hindi automatic. Talagang ina-isa-isa yan.

**[06:49:33.900] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-006420)  
Maraming salamat po sa pag-confirm, Mr. Sponsor.

**[06:49:38.280] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-006421)  
Mr. Speaker, Distinguished Sponsor, ngayong araw ko lang po natanggap ang sagot ng DNR

**[06:49:45.360] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-006422)  
hinggil sa FTAA No. 1 ng Oceana Gold Philippines Inc.

**[06:49:50.280] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-006423)  
at FTAA No. 2 ng Tampakan Project.

**[06:49:54.860] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-006424)  
Ang paliwanag ng DNR ay sa FTAA No. 1,

**[06:49:58.440] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-006425)  
walang grave abuse of discretion dahil ito raw ay sumusunod sa intention of the agreement.

**[06:50:05.800] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-006426)  
At ang FTAA No. 2, restoration of the lost term naman daw po.

**[06:50:12.420] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-006427)  
Paano po ito na ipapaliwanag ng DNR sa ating mga lokal, sa ating mga katutubo?

**[06:50:19.860] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-006428)  
Binababaan po ba natin ang ating mga komunidad para sabihin ang ganitong technical na reasoning sa kanila?

**[06:50:28.440] Deputy Speaker Jose C. Alvarez** (asr-006429)  
Siyempre, Mr. Speaker, unang-una, hirap ka mang pumasok doon kung may mga katutubong nagbabantay.

**[06:50:36.760] Deputy Speaker Jose C. Alvarez** (asr-006430)  
So, kailangan kasapin mo yan sila.

**[06:50:39.120] Deputy Speaker Jose C. Alvarez** (asr-006431)  
Katulad ng Tampakan, eh patagal yan natulog ang Tampakan eh. Ngayon na lang yan tinatampak.

**[06:50:43.920] Deputy Speaker Jose C. Alvarez** (asr-006432)  
So, hopefully, if you ask me, Mr. Speaker, Distinguished Colleague,

**[06:50:48.860] Deputy Speaker Jose C. Alvarez** (asr-006433)  
yung mina hindi naman talagang masama per se.

**[06:50:51.360] Deputy Speaker Jose C. Alvarez** (asr-006434)  
Basta huwag lang mag-abuso. Lalong-lalo na kung yung nagmimina ay Pilipino.

**[06:50:55.440] Deputy Speaker Jose C. Alvarez** (asr-006435)  
Ang ayaw natin makita, kung yung nagmimina,

**[06:50:58.440] Deputy Speaker Jose C. Alvarez** (asr-006436)  
hindi Pilipino. Dahil ang mina, reserve lang yan, natural resources,

**[06:51:02.320] Deputy Speaker Jose C. Alvarez** (asr-006437)  
is reserved only for Pilipino citizens.

**[06:51:05.780] Deputy Speaker Jose C. Alvarez** (asr-006438)  
I repeat that, Mr. Speaker.

**[06:51:08.060] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-006439)  
Mr. Speaker, Distinguished Sponsor,

**[06:51:12.160] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-006440)

**[06:51:15.880] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-006441)

**[06:51:19.380] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-006442)  
Hindi pa po nila nasasabihan, naa-update sa status.

**[06:51:23.540] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-006443)  
Nagpadala po kayo sa akin ng sulat,

**[06:51:25.820] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-006444)  
pero hindi nyo po sinabihan sa baba.

**[06:51:28.440] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-006445)  
Ang LGU lamang po ang sinabihan ninyo,

**[06:51:31.440] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-006446)

**[06:51:33.440] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-006447)  
In closing, Mr. Speaker, Distinguished Sponsor,

**[06:51:38.200] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-006448)

**[06:51:43.720] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-006449)

**[06:51:47.960] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-006450)  
ang ating mga komunidad na direktang apektado.

**[06:51:51.280] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-006451)  
Ang DNR ay hindi Department of Extract Our Natural Resources.

**[06:51:58.280] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-006452)  
Ang DNR ay pinagkatiwala ng taong bayan kasama ang mga katutubong Pilipino

**[06:52:05.280] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-006453)  
na pangalagaan ang ating kalikasan.

**[06:52:08.280] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-006454)  
Maraming salamat, Distinguished Sponsor, for yielding to my question.

**[06:52:13.280] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-006455)  
Salamat po.

**[06:52:14.280] Deputy Speaker Jose C. Alvarez** (asr-006456)  
Maraming salamat, Madam Speaker, Distinguished Sponsor.

**[06:52:17.280] Deputy Speaker Jose C. Alvarez** (asr-006457)  
Takang salamat.

**[06:52:19.280] Deputy Speaker Jose C. Alvarez** (asr-006458)  
Next time pwede at i-allow nila magbinisaya at nakaka-kapoy kay magtinagalog.

**[06:52:25.280] Deputy Speaker Mercedes K. Alvarez** (asr-006459)  
Majority Leader?

**[06:52:28.280] Rep. Mark Anthony G. Santos** (asr-006460)  
Madam Speaker, next to interpolate the sponsor,

**[06:52:31.280] Rep. Mark Anthony G. Santos** (asr-006461)  
I move to recognize Representative Christopher Sheen Gonzales

**[06:52:37.280] Rep. Mark Anthony G. Santos** (asr-006462)  
from the Lone District of Eastern Samar.

**[06:52:40.280] Rep. Mark Anthony G. Santos** (asr-006463)  
So move, Madam Speaker.

**[06:52:42.280] Deputy Speaker Mercedes K. Alvarez** (asr-006464)  
Representative Christopher Sheen Gonzales from the Lone District of Eastern Samar

**[06:52:46.280] Deputy Speaker Mercedes K. Alvarez** (asr-006465)  
is hereby recognized for his interpolation.

**[06:52:48.280] Rep. Christopher Sheen P. Gonzales** (asr-006466)  
Maraming salamat po, Madam Speaker, to colleagues and to the good sponsor.

**[06:52:55.280] Rep. Christopher Sheen P. Gonzales** (asr-006467)  
Magandang hapon po sa ating lahat.

**[06:52:58.280] Rep. Christopher Sheen P. Gonzales** (asr-006468)  
Will the good sponsor yield for a few questions po?

**[06:53:02.280] Deputy Speaker Jose C. Alvarez** (asr-006469)  
Gladly, Madam Speaker, Distinguished Colleague.

**[06:53:05.280] Deputy Speaker Jose C. Alvarez** (asr-006470)  
Please, go ahead.

**[06:53:07.280] Rep. Christopher Sheen P. Gonzales** (asr-006471)  
Madam Speaker, nung nakaraang budget briefing sa budget ng Department of Environment and Natural Resources,

**[06:53:15.280] Rep. Christopher Sheen P. Gonzales** (asr-006472)  
nag-commit ang ahensya particular po ang Mines and Geosciences Bureau na personal napuntahan ang isla ng Humonhon sa giwan Eastern Samar

**[06:53:26.280] Rep. Christopher Sheen P. Gonzales** (asr-006473)  
para mag-imbestiga ng mga reported issues and concerns sa ginagawang mining activities po doon.

**[06:53:33.280] Rep. Christopher Sheen P. Gonzales** (asr-006474)  
Sa ngayon po, ang question ko po, Madam Speaker, to the good sponsor,

**[06:53:39.280] Rep. Christopher Sheen P. Gonzales** (asr-006475)  
nakapagsagawa po ba ng imbistigasyon ng ahensya sa isla ng Humonhon?

**[06:53:45.280] Rep. Christopher Sheen P. Gonzales** (asr-006476)  
Nakapagsagawa po ba ng mga actual inspection ng DENR o MGB sa isla ng Humonhon?

**[06:53:53.280] Deputy Speaker Jose C. Alvarez** (asr-006477)  
Madam Speaker, yes.

**[06:53:56.280] Rep. Christopher Sheen P. Gonzales** (asr-006478)  
Ang tanong ko po, alam ko naman na nakita doon ng nag-inspeksyon ng mga taga-DENR.

**[06:54:11.280] Rep. Christopher Sheen P. Gonzales** (asr-006479)  
Madam Speaker, kung mapatunayan po na may mga violations ang MPSA o mga nagmimina po doon,

**[06:54:23.280] Rep. Christopher Sheen P. Gonzales** (asr-006480)  
inaasahan po ng representasyong ito na gagawa po ng mga hakbang ang DENR at ang representasyon po ito ay babantayan ang mga pangyayaring ito.

**[06:54:45.280] Rep. Christopher Sheen P. Gonzales** (asr-006481)  
So, sa tingin ko po, Madam Speaker,

**[06:54:50.280] Rep. Christopher Sheen P. Gonzales** (asr-006482)  
ang nararapat po ba na sanksyon sa mga nag-iiligal kung meron man po, gagawin po ba ng DENR?

**[06:55:00.280] Deputy Speaker Jose C. Alvarez** (asr-006483)  
Madam Speaker, distinguished colleague, ito lahat sila sa likod ko nagkocommit sa iyo na pag may mga violation dyan sa mina,

**[06:55:11.280] Deputy Speaker Jose C. Alvarez** (asr-006484)  
lalong-lalong na hindi Pilipino yung gumagawa ng mina, eh...

**[06:55:17.280] Rep. Christopher Sheen P. Gonzales** (asr-006485)  
Maraming salamat po.

**[06:55:18.280] Deputy Speaker Jose C. Alvarez** (asr-006486)  
Maraming salamat po.

**[06:55:20.280] Deputy Speaker Jose C. Alvarez** (asr-006487)  
Sobrang sila isandaan dito. They fully commit to support the local.

**[06:55:26.280] Deputy Speaker Jose C. Alvarez** (asr-006488)  
O yun, palakpak!

**[06:55:28.280] Deputy Speaker Jose C. Alvarez** (asr-006489)  
Salamat, salamat.

**[06:55:36.280] Rep. Christopher Sheen P. Gonzales** (asr-006490)  
During, with regards to the MPSA, during the DENR budget briefing, concerns were raised regarding possible violations of the terms and conditions

**[06:55:47.280] Rep. Christopher Sheen P. Gonzales** (asr-006491)  
governing these mining operations in MPSA.

**[06:55:48.280] Rep. Christopher Sheen P. Gonzales** (asr-006492)  
The MPSA itself requires compliance with the Mining Act.

**[06:55:57.280] Rep. Christopher Sheen P. Gonzales** (asr-006493)  
Its implementing rules, Madam Speaker, approve work programs and other applicable laws.

**[06:56:04.280] Rep. Christopher Sheen P. Gonzales** (asr-006494)  
DENR, on the other hand, retains regularity, supervisory, and monitoring authority.

**[06:56:14.280] Rep. Christopher Sheen P. Gonzales** (asr-006495)  
Ang tanong,

**[06:56:16.280] Rep. Christopher Sheen P. Gonzales** (asr-006496)  
maaari po bang mangako ulit ang DENR na ang kanilang isa sa gawa ay comprehensive review ng MPSA na ito, kasama ang lahat ng mga amendments, assignment, operating agreements, at kung nasusunod ba talaga ang mga terms and conditions dito?

**[06:56:37.280] Rep. Christopher Sheen P. Gonzales** (asr-006497)  
Madam Speaker, to the board sponsor...

**[06:56:39.280] Deputy Speaker Jose C. Alvarez** (asr-006498)  
Madam Speaker, distinguished colleague, lahat sila nung sa likod ko, in-ok.

**[06:56:44.280] Deputy Speaker Jose C. Alvarez** (asr-006499)  
Sabi nila, pag ikaw ang nag-lead doon sa inspection team at nakita nila, in spite doon sa mga monitoring nila, kung mayroon pang ibang violation, hindi lang suspension.

**[06:56:58.280] Deputy Speaker Jose C. Alvarez** (asr-006500)  
Kung hindi, talagang iteterminate yung pagmimina doon dahil napakaliit nung is lang yan.

**[06:57:03.280] Deputy Speaker Jose C. Alvarez** (asr-006501)  
Nadaan nga ako raw, wala nang matamnan eh, hukay na lang sila ng hukay.

**[06:57:07.280] Deputy Speaker Jose C. Alvarez** (asr-006502)  
Ayaw natin na yung humungun, pareha sa nauru.

**[06:57:11.280] Deputy Speaker Jose C. Alvarez** (asr-006503)  
Yung nauru, wala nang, wala nang phosphate.

**[06:57:14.280] Deputy Speaker Jose C. Alvarez** (asr-006504)  
Ang mga tao doon ngayon sa Australia na nakatira.

**[06:57:17.280] Deputy Speaker Jose C. Alvarez** (asr-006505)  
Lihit na lang yung village na naiwan kasi kot-kot sila ng kot-kot.

**[06:57:21.280] Deputy Speaker Jose C. Alvarez** (asr-006506)

**[07:23:23.800] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-006819)

**[07:23:34.180] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-006820)  
We'd like to supplement a little further given that we've been asking various agencies what they know of this project.

**[07:23:43.120] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-006821)

**[07:23:53.800] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-006822)  
Sa mga mamayan tungkol sa iba't-ibang issues.

**[07:23:57.160] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-006823)  
Pero ang pinaka-na-perview naman po ng DNR dito ay yung usaping kalikasan, usaping tubig, usaping lupa.

**[07:24:05.420] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-006824)  
In the last Committee on Appropriations for DTI meeting, Undersecretary Ridolfo stated that investors are expected to provide their own power and water for the proposed Paxilica development in New Clark City.

**[07:24:17.520] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-006826)  
But contrary to this, the government, through a Malacañang press conference, has publicly assured

**[07:24:23.800] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-006827)  
that the said development will have sufficient water without compromising the supply of surrounding communities. The National Water Resources Board reportedly stated that available sources within the project area can meet the proposed facilities' requirements and that community water needs will remain the priority. BCDA has separately stated that the proposed surface water harvesting system could initially supply up to 120 million liters per day, compared

**[07:24:53.800] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-006828)  
With an estimated Paxilica requirement of approximately 65 to 90 million liters per day, with potential expansion of the system to 300 million liters per day.

**[07:25:05.060] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-006829)  
So ang pansin po natin, iba-iba yung nire-report sa atin ng iba't-ibang ahensya tungkol sa tubig.

**[07:25:12.840] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-006830)  
Yung una, sige pahala po yung Paxilica na maghanap ng tubig.

**[07:25:17.260] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-006831)  
Tapos ngayon, meron na pong mga iba't-ibang sources na tinutukoy at may pag-aaral at pag-iimbestiga na po.

**[07:25:23.800] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-006832)

**[07:26:22.280] Deputy Speaker Jose C. Alvarez** (asr-006841)

**[07:26:27.000] Deputy Speaker Jose C. Alvarez** (asr-006842)

**[07:26:33.240] Deputy Speaker Jose C. Alvarez** (asr-006843)

**[07:26:43.380] Deputy Speaker Jose C. Alvarez** (asr-006844)

**[07:26:52.280] Deputy Speaker Jose C. Alvarez** (asr-006845)

**[07:27:00.940] Deputy Speaker Jose C. Alvarez** (asr-006846)  
Hindi lang natin iniipon yung tubig natin at saka ginagamit.

**[07:27:06.840] Deputy Speaker Jose C. Alvarez** (asr-006847)

**[07:27:15.300] Deputy Speaker Jose C. Alvarez** (asr-006848)  
Kaya maski abot sila ng limang buwan hanggang anum na buwan na ilin ninyo, ay hindi po sila naubusan ng tubig.

**[07:27:22.280] Deputy Speaker Jose C. Alvarez** (asr-006849)  
Doon sa atin, sa Central Luzon, napakaraming underground water.

**[07:27:25.700] Deputy Speaker Jose C. Alvarez** (asr-006850)  
But if I were the government, I will not allow this kind of project to extract water from the underground aquifer, Madam Speaker,

**[07:27:34.320] Deputy Speaker Jose C. Alvarez** (asr-006851)

**[07:27:40.060] Deputy Speaker Jose C. Alvarez** (asr-006852)  
Walang tubig na nanggagaling sa ilalim ng lupa. Lahat yan ay recharged ng rainfall.

**[07:27:46.940] Deputy Speaker Jose C. Alvarez** (asr-006853)  
Papunta po, nag-infiltrate sa ilalim ng lupa. Kaya na-stack up sila doon sa underground aquifer.

**[07:27:52.280] Deputy Speaker Jose C. Alvarez** (asr-006854)  
Kung marami naman ang extraction natin, makakaroon niya ng mga land subsidence.

**[07:27:58.020] Deputy Speaker Jose C. Alvarez** (asr-006855)  
Kaya dyan sa Northern Luzon, starting from Pampanga up doon sa mga kabundukan sa Sierra Madre, ay maraming tubig dyan.

**[07:28:08.260] Deputy Speaker Jose C. Alvarez** (asr-006856)  
Ngunit, that is reserved for the communities.

**[07:28:12.080] Deputy Speaker Jose C. Alvarez** (asr-006857)  
If itong Paxilica, if I were the government, they will have to bring their own power.

**[07:28:16.980] Deputy Speaker Jose C. Alvarez** (asr-006858)

**[07:28:18.860] Deputy Speaker Jose C. Alvarez** (asr-006859)

**[07:28:22.280] Deputy Speaker Jose C. Alvarez** (asr-006860)

**[07:28:24.580] Deputy Speaker Jose C. Alvarez** (asr-006861)  
You can rely on the surface water.

**[07:28:27.440] Deputy Speaker Jose C. Alvarez** (asr-006862)  
Yung sinasabi nilang 100 million liters a day is only 100,000 cubic meters yan, Madam Speaker, distinguished sponsor.

**[07:28:38.860] Deputy Speaker Jose C. Alvarez** (asr-006863)  
Kaya naman, kung 10 million liters, 100 million liters ang kailangan nila isang araw,

**[07:28:46.200] Deputy Speaker Jose C. Alvarez** (asr-006864)

**[07:28:47.820] Deputy Speaker Jose C. Alvarez** (asr-006865)  
Hindi masyadong marami yan.

**[07:28:49.980] Deputy Speaker Jose C. Alvarez** (asr-006866)  
If you measure, Madam Speaker,

**[07:28:52.280] Deputy Speaker Jose C. Alvarez** (asr-006867)  
distinguished colleague,

**[07:28:53.900] Deputy Speaker Jose C. Alvarez** (asr-006868)  
yung outflow ng tubig natin sa Central Luzon,

**[07:28:57.320] Deputy Speaker Jose C. Alvarez** (asr-006869)

**[07:29:00.020] Deputy Speaker Jose C. Alvarez** (asr-006870)

**[07:29:01.240] Deputy Speaker Jose C. Alvarez** (asr-006871)

**[07:29:05.580] Deputy Speaker Jose C. Alvarez** (asr-006872)  
Kaya huwag tayo matakot na walang tubig sa Pilipinas.

**[07:29:08.800] Deputy Speaker Jose C. Alvarez** (asr-006873)  
Napakarami natin tubig dito sa Pilipinas.

**[07:29:11.420] Deputy Speaker Jose C. Alvarez** (asr-006874)

**[07:29:13.460] Deputy Speaker Jose C. Alvarez** (asr-006875)

**[07:29:18.900] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-006876)  
Mr. Speaker, good sponsor.

**[07:29:56.560] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-006885)

**[07:30:00.000] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-006887)

**[07:31:17.120] Deputy Speaker Jose C. Alvarez** (asr-006899)

**[07:31:22.680] Deputy Speaker Jose C. Alvarez** (asr-006900)

**[07:31:23.300] Deputy Speaker Jose C. Alvarez** (asr-006901)

**[07:31:27.120] Deputy Speaker Jose C. Alvarez** (asr-006902)

eh that is too early.

**[08:18:39.200] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-008070)  
at particular,

**[08:18:42.360] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-008071)

**[08:18:51.540] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-008075)

**[08:18:55.960] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-008077)

**[08:18:57.140] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-008078)

**[08:18:59.200] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-008079)  
ang counterpart niya sa US,

**[08:19:00.880] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-008080)

**[08:19:04.100] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-008081)

**[08:19:06.280] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-008082)  
Itong si Jacob Helberg,

**[08:19:07.600] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-008083)

**[08:19:09.100] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-008084)

**[08:19:13.640] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-008085)

**[08:19:17.500] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-008086)

**[08:19:20.320] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-008087)  
So, yung Memorandum of Understanding na ito,

**[08:19:23.760] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-008088)  
the formal title is

**[08:19:25.340] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-008089)  
Memorandum of Understanding

**[08:19:27.140] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-008094)  
between the Government of the Republic of the Philippines

**[08:19:29.440] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-008095)  
and the Government of the United States

**[08:19:31.160] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-008096)  
of America

**[08:19:33.440] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-008097)  
concerning cooperation

**[08:19:35.120] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-008098)  
to diversify global critical

**[08:19:37.460] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-008099)  
mineral supply chains

**[08:19:39.300] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-008100)  
and promote investments.

**[08:19:42.360] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-008101)  
At sa unang wera,

**[08:19:43.440] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-008102)  
sabi, critical minerals

**[08:19:45.500] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-008103)  
and rare earth

**[08:19:46.600] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-008104)  
are essential for the production of advanced

**[08:19:49.120] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-008105)  
technologies and industrial activities,

**[08:19:51.460] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-008106)  
et cetera, et cetera.

**[08:19:52.820] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-008107)  
Ang unang tanong ko po, pinirmahan ito ng

**[08:19:54.820] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-008108)  
US at Pilipinas,

**[08:19:57.140] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-008109)  
di yan narang pumirma,

**[08:20:00.420] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-008110)  
pero ano bang pinirmahan nila?

**[08:20:03.620] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-008111)  
In the first place,

**[08:20:06.340] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-008112)  
ano ba ang ibig sabihin

**[08:20:07.580] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-008113)  
ng critical minerals?

**[08:20:10.200] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-008114)  
Sa batas ng Pilipinas,

**[08:20:12.260] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-008115)  
hindi ng US,

**[08:20:13.840] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-008116)  
meron bang legal

**[08:20:15.040] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-008117)  
definition of what

**[08:20:17.340] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-008118)  
critical minerals are?

**[08:20:20.280] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-008119)  
Kasi sa US,

**[08:20:21.280] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-008120)  
meron po.

**[08:20:23.120] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-008121)  
Malinaw po sa US.

**[08:20:25.900] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-008122)  
In US,

**[08:20:27.140] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-008123)  
critical minerals is legally

**[08:20:29.160] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-008124)  
defined

**[08:20:29.720] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-008125)  
through the Energy Act of 2020

**[08:20:32.860] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-008126)  
of the United States.

**[08:20:35.040] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-008127)  
Ano ang definition?

**[08:20:36.560] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-008128)  
Critical minerals,

**[08:20:38.480] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-008129)  
those that are essential to the economic

**[08:20:41.100] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-008130)  
or national security of the United States,

**[08:20:45.780] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-008131)  
have a supply chain

**[08:20:47.260] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-008132)  
that is vulnerable to disruption

**[08:20:49.260] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-008133)  
and serve

**[08:20:51.060] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-008134)  
as an essential function

**[08:20:52.980] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-008135)  
in the manufacturing of a product

**[08:20:55.140] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-008136)  
the absence of which

**[08:20:56.700] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-008137)  
would have significant consequences

**[08:20:58.980] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-008138)  
for the economic

**[08:21:00.400] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-008139)  
or national

**[08:21:01.900] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-008140)  
or national security

**[08:21:03.740] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-008141)  
of the US.

**[08:21:06.180] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-008142)  
Okay?

**[08:21:07.580] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-008143)  
So,

**[08:21:08.860] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-008144)  
defined sa batas ng US,

**[08:21:11.340] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-008145)  
kung ano yung critical minerals.

**[08:21:13.380] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-008146)  
Bakit siya critical?

**[08:21:14.720] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-008147)  
Critical for whom?

**[08:21:16.380] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-008148)  
Critical for the United States

**[08:21:18.040] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-008149)  
and particularly for its national security.

**[08:21:21.100] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-008150)  
So, malinaw po yun.

**[08:21:22.540] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-008151)  
And their law does more than that.

**[08:21:25.400] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-008152)  
At tinatakda ang kanilang

**[08:21:27.920] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-008153)  
United States Geological Survey,

**[08:21:30.620] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-008154)  
may ahensya ng gobyerno

**[08:21:32.200] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-008155)  
na taon-taon naglalabas

**[08:21:34.600] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-008156)  
ng opisyal na listahan

**[08:21:36.440] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-008157)  
ng critical minerals.

**[08:21:39.280] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-008158)  
So, as of 2025,

**[08:21:41.220] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-008159)  
the latest list available,

**[08:21:43.480] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-008160)  
25 minerals po yan

**[08:21:45.120] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-008161)  
na tinatawag ng US

**[08:21:47.380] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-008162)  
na critical minerals.

**[08:21:49.340] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-008163)  
Babanggiting ko po,

**[08:21:51.080] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-008164)  
please bear with me,

**[08:21:52.380] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-008165)  
aluminum, antimony, arsenic,

barite, beryllium,

**[08:22:42.660] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-008190)

**[08:22:44.960] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-008191)  
25?

**[08:22:45.960] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-008192)  
25 critical minerals.

**[08:22:48.780] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-008193)  
Yan po. And why

**[08:22:49.840] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-008194)
