Sabi nila, tanong nyo sa DNR.

**[08:52:39.380] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-009104)  
Tinatanong ko po sa inyo ngayon.

**[08:52:41.380] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-009105)  
Dadalo po ba kayo?

**[08:52:43.380] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-009106)  
Madam Speaker, upcoming, they will attend daw.

**[08:52:46.380] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-009107)  
We will attend.

**[08:52:48.380] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-009108)  
Okay.

**[08:52:49.380] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-009109)  
Magiging agenda po ba ang pinsala na nangyayari sa Pilipinas at sa mga mamamayang Pilipino?

**[08:52:58.380] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-009110)  
Dala nito sa ASEAN Summit sa November.

**[08:53:03.380] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-009111)  
Nagaganapin dito mismo sa Pilipinas.

**[08:53:06.380] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-009112)  
Siguro habang nandito sila sa PICC po yun.

**[08:53:08.380] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-009113)  
Ngayon, mararanasan nung lahat yung usok na yan.

**[08:53:14.030] Deputy Speaker Jose C. Alvarez** (asr-009114)  
Pag November, wala na yung usok.

**[08:53:15.380] Deputy Speaker Jose C. Alvarez** (asr-009116)  
First issue lang, I don't know how to collect.

**[08:53:17.380] Deputy Speaker Jose C. Alvarez** (asr-009117)  
If you can help verbalize or memorize, memorialize what you are saying today.

**[08:53:25.380] Deputy Speaker Jose C. Alvarez** (asr-009118)  
That will help the DNR in order to present our case.

**[08:53:29.380] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-009119)  
Mr. Speaker, to wrap up, dahil lagpas na po tayo sa oras.

**[08:53:33.380] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-009120)  
Wala na eh, time expired ka na, sayang.

**[08:53:35.380] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-009121)  
Well, nakikita po natin kung gaano ka, inutil, walang ginagawa ang Marcos Jr. Administration sa issue ng trans-border haze pollution.

**[08:53:45.380] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-009122)  
No?

**[08:53:46.380] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-009123)  
Right now, September 17, yung Deputy Prime Minister ng Malaysia ay nasa Indonesia nakikipag-meeting.

**[08:53:56.380] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-009124)  
Naniningil sila. Ano bang pwede natin gawin para huwag na maulit ito and so on.

**[08:54:02.380] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-009125)  
Ganon po ka high level ang usapin dahil naisprotektahan ng Malaysia, katulad din ng Singapore, ang kanilang mamamayan, populasyon sa epekto ng pollution.

**[08:54:14.380] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-009126)  
Pero dito sa Pilipinas, abay, pabanjing-banjing lang ang Malacanang.

**[08:54:19.380] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-009127)  
Hayaan nyo na lang, mag-mask na lang daw kayo.

**[08:54:22.380] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-009128)  
O kaya mag-cancel tayo ng klase pagkasobra na ang haze.

**[08:54:27.380] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-009129)  
So kaya po, we expect that the DNR and Malacanang will do its job in protecting the health of the Filipino people and our environment.

**[08:54:40.380] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-009130)  
So hanggang doon na lang po, Madam Speaker. Maraming salamat.

**[08:54:44.380] Deputy Speaker Jose C. Alvarez** (asr-009131)  
Majority Leader.

**[08:54:46.380] Deputy Speaker Jose C. Alvarez** (asr-009132)  
Madam Speaker, thank you. Distinguished colleague, thank you.

Representative Edgar Erice from the 2nd District of Caloocan City.

Ready and willing.

**[08:55:27.380] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-009141)  
Oo, Madam Speaker.

**[08:55:28.380] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-009142)  
Ikukumpirma ko lang po sa LLDA Charter kung yung mandate ng Laguna Lake Development Authority

**[08:55:39.380] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-009143)  
eh sa Laguna Lake lang o kasama yung mga nearby provinces.

**[08:55:43.380] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-009144)  
Provinces, cities, and municipalities. Kasama po ba?

**[08:55:46.380] Deputy Speaker Jose C. Alvarez** (asr-009145)  
Yes, kasama, Madam Speaker.

**[08:55:52.380] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-009146)  
Kasama po dito yung mga reclamation na ginagawa doon sa iba't ibang bayan at lungsod.

**[08:56:00.380] Deputy Speaker Jose C. Alvarez** (asr-009147)  
Yes, within Laguna Lake.

**[08:56:02.380] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-009148)

**[08:56:05.380] Deputy Speaker Jose C. Alvarez** (asr-009149)  
Within Laguna Lake.

**[08:56:07.380] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-009150)  
Muntinlupa and Taguig.

**[08:56:30.380] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-009154)

At ano po yung mga project na...

**[08:56:44.380] Deputy Speaker Jose C. Alvarez** (asr-009156)

**[08:56:47.380] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-009157)  
Wala na po?

**[08:56:48.380] Deputy Speaker Jose C. Alvarez** (asr-009158)  
Wala pa.

**[08:56:49.380] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-009159)

**[08:56:53.380] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-009160)

**[08:56:58.380] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-009161)

**[09:12:30.000] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-009285)  
of espionage law.

**[09:12:31.640] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-009286)  
Madam Speaker, balikan natin

**[09:12:33.040] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-009287)  
ang nilalaman ng House Bill 9642.

**[09:12:36.140] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-009288)  
Pinapalawak nito ang espionage law

**[09:12:38.160] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-009289)  
to cover any unauthorized

**[09:12:40.460] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-009290)  
quote-unquote dealing

**[09:12:42.040] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-009291)  
with a classified matter

**[09:12:44.260] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-009292)  
committed in time of war or peace

**[09:12:46.860] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-009293)  
through physical, electronic, cyber

**[09:12:49.000] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-009294)  
or any other means.

**[09:12:50.500] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-009295)  
It criminalizes accessing a sketch,

**[09:12:52.860] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-009296)  
map, or graphical or multimedia

**[09:12:54.820] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-009297)  
representation of a prohibited place

**[09:12:57.580] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-009298)  
or piece of critical infrastructure

**[09:13:00.000] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-009299)  
and makes unauthorized possession

**[09:13:01.680] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-009300)  
of surveillance or mapping equipment

**[09:13:03.640] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-009301)  
like cameras, drones, tracking devices.

**[09:13:06.860] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-009302)  
Conspiracy and even the mere proposal

**[09:13:09.360] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-009303)  
to commit espionage

**[09:13:10.460] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-009304)  
are separately punishable

**[09:13:12.380] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-009305)  
with conspiracy carrying the same life sentence

**[09:13:16.040] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-009306)  
as the underlying offense.

**[09:13:18.480] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-009307)  
The bill also empowers

**[09:13:19.700] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-009308)  
the Anti-Money Laundering Council

**[09:13:21.760] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-009309)  
to investigate, freeze,

**[09:13:24.080] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-009310)  
and forfeit assets connected

**[09:13:25.560] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-009311)  
to any of these offenses

**[09:13:26.960] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-009312)  
and allows military and law enforcement

**[09:13:29.140] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-009313)  
personnel to wiretap

**[09:13:31.600] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-009314)  
and intercept the communications

**[09:13:33.760] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-009315)  
of anyone merely suspected

**[09:13:35.840] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-009316)  
merely suspected

**[09:13:37.120] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-009317)  
of committing them.

**[09:13:39.020] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-009318)  
Madam Speaker, my esteemed colleagues,

**[09:13:41.240] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-009319)  
and to all concerned citizens watching,

**[09:13:43.880] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-009320)  
what sits at the center

**[09:13:45.360] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-009321)  
of this strategy is the

**[09:13:47.020] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-009322)  
2025-2028 National Action Plan

**[09:13:49.860] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-009323)  
for Unity, Peace, and Development

**[09:13:51.500] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-009324)  
or NAPUPD,

**[09:13:53.040] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-009325)  
the government's counterinsurgency blueprint

**[09:13:55.180] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-009326)  
na pinapatakbo ng National Task Force

**[09:13:57.900] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-009327)  
to End Local Communist Armed

**[09:13:59.240] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-009328)  
Conflict, or NTF-ELCAC,

**[09:14:00.200] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-009329)  
with a proven track record

**[09:14:01.500] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-009330)  
of human rights violations.

**[09:14:04.640] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-009333)  
Madam Speaker,

**[09:14:05.500] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-009334)  
marami na pong mga nabiktima

**[09:14:06.700] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-009335)  
ng mga naunang ipinasang

**[09:14:08.000] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-009336)  
anti-terror law

**[09:14:09.200] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-009337)  
at kahit ang

**[09:14:10.000] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-009338)  
Terrorist Financing Prevention

**[09:14:11.700] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-009339)  
and Suppression Act.

**[09:14:13.500] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-009340)  
Marami na rin po

**[09:14:14.040] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-009341)  
ang mga kasong pinatawan nito

**[09:14:16.940] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-009342)  
na dismissed din.

**[09:14:18.460] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-009343)  
Ginagamit lamang

**[09:14:19.420] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-009344)  
ang mga batas na ito

**[09:14:20.380] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-009345)  
gaya ng gagawin

**[09:14:21.380] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-009346)  
sa batas na ito

**[09:14:22.280] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-009347)  
na taktika

**[09:14:23.920] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-009348)  
upang supilin

**[09:14:25.040] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-009349)  
ang mga mamamayan

**[09:14:25.860] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-009350)  
sa mga gawagawang kaso,

**[09:14:27.400] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-009351)  
Making it more easier

**[09:14:28.640] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-009352)  
para po sila ay

**[09:14:30.740] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-009353)  
magawan ng ganitong mga

**[09:14:32.240] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-009354)  
arbitrary cases.

**[09:14:34.700] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-009355)  
Thus, Madam Speaker,

**[09:14:36.100] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-009356)  
we register the vote

**[09:14:37.140] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-009357)  
no.

**[09:14:38.040] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-009358)  
Thank you so much.

**[09:14:39.220] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-009359)

**[09:14:40.980] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-009360)

**[09:14:42.260] Rep. Marlyn L. Primicias-Agabas** (asr-009361)  
Madam Speaker,

**[09:14:43.300] Rep. Marlyn L. Primicias-Agabas** (asr-009362)  
I move that

**[09:14:44.200] Rep. Marlyn L. Primicias-Agabas** (asr-009363)  
we recognize

**[09:14:44.800] Rep. Marlyn L. Primicias-Agabas** (asr-009364)  
Honorable Antonio Tinio

**[09:14:46.000] Rep. Marlyn L. Primicias-Agabas** (asr-009365)  
of ACT Teachers

**[09:14:47.200] Rep. Marlyn L. Primicias-Agabas** (asr-009366)  
to explain his vote.

**[09:14:49.330] Rep. Marlyn L. Primicias-Agabas** (asr-009367)  
Okay.

**[09:14:51.070] Rep. Marlyn L. Primicias-Agabas** (asr-009368)

**[09:14:53.140] Rep. Marlyn L. Primicias-Agabas** (asr-009369)  
I move that

**[09:14:53.680] Rep. Marlyn L. Primicias-Agabas** (asr-009370)  
Honorable Sarah Elago

**[09:14:55.480] Rep. Marlyn L. Primicias-Agabas** (asr-009371)  
of Gabriela Party-list

**[09:14:56.600] Rep. Marlyn L. Primicias-Agabas** (asr-009372)  
be recognized to explain her vote.

**[09:14:58.080] Deputy Speaker Mercedes K. Alvarez** (asr-009390)  
Representative Sarah Jane Elago of Gabriela Women's Party-list is hereby recognized to explain her vote. She has three minutes.

**[09:15:04.760] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-009391)  
This representation from Gabriela, Women's Party, votes no to House Bill No. 9642 or the proposed new Espionage Bill.

**[09:15:13.060] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-009392)  
Malawak ang definisyon ng espionage sa ilalim ng Section 5.

**[09:15:16.920] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-009393)  
Sa panukalang batas, maaaring ituring na espionage ang mga gawaing sinasabing nagdudulot o maaaring magdulot ng pinsala sa Estado.

**[09:15:25.100] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-009394)  
Malabo at malawak ang ganitong pamantayan at nagbubukas ito ng possibility na gamitin laban sa lehitimong kritisismo.

**[09:15:34.340] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-009395)  
Sa ilalim ng seksyong ito, habang buhay na pagkakakulong ng walang benefit of parole at benefits of good conduct time allowance ang parusa.

**[09:15:43.920] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-009396)  
Sa kasalukuyang kalagayan kung saan ang mga kritiko ng pamahalaan ay nababansagan na terorista,

**[09:15:50.380] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-009397)  
napakamapanganib ng isang panukalang batas na maaaring magturing sa paglalabas.

**[09:15:55.100] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-009398)  
Paglalantad ng korupsyon, pagbatiko sa mga palisiya o paglalabas ng impormasyong nasa interes ng publiko bilang isang uri ng quote-unquote injury to the state.

**[09:16:07.380] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-009399)  
The bill also expands the definition of critical infrastructure to shield private corporate interest by including telecommunications, energy and food security under the umbrella of espionage protection.

**[09:16:22.140] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-009400)  
The bill effectively bans the public from scrutinizing.

**[09:16:25.100] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-009401)  
The industries that most affect their lives.

**[09:16:28.040] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-009402)  
Nagbibigay ang panukala ng mas malawak na kapangyarihan para sa surveillance at pagmonitor ng mga communication at financial transactions.

**[09:16:37.020] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-009403)  
Sa section 16 at section 17, sa pamamagitan ng pag-bypass sa anti-wiretapping law at pagbibigay ng kapangyarihan sa AMLAC na i-freeze ang mga pondo batay lamang sa hinala,

**[09:16:49.060] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-009404)  
ginagawa nitong normal ang surveillance, harassment at pag-target sa mga kritiko.

**[09:16:55.100] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-009405)  
Maaaring gamitin ang ganitong malawak na kapangyarihan upang matahimik o patahimikin ang mga naglalantad ng katotohanan sa mga katiwalian at umaalma laban sa mga mapanupil at mapang-abuso na palisiya.

**[09:17:11.140] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-009406)  
Dahil dito, ang Gabriela Women's Party ay bumuboto ng no sa House Bill No. 9642.

**[09:17:16.920] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-009407)  
Maraming salamat.

**[09:17:18.680] Deputy Speaker Mercedes K. Alvarez** (asr-009408)  
Majority Leader.

**[09:17:19.780] Rep. Marlyn L. Primicias-Agabas** (asr-009409)  
Madam Speaker, I move that we recognize Honorable Antonio Tinio of ACT Teachers to explain his vote.

**[09:17:24.760] Deputy Speaker Mercedes K. Alvarez** (asr-009411)  
Representative Antonio Tinio of ACT Teachers Party-list is hereby recognized to explain his vote.

**[09:17:30.180] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-009412)  
Maraming salamat, Madam Speaker.

**[09:17:32.360] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-009413)  
First of all, we note that some effort has been made during the period of amendments,

**[09:17:39.460] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-009414)  
effort by the sponsors to introduce certain amendments that would somehow limit the scope of certain provisions.

**[09:17:53.220] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-009415)  
Thereby,

**[09:17:55.100] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-009416)  
acknowledging our essential critique that the definitions and the scope and coverage of the bill is so overly broad that it poses threat and danger to the rights and civil liberties of Filipinos.

**[09:18:23.220] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-009418)  
In the name of national security.

**[09:18:28.420] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-009419)  
However, these amendments have not gone far enough.

**[09:18:32.500] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-009420)  
So, nananatili pa rin po ang masaklaw na coverage ng national security, ng critical infrastructure, ng mga pinagbabawal na acto,

**[09:18:47.260] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-009421)  
na nanatiling banta.

**[09:18:53.220] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-009422)  
Ang panukalang batas na ito, lalo na para sa mga kritiko at mga tumututol at lumalaban para sa kanilang karapatan.

**[09:19:06.900] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-009423)  
Kaya maaaring gamitin ang panukalang batas na ito bilang isa na namang instrumento, dagdag na instrumento,

**[09:19:19.200] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-009424)  
para sa pagsupil ng fundamental rights ng mamamayang Pilipino to free expression, the right to dissent in the name of national security.

**[09:19:35.420] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-009425)  
Katulad din ng anti-terror law at mga kaugnay na batas.

**[09:19:39.400] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-009426)  
Kaya dahil po rito ay bumuboto tayo ng no sa panukalang batas, an act expanding the coverage of espionage law.

**[09:19:49.200] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-009427)  
Maraming salamat Madam Speaker.

**[09:19:53.360] Deputy Speaker Mercedes K. Alvarez** (asr-009428)  
Majority Leader.

**[09:19:54.980] Rep. Marlyn L. Primicias-Agabas** (asr-009428-split-2)
